===== src/features/admin/analytics/overview/overviewCalculations.ts =====
import type { Storefront } from '../../types/admin.types';
import type {
  OverviewAnalytics,
  OverviewBusinessSummary,
  OverviewDateRangeValue,
  OverviewOrder,
  OverviewOrderOperations,
  OverviewProductPerformanceItem,
  OverviewSalesPerformance,
  OverviewSalesPoint,
  OverviewStorePerformance,
} from '../../types/overview.types';
import {
  getOverviewDateKey,
  isWithinOverviewRange,
} from '../../utils/overview/dateRanges';

const STOREFRONTS: Storefront[] = ['yotaabo', 'poshies'];

function isDelivered(order: OverviewOrder): boolean {
  return order.status === 'delivered';
}

function isCancelled(order: OverviewOrder): boolean {
  return order.status === 'cancelled';
}

function isEligibleForSales(order: OverviewOrder): boolean {
  return isDelivered(order) && !isCancelled(order);
}

function getOrderValue(order: OverviewOrder): number {
  return Number.isFinite(order.subtotal) ? order.subtotal : 0;
}

function getItemValue(price: number, quantity: number): number {
  return price * quantity;
}

function getStatusCounts(orders: OverviewOrder[]) {
  return {
    pending: orders.filter((order) => order.status === 'pending').length,
    confirmed: orders.filter((order) => order.status === 'confirmed').length,
    delivered: orders.filter(isDelivered).length,
    cancelled: orders.filter(isCancelled).length,
  };
}

export function calculateBusinessSummary(
  periodOrders: OverviewOrder[],
  catalogue: {
    total: number;
    active: number;
    hidden: number;
  },
): OverviewBusinessSummary {
  const statuses = getStatusCounts(periodOrders);

  return {
    ordersReceived: periodOrders.filter(
      (order) => !isCancelled(order),
    ).length,
    outstandingOrders: statuses.pending + statuses.confirmed,
    pendingOrders: statuses.pending,
    confirmedOrders: statuses.confirmed,
    deliveredOrders: statuses.delivered,
    cancelledOrders: statuses.cancelled,
    totalProducts: catalogue.total,
    activeProducts: catalogue.active,
    hiddenProducts: catalogue.hidden,
  };
}

export function calculateSalesPerformance(
  periodOrders: OverviewOrder[],
): OverviewSalesPerformance {
  const deliveredOrders = periodOrders.filter(isEligibleForSales);

  const salesByDate = new Map<string, OverviewSalesPoint>();

  for (const order of deliveredOrders) {
    const date = getOverviewDateKey(order.created_at);

    const point = salesByDate.get(date) ?? {
      date,
      label: date,
      value: 0,
      orders: 0,
    };

    point.value += getOrderValue(order);
    point.orders += 1;

    salesByDate.set(date, point);
  }

  const dailySales = [...salesByDate.values()].sort(
    (a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date),
  );

  return {
    recordedSalesValue: deliveredOrders.reduce(
      (total, order) => total + getOrderValue(order),
      0,
    ),
    deliveredOrders: deliveredOrders.length,
    dailySales,
  };
}

export function calculateOrderOperations(
  periodOrders: OverviewOrder[],
): OverviewOrderOperations {
  const statuses = getStatusCounts(periodOrders);

  const recentOrders = [...periodOrders]
    .sort(
      (a, b) =>
        new Date(b.created_at).getTime() -
        new Date(a.created_at).getTime(),
    )
    .slice(0, 10);

  return {
    total: periodOrders.length,
    ...statuses,
    recentOrders,
  };
}

export function calculateProductPerformance(
  periodOrders: OverviewOrder[],
): OverviewProductPerformanceItem[] {
  const products = new Map<string, OverviewProductPerformanceItem>();

  for (const order of periodOrders) {
    if (!isEligibleForSales(order)) continue;

    for (const item of order.items) {
      // Deleted products retain their order lines, but cannot
      // be attributed to a product in performance rankings.
      if (
        typeof item.product_id !== 'string' ||
        item.product_id.trim().length === 0
      ) continue;

      const current = products.get(item.product_id) ?? {
        productId: item.product_id,
        productName: item.name,
        unitsSold: 0,
        recordedValue: 0,
      };

      current.unitsSold += item.qty;
      current.recordedValue += getItemValue(item.price, item.qty);

      products.set(item.product_id, current);
    }
  }

  return [...products.values()].sort(
    (a, b) =>
      b.unitsSold - a.unitsSold ||
      b.recordedValue - a.recordedValue,
  );
}

export function calculateStoreComparison(
  periodOrders: OverviewOrder[],
  selectedStore: Storefront | 'all',
): OverviewStorePerformance[] {
  const stores =
    selectedStore === 'all' ? STOREFRONTS : [selectedStore];

  return stores.map((storefront) => {
    const storeOrders = periodOrders.filter(
      (order) => order.storefront === storefront,
    );

    const deliveredOrders = storeOrders.filter(isEligibleForSales);

    const unitsSold = deliveredOrders.reduce(
      (total, order) =>
        total +
        order.items.reduce((itemTotal, item) => itemTotal + item.qty, 0),
      0,
    );

    return {
      storefront,
      orders: storeOrders.filter((order) => !isCancelled(order)).length,
      deliveredOrders: deliveredOrders.length,
      recordedSalesValue: deliveredOrders.reduce(
        (total, order) => total + getOrderValue(order),
        0,
      ),
      unitsSold,
    };
  });
}

export function calculateOverviewAnalytics(args: {
  orders: OverviewOrder[];
  dateRange: OverviewDateRangeValue;
  catalogue: {
    total: number;
    active: number;
    hidden: number;
  };
  selectedStore: Storefront | 'all';
}): OverviewAnalytics {
  const filteredOrders = args.orders.filter((order) =>
    isWithinOverviewRange(order.created_at, args.dateRange),
  );

  const periodOrders =
    args.selectedStore === 'all'
      ? filteredOrders
      : filteredOrders.filter(
          (order) => order.storefront === args.selectedStore,
        );

  return {
    businessSummary: calculateBusinessSummary(
      periodOrders,
      args.catalogue,
    ),
    salesPerformance: calculateSalesPerformance(periodOrders),
    orderOperations: calculateOrderOperations(periodOrders),
    productPerformance: calculateProductPerformance(periodOrders),
    storeComparison: calculateStoreComparison(
      filteredOrders,
      args.selectedStore,
    ),
  };
}

===== src/features/admin/components/AccentButton.tsx =====
import type { CSSProperties, ReactNode } from 'react';

type AccentButtonProps = {
  variant?: 'accent' | 'active' | 'neutral' | 'dark' | 'transparent';
  shape?: 'pill' | 'circle' | 'tab' | 'segment';
  onClick?: () => void;
  ariaLabel?: string;
  ariaCurrent?: 'page' | 'true' | undefined;
  className?: string;
  style?: CSSProperties;
  storeColor?: string;
  activeColor?: string;
  children: ReactNode;
};

export default function AccentButton({
  variant = 'neutral',
  shape = 'pill',
  onClick,
  ariaLabel,
  ariaCurrent,
  className = '',
  style,
  storeColor,
  activeColor,
  children,
}: AccentButtonProps) {
  const variantClass =
    variant === 'accent'
      ? 'admin-btn-accent'
      : variant === 'active'
        ? 'admin-btn-active'
        : variant === 'dark'
          ? 'admin-btn-dark'
          : variant === 'transparent'
            ? 'admin-btn-transparent'
            : 'admin-btn-neutral';

  const shapeClass =
    shape === 'circle'
      ? 'admin-btn-circle'
      : shape === 'tab'
        ? 'admin-btn-tab'
        : shape === 'segment'
          ? 'admin-btn-segment'
          : 'admin-btn-pill';

  const inlineStyle: CSSProperties & { [key: string]: string | number } = {
    ...(style || {}),
    ...(storeColor ? { '--store-chip-color': storeColor } : {}),
    ...(variant === 'active'
      ? {
          '--btn-active-bg': activeColor || 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
          backgroundColor: activeColor || 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
          borderColor: activeColor || 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
          color: 'var(--admin-on-accent)',
        }
      : {}),
  };

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onClick}
      aria-label={ariaLabel}
      aria-current={ariaCurrent}
      data-store-color={storeColor}
      className={`admin-btn ${variantClass} ${shapeClass} ${className}`}
      style={inlineStyle}
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/components/AdminActivities.tsx =====
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { FiDownload } from 'react-icons/fi';
import CustomDropdown from '../../../components/CustomDropdown';
import type { AdminActivity } from '../hooks/useAdminActivity';

type ActivityPeriod = 'day' | 'month' | 'year';

type AdminActivitiesProps = {
  activities: AdminActivity[];
  isLoadingActivities: boolean;
};

function getActivityDescription(activity: AdminActivity) {
  const metadata = activity.metadata ?? {};

  const productName =
    typeof metadata.product_name === 'string'
      ? metadata.product_name.trim()
      : '';

  const rawOrderNumber =
    typeof metadata.order_number === 'string'
      ? metadata.order_number.trim()
      : '';

  const orderNumber = rawOrderNumber.replace(/^#+/, '');

  return activity.event_type === 'product.created' && productName
    ? `${productName} added`
    : activity.event_type === 'order.received' && orderNumber
      ? `Order #${orderNumber} received`
      : activity.description.replace(/^(Order)\s*#+/i, '$1 #');
}

function getDateKey(date: Date, period: ActivityPeriod) {
  const year = date.getFullYear();
  const month = String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  const day = String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');

  if (period === 'year') return String(year);
  if (period === 'month') return `${year}-${month}`;
  return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;
}

function getGroupLabel(key: string, period: ActivityPeriod) {
  if (period === 'year') return key;

  if (period === 'month') {
    const [year, month] = key.split('-').map(Number);
    return new Date(year, month - 1, 1).toLocaleDateString('en-ZA', {
      month: 'long',
      year: 'numeric',
    });
  }

  const [year, month, day] = key.split('-').map(Number);
  return new Date(year, month - 1, day).toLocaleDateString('en-ZA', {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'long',
    year: 'numeric',
  });
}

function escapeCsvCell(value: unknown) {
  let text = String(value ?? '');

  if (/^[\s]*[=+\-@]/.test(text)) {
    text = `'${text}`;
  }

  return `"${text.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`;
}

export default function AdminActivities({
  activities,
  isLoadingActivities,
}: AdminActivitiesProps) {
  const [period, setPeriod] = useState<ActivityPeriod>('day');

  const groups = useMemo(() => {
    const grouped = new Map<string, AdminActivity[]>();

    [...activities]
      .sort(
        (a, b) =>
          new Date(b.created_at).getTime() -
          new Date(a.created_at).getTime()
      )
      .forEach((activity) => {
        const date = new Date(activity.created_at);
        if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return;

        const key = getDateKey(date, period);
        const group = grouped.get(key) ?? [];
        group.push(activity);
        grouped.set(key, group);
      });

    return [...grouped.entries()];
  }, [activities, period]);

  const handleExport = () => {
    const rows = [
      ['Description', 'Event type', 'Entity type', 'Actor email', 'Date and time', 'Activity ID'],
      ...groups.flatMap(([, group]) =>
        group.map((activity) => [
          getActivityDescription(activity),
          activity.event_type,
          activity.entity_type,
          activity.actor_email ?? '',
          activity.created_at,
          activity.id,
        ])
      ),
    ];

    const csv = rows
      .map((row) => row.map(escapeCsvCell).join(','))
      .join('\r\n');

    const blob = new Blob(['\uFEFF', csv], {
      type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;',
    });

    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.href = url;
    link.download = `yotaabo-activities-${period}-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.csv`;
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    link.remove();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  return (
    <section className="w-full">
      <div className="admin-section-header">
        <h1 className="admin-section-header-title admin-type-page-title text-[#4d4d4d]">
          Recent Activities
        </h1>

        <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-end gap-3">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleExport}
            disabled={activities.length === 0}
            className="inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2 border border-[#4d4d4d] bg-[#4d4d4d] px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-white transition-colors hover:bg-transparent hover:text-[#4d4d4d] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
          >
            <FiDownload size={15} />
            Export
          </button>
          <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-[#4d4d4d]/65">
            Filter
          </span>

          <div className="w-32">
            <CustomDropdown
              value={period}
              onChange={(value) => setPeriod(value as ActivityPeriod)}
              options={[
                { value: 'day', label: 'Day' },
                { value: 'month', label: 'Month' },
                { value: 'year', label: 'Year' },
              ]}
              compact
              menuWidth="144px"
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mx-auto mt-6 w-full max-w-[896px]">
        {isLoadingActivities && activities.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="px-4 py-8 text-sm text-[#4d4d4d]/50">
            Loading activities...
          </p>
        ) : groups.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="px-4 py-8 text-sm text-[#4d4d4d]/50">
            No activities found.
          </p>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-7">
            {groups.map(([key, group]) => (
              <section key={key}>
                <h2 className="sticky top-[61px] z-20 mb-3 bg-white py-2 text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-[#4d4d4d]">
                  {getGroupLabel(key, period)}
                </h2>

                <div className="space-y-2">
                  {group.map((activity) => (
                    <article
                      key={activity.id}
                      className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-[5px] border border-[#4d4d4d]/[0.10] bg-[#f5f5f5] px-4 py-2.5 shadow-[0_1px_3px_rgba(0,0,0,0.035)]"
                    >
                      <p className="min-w-0 text-sm leading-relaxed text-[#4d4d4d]/45">
                        {getActivityDescription(activity)}
                      </p>

                      <time
                        dateTime={activity.created_at}
                        className="shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap text-right text-xs text-[#4d4d4d]/45"
                      >
                        {new Date(activity.created_at).toLocaleString('en-ZA', {
                          day: '2-digit',
                          month: 'short',
                          hour: '2-digit',
                          minute: '2-digit',
                        })}
                      </time>
                    </article>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </section>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/components/AdminBottomNavigation.tsx =====
import { FiGrid, FiPackage, FiSettings } from 'react-icons/fi';
import { VscPackage } from 'react-icons/vsc';
import { FaOpencart } from 'react-icons/fa';
import type { Tab } from '../types/admin.types';
import AccentButton from './AccentButton';

type AdminBottomNavigationProps = {
  activeTab: Tab;
  onTabChange: (tab: Tab) => void;
};

export default function AdminBottomNavigation({
  activeTab,
  onTabChange,
}: AdminBottomNavigationProps) {
  const tabs: { tab: Tab; label: string; Icon: typeof FiGrid }[] = [
    { tab: 'overview', label: 'Overview', Icon: FiGrid },
    { tab: 'products', label: 'Products', Icon: VscPackage },
    { tab: 'orders', label: 'Orders', Icon: FaOpencart },
    { tab: 'inventory', label: 'Inventory', Icon: FiPackage },
    { tab: 'settings', label: 'Settings', Icon: FiSettings },
  ];

  return (
    <nav
      aria-label="Admin navigation"
      className="fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 z-50"
      style={{
        paddingInline: 'var(--admin-space-sm, 12px)',
        paddingBottom: 'max(var(--admin-space-sm, 12px), env(safe-area-inset-bottom))',
      }}
    >
      <div
        className="relative mx-auto flex items-center justify-around"
        style={{
          maxWidth: 'var(--admin-bottom-nav-max-width)',
          paddingInline: 'var(--admin-space-xs, 8px)',
          paddingBlock: 'var(--admin-space-xs, 8px)',
          borderRadius: 'var(--ds-radius-xl, 0.75rem)',
          borderWidth: '1px',
          borderStyle: 'solid',
          borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-subtle)',
          backgroundColor: 'var(--admin-surface)',
          boxShadow: 'var(--admin-shadow-mobile-nav)',
        }}
      >
        {tabs.map(({ tab, label, Icon }) => {
          const isActive = activeTab === tab;
          return (
            <AccentButton
              key={tab}
              variant={isActive ? 'dark' : 'transparent'}
              shape="tab"
              onClick={() => onTabChange(tab)}
              ariaLabel={label}
              ariaCurrent={isActive ? 'page' : undefined}
              style={{ border: 0 }}
            >
              <Icon size={19} aria-hidden="true" />
              <span>{label}</span>
            </AccentButton>
          );
        })}

      </div>
    </nav>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/components/AdminCategories.tsx =====
import { useMemo } from 'react';
import AccentButton from './AccentButton';
import CategoryForm from '../settings/categories/CategoryForm';
import CategoryRow from '../settings/categories/CategoryRow';
import { useAdminCategories } from '../settings/categories/useAdminCategories';
import type { CategoriesProps, Storefront } from '../settings/categories/types';

export default function AdminCategories({ accessToken, adminRole, assignedStorefront }: CategoriesProps) {
  const c = useAdminCategories({ accessToken, adminRole, assignedStorefront });

  const sortedCategories = useMemo(() => {
    return [...c.categories].sort((a, b) => a.sort_order - b.sort_order);
  }, [c.categories]);


  return (
    <section className="mb-8" style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 'var(--admin-space-md)' }}>
      {/* Header row */}
      <div className="flex flex-col" style={{ gap: 'var(--admin-space-xs)' }}>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between" style={{ gap: 'var(--admin-space-md)' }}>
          <h2
            style={{
              fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-heading-2)',
              fontWeight: 'var(--admin-weight-semibold)',
              color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
              margin: 0,
              lineHeight: 1.2,
            }}
          >
            Categories
          </h2>
          <AccentButton
            variant="dark"
            shape="pill"
            onClick={() => c.openEditor({ kind: 'new-category' })}
            ariaLabel="Add category"
          >
            + Category
          </AccentButton>
        </div>
        <p
          style={{
            fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-supporting)',
            color: 'var(--admin-text-secondary)',
            margin: 0,
            lineHeight: 1.5,
          }}
        >
          Manage product categories and subcategories per storefront.
        </p>
      </div>

      {c.isSuperAdmin && !assignedStorefront && (
        <label style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 'var(--admin-space-xs)', maxWidth: '280px' }}>
          <span className="admin-type-supporting text-xs text-[var(--admin-text-secondary)]">Storefront</span>
          <select
            value={c.storefront}
            onChange={(e) => c.setStorefront(e.target.value as Storefront)}
            className="border border-solid border-[var(--admin-border-strong)] bg-[var(--admin-surface)] px-3 py-2 text-sm text-[var(--admin-text-primary)]"
          >
            <option value="yotaabo">Yotaabo</option>
            <option value="poshies">Poshies</option>
          </select>
        </label>
      )}

      {c.error && <p className="text-sm" style={{ color: 'var(--admin-status-red)' }}>{c.error}</p>}
      {c.notice && <p className="text-sm" style={{ color: 'var(--admin-status-green)' }}>{c.notice}</p>}

      {/* Floating category form — driven by c.editor */}
      {c.editor && (() => {
        const ed = c.editor;

        // Find the category this form relates to (for edit or subcategory editors)
        const categoryIdForEdit =
          ed.kind === 'edit-category'
            ? ed.id
            : ed.kind === 'edit-subcategory' || ed.kind === 'new-subcategory'
              ? ed.categoryId
              : null;

        const editingCategory = categoryIdForEdit
          ? c.categories.find((cat) => cat.id === categoryIdForEdit) ?? null
          : null;

        // Position count: for new category, current total + 1 (new one goes to end)
        // For edit category, count of same-group categories
        const totalPositions =
          ed.kind === 'new-category'
            ? c.categories.length + 1
            : editingCategory
              ? c.categories.filter((cat) => cat.is_active === editingCategory.is_active).length
              : c.categories.length;

        // Initial sort order: for new category, end of list; for edit, current sort
        const initialSortOrder =
          ed.kind === 'new-category'
            ? c.categories.length + 1
            : editingCategory?.sort_order ?? 1;

        return (
          <CategoryForm
            editor={ed}
            category={editingCategory}
            totalPositions={totalPositions}
            initialSortOrder={initialSortOrder}
            isSaving={c.isSaving}
            onSubmit={c.submitEditor}
            onCancel={c.closeEditor}
          />
        );
      })()}

      {c.isLoading ? (
        <p
          style={{
            paddingBlock: 'var(--admin-space-lg)',
            textAlign: 'center',
            fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-supporting)',
            color: 'var(--admin-text-tertiary)',
          }}
        >
          Loading categories…
        </p>
      ) : sortedCategories.length === 0 ? (
        <p
          style={{
            paddingBlock: 'var(--admin-space-md)',
            paddingInline: 'var(--admin-space-md)',
            textAlign: 'center',
            fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-supporting)',
            color: 'var(--admin-text-tertiary)',
            borderRadius: 'var(--ds-radius-lg)',
            borderWidth: '1px',
            borderStyle: 'dashed',
            borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-subtle)',
            margin: 0,
          }}
        >
          No categories yet.
        </p>
      ) : (
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 'var(--admin-space-md)' }}>
          {sortedCategories.map((category) => (
            <CategoryRow
              key={category.id}
              category={category}
              onChangeStatus={c.changeStatus}
              onEditCategory={c.editCategory}
              onMoveToPosition={c.moveCategoryToPosition}
              groupPosition={sortedCategories.findIndex((cat) => cat.id === category.id) + 1}
              groupSize={sortedCategories.length}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/components/AdminConfirmModal.tsx =====
import Modal from '../../../components/Modal';

type ConfirmModalProps = {
  isOpen: boolean;
  title: string;
  message: string;
  confirmText?: string;
  cancelText?: string;
  variant?: 'grey' | 'green';
  reverseActions?: boolean;
  isLoading?: boolean;
  onConfirm: () => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
};

export default function ConfirmModal({
  isOpen,
  title,
  message,
  confirmText = 'Remove',
  cancelText = 'Cancel',
  variant = 'grey',
  reverseActions = false,
  isLoading = false,
  onConfirm,
  onCancel,
}: ConfirmModalProps) {
  const confirmColor = variant === 'green' ? 'var(--admin-status-green)' : 'var(--admin-text-primary)';

  return (
    <Modal
      isOpen={isOpen}
      onClose={isLoading ? () => {} : onCancel}
      maxWidth="max-w-sm"
    >
      <div className="p-6">
        <h3 className="text-xl font-semibold text-[var(--admin-text-primary)]">{title}</h3>
        <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-relaxed text-[var(--admin-text-primary)]/70">{message}</p>

        <div className="mt-6 flex gap-3">
          <button
            onClick={reverseActions ? onConfirm : onCancel}
            disabled={isLoading}
            className="flex h-11 flex-1 items-center justify-center border border-transparent bg-[var(--admin-surface-subtle)] text-xs font-semibold text-black/60 transition-colors hover:bg-[var(--admin-surface-subtle)] hover:text-black active:bg-[var(--admin-surface-subtle)] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            {reverseActions ? confirmText : cancelText}
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={reverseActions ? onCancel : onConfirm}
            disabled={isLoading}
            className="flex h-11 flex-1 items-center justify-center border border-solid text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--admin-bg)] transition-colors disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
            style={{ backgroundColor: confirmColor, borderColor: confirmColor }}
            onMouseEnter={(e) => {
              e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = 'transparent';
              e.currentTarget.style.color = confirmColor;
            }}
            onMouseLeave={(e) => {
              e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = confirmColor;
              e.currentTarget.style.color = 'var(--admin-bg)';
            }}
          >
            {isLoading ? (
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1" aria-label="Loading">
                <span className="loading-dot"></span>
                <span className="loading-dot"></span>
                <span className="loading-dot"></span>
              </span>
            ) : reverseActions ? cancelText : confirmText}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/components/AdminDropdown.tsx =====
import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';

type Option = {
  value: string;
  label: string;
};

type AdminDropdownProps = {
  value: string;
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
  options: Option[];
  compact?: boolean;
  placeholder?: string;
  menuWidth?: string;
  align?: 'left' | 'right';
  iconOnly?: boolean;
  ariaLabel?: string;
  triggerIcon?: ReactNode;
};

export default function AdminDropdown({
  value,
  onChange,
  options,
  compact = false,
  placeholder = 'Select...',
  menuWidth = '100%',
  align = 'left',
  iconOnly = false,
  ariaLabel,
  triggerIcon,
}: AdminDropdownProps) {
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;

    const handler = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setIsOpen(false);
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handler);

    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handler);
  }, [isOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;

    const handler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') setIsOpen(false);
    };

    document.addEventListener('keydown', handler);

    return () => document.removeEventListener('keydown', handler);
  }, [isOpen]);

  const selected = options.find((option) => option.value === value);

  return (
    <div className="relative overview-filter-dropdown" ref={ref}>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setIsOpen(!isOpen)}
        aria-label={ariaLabel}
        aria-haspopup="listbox"
        aria-expanded={isOpen}
        title={ariaLabel}
        className={`admin-type-body ${iconOnly ? 'overview-filter-trigger-icon' : ''}`}
        style={{
          WebkitAppearance: 'none',
          appearance: 'none',
          margin: 0,
          textDecoration: 'none',
          cursor: 'pointer',
          outline: 'none',
          boxSizing: 'border-box',
          width: iconOnly ? '44px' : '100%',
          height: '44px',
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          justifyContent: iconOnly ? 'center' : 'space-between',
          textAlign: 'left',
          borderWidth: '1px',
          borderStyle: 'solid',
          borderColor: 'var(--admin-accent)',
          borderRadius: iconOnly ? '50%' : 'var(--ds-radius-lg)',
          backgroundColor: 'var(--admin-accent)',
          color: 'var(--admin-on-accent)',
          padding: iconOnly ? 0 : compact ? '6px 12px' : '12px 16px',
        }}
      >
        {iconOnly ? (
          triggerIcon
        ) : (
          <>
            <span
              style={{
                color: selected
                  ? 'var(--admin-text-primary)'
                  : 'var(--admin-text-tertiary)',
                minWidth: 0,
                overflow: 'hidden',
                textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
                whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
              }}
            >
              {selected ? selected.label : placeholder}
            </span>

            <svg
              style={{
                marginLeft: '8px',
                width: '16px',
                height: '16px',
                flexShrink: 0,
                color: 'var(--admin-text-tertiary)',
                transform: isOpen ? 'rotate(180deg)' : 'rotate(0deg)',
                transition: 'transform 200ms ease',
              }}
              viewBox="0 0 24 24"
              fill="none"
              stroke="currentColor"
              strokeWidth="2"
              strokeLinecap="round"
              strokeLinejoin="round"
            >
              <polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" />
            </svg>
          </>
        )}
      </button>

      {isOpen && (
        <div
          role="listbox"
          style={{
            position: 'absolute',
            [align === 'right' ? 'right' : 'left']: 0,
            top: '100%',
            width: menuWidth,
            marginTop: '4px',
            zIndex: 100,
            maxHeight: '240px',
            overflowY: 'auto',
            backgroundColor: 'var(--admin-surface)',
            boxShadow: 'var(--admin-shadow)',
            borderWidth: '1px',
            borderStyle: 'solid',
            borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-subtle)',
            borderRadius: 'var(--ds-radius-lg)',
          }}
        >
          {options.map((option) => (
            <button
              key={option.value}
              type="button"
              role="option"
              aria-selected={option.value === value}
              onClick={() => {
                onChange(option.value);
                setIsOpen(false);
              }}
              className="admin-type-body"
              style={{
                WebkitAppearance: 'none',
                appearance: 'none',
                margin: 0,
                textDecoration: 'none',
                cursor: 'pointer',
                outline: 'none',
                boxSizing: 'border-box',
                width: '100%',
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'space-between',
                textAlign: 'left',
                padding: '10px 16px',
                border: 'none',
                background: 'transparent',
                color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
              }}
              onMouseEnter={(e) => {
                e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor =
                  'var(--admin-surface-subtle)';
              }}
              onMouseLeave={(e) => {
                e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = 'transparent';
              }}
            >
              <span>{option.label}</span>

              <span
                style={{
                  display: 'inline-flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  justifyContent: 'center',
                  width: '16px',
                  height: '16px',
                  borderRadius: '50%',
                  border:
                    option.value === value
                      ? '5px solid var(--admin-accent)'
                      : '1.5px solid var(--admin-border-strong)',
                  flexShrink: 0,
                  transition: 'all 200ms ease',
                }}
              />
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import type { FormEvent } from 'react';

            Assign storefront
          </label>
          <select
            id="new-admin-store"
            value={storeSlug}
            onChange={(event) => setStoreSlug(event.target.value as 'yotaabo' | 'poshies')}
            disabled={isInviting}
            className="w-full rounded-none border border-[var(--admin-border-strong)] bg-[var(--admin-surface)] px-3 py-3 text-sm text-[var(--admin-text-primary)] outline-none focus:border-[var(--admin-focus)] disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            <option value="yotaabo">Yotaabo</option>
            <option value="poshies">Poshies</option>
          </select>
        </div>

        <button
          type="submit"
          disabled={isInviting || !email.trim()}
          className="w-full bg-[var(--admin-text-primary)] px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-white transition-opacity hover:opacity-90 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          {isInviting ? 'Sending invitation...' : 'Send invitation'}
        </button>
      </form>
    </section>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/components/AdminItemCard.tsx =====
import type * as React from 'react';

type AdminItemCardProps = {
  title: string;
  status: string;
  price: string;
  meta: string;
  onClick: () => void;
  ariaLabel: string;
  layout?: 'default' | 'product';
  leftContent?: React.ReactNode;
  leftClassName?: string;
  statusClassName?: string;
  className?: string;
};

export default function AdminItemCard({
  title,
  status,
  price,
  meta,
  onClick,
  layout = 'default',
  ariaLabel,
  leftContent,
  leftClassName = 'bg-[var(--admin-surface-subtle)]',
  statusClassName = 'text-[var(--admin-text-tertiary)]',
  className = '',
}: AdminItemCardProps) {
  return (
    <div
      onClick={onClick}
      onKeyDown={(event) => {
        if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
          event.preventDefault();
          onClick();
        }
      }}
      role="button"
      tabIndex={0}
      aria-label={ariaLabel}
      className={`admin-item-card flex cursor-pointer items-stretch overflow-hidden admin-font-body ${className}`}
      style={{ WebkitTapHighlightColor: 'transparent' }}
    >
      <div className={`admin-item-card-media relative shrink-0 overflow-hidden ${leftClassName}`}>
        {leftContent}
      </div>

      <div className="admin-item-card-content flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col justify-center">
        {layout === 'product' ? (
          <div className="admin-item-card-copy">
            <div className="admin-item-card-row">
              <p className="admin-type-card-title admin-color-primary admin-item-card-title">
                {title}
              </p>
              <p className="admin-type-card-title admin-color-primary admin-type-price admin-item-card-price">
                {price}
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="admin-item-card-row admin-item-card-secondary-row">
              <span className={`admin-type-micro admin-item-card-status ${statusClassName}`}>
                {status}
              </span>
              <p className="admin-type-micro admin-color-muted admin-item-card-meta">
                {meta}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="admin-item-card-copy">
            <div className="admin-item-card-row">
              <p className="admin-type-card-title admin-color-primary admin-item-card-title">
                {title}
              </p>
              <span className={`admin-type-micro admin-item-card-status ${statusClassName}`}>
                {status}
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="admin-item-card-row admin-item-card-secondary-row">
              <p className="admin-type-price admin-color-disabled admin-item-card-price">
                {price}
              </p>
              <p className="admin-type-micro admin-color-muted admin-item-card-meta">
                {meta}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/components/AdminList.tsx =====
export type AdminRecord = {
  id: string;
  email: string;
  role: 'super_admin' | 'store_admin';
  store_id: string | null;
  store_name: string | null;
  store_slug: string | null;
  is_active: boolean;
  created_at: string;
  invitation_id: string | null;
  invitation_status: 'pending' | 'accepted' | 'expired' | 'revoked' | null;
  invitation_created_at: string | null;
  invitation_expires_at: string | null;
};

type AdminListProps = {
  admins: AdminRecord[];
  isLoading: boolean;
  loadError: string;
  onRefresh: () => void;
  onResend: (admin: AdminRecord) => void;
  onRevoke: (admin: AdminRecord) => void;
  onDelete: (admin: AdminRecord) => void;
  actionLoadingId: string | null;
};

export default function AdminList({
  admins,
  isLoading,
  loadError,
  onRefresh,
  onResend,
  onRevoke,
  onDelete,
  actionLoadingId,
}: AdminListProps) {
  return (
    <section className="border border-[var(--admin-border-subtle)] bg-[var(--admin-surface)] p-5">
      <div className="mb-5 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h3 className="admin-type-section-title text-[var(--admin-text-primary)]">
            Administrators
          </h3>
          <p className="admin-type-supporting mt-1 text-sm text-[var(--admin-text-secondary)]">
            Accounts with access to your administration system.
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onRefresh}
          disabled={isLoading}
          aria-label="Refresh administrators"
          className="border border-[var(--admin-border-strong)] px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-[var(--admin-text-primary)] hover:bg-[var(--admin-surface-subtle)] disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          {isLoading ? 'Loading...' : 'Refresh'}
        </button>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <p className="py-6 text-center text-sm text-[var(--admin-text-tertiary)]">
          Loading administrators...
        </p>
      ) : loadError ? (
        <div className="py-4">
          <p className="text-sm text-red-600">{loadError}</p>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onRefresh}
            className="mt-3 text-sm font-medium text-[var(--admin-text-primary)] underline"
          >
            Try again
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : admins.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="py-6 text-center text-sm text-[var(--admin-text-tertiary)]">
          No administrator accounts found.
        </p>
      ) : (
        <div className="divide-y divide-[var(--admin-border-subtle)]">
          {admins.map((item) => {
            const status = item.is_active
              ? 'active'
              : item.invitation_status === 'pending' &&
                  item.invitation_expires_at &&
                  new Date(item.invitation_expires_at).getTime() <= Date.now()
                ? 'expired'
                : item.invitation_status || 'inactive';

            const isActionLoading = actionLoadingId === item.id;
            const canManageInvitation =
              !item.is_active &&
              ['pending', 'expired'].includes(status);

            const statusStyles: Record<string, string> = {
              active: 'bg-[color-mix(in_srgb,var(--admin-status-green)_14%,transparent)] text-[var(--admin-status-green)]',
              pending: 'bg-[color-mix(in_srgb,var(--admin-status-amber)_14%,transparent)] text-[var(--admin-status-amber)]',
              expired: 'bg-[color-mix(in_srgb,var(--admin-status-red)_14%,transparent)] text-[var(--admin-status-red)]',
              revoked: 'bg-[color-mix(in_srgb,var(--admin-status-red)_14%,transparent)] text-[var(--admin-status-red)]',
              accepted: 'bg-[color-mix(in_srgb,var(--admin-status-green)_14%,transparent)] text-[var(--admin-status-green)]',
              inactive: 'bg-[var(--admin-surface-subtle)] text-[var(--admin-text-secondary)]',
            };

            const statusLabels: Record<string, string> = {
              active: 'Active',
              pending: 'Pending',
              expired: 'Expired',
              revoked: 'Revoked',
              accepted: 'Accepted',
              inactive: 'Inactive',
            };

            return (
              <div
                key={item.id}
                className="flex flex-col gap-3 py-4 first:pt-0 last:pb-0"
              >
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <p className="break-all text-sm font-medium text-[var(--admin-text-primary)]">
                      {item.email}
                    </p>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--admin-text-tertiary)]">
                      {item.role === 'super_admin'
                        ? 'Super Admin'
                        : 'Store Admin'}
                      {item.role === 'store_admin' && item.store_name
                        ? ` · ${item.store_name}`
                        : ''}
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  <span
                    className={`shrink-0 px-2 py-1 text-[10px] font-medium ${
                      statusStyles[status] || statusStyles.inactive
                    }`}
                  >
                    {statusLabels[status] || 'Inactive'}
                  </span>
                </div>

                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-xs text-[var(--admin-text-primary)]/40">
                      Added{' '}
                      {item.created_at
                        ? new Date(item.created_at).toLocaleDateString()
                        : 'Unknown'}
                    </p>

                    {item.invitation_expires_at &&
                      !item.is_active &&
                      item.invitation_status === 'pending' && (
                        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--admin-text-tertiary)]">
                          Invitation expires{' '}
                          {new Date(
                            item.invitation_expires_at,
                          ).toLocaleString()}
                        </p>
                      )}
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
                    {canManageInvitation && (
                      <>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => onResend(item)}
                          disabled={isActionLoading}
                          className="text-xs font-medium text-[var(--admin-text-primary)] underline disabled:opacity-50"
                        >
                          {isActionLoading ? 'Please wait...' : 'Resend invitation'}
                        </button>

                        {status === 'pending' && (
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => onRevoke(item)}
                            disabled={isActionLoading}
                            className="text-xs font-medium text-[var(--admin-status-red)] underline disabled:opacity-50"
                          >
                            Revoke
                          </button>
                        )}
                      </>
                    )}

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => onDelete(item)}
                      disabled={isActionLoading}
                      className="text-xs font-medium text-[var(--admin-status-red)] underline disabled:opacity-50"
                    >
                      {isActionLoading ? 'Please wait...' : 'Delete administrator'}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/components/AdminLoginScreen.tsx =====
import {
  useEffect,
  useLayoutEffect,
  useRef,
  useState,
} from 'react';
import type { FormEvent } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { FiEye, FiEyeOff } from 'react-icons/fi';
import ForgotPasswordModal from '../../../components/ForgotPasswordModal';

const dotAnimation = `
@keyframes yotaabo-dot-size {
  0%, 100% { transform: scale(0.55); opacity: 0.55; }
  50% { transform: scale(1.5); opacity: 1; }
}
`;

type AdminLoginScreenProps = {
  email: string;
  password: string;
  loginError: string | null;
  isLoggingIn: boolean;
  isResolving: boolean;
  showAuthIndicator: boolean;
  isTransitioning: boolean;
  onTransitionComplete: () => void;
  onEmailChange: (value: string) => void;
  onPasswordChange: (value: string) => void;
  onLogin: (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => void;
  onClearLoginError: () => void;
};

export default function AdminLoginScreen({
  email,
  password,
  loginError,
  isLoggingIn,
  isResolving,
  showAuthIndicator,
  isTransitioning,
  onTransitionComplete,
  onEmailChange,
  onPasswordChange,
  onLogin,
  onClearLoginError,
}: AdminLoginScreenProps) {
  const isFullscreen = isResolving || isTransitioning;
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [isForgotMode, setIsForgotMode] = useState(false);
  const [wordmarkCentered, setWordmarkCentered] = useState(false);
  const [wordmarkPosition, setWordmarkPosition] = useState({
    top: 0,
    width: 0,
  });

  const wordmarkContainerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const wordmarkTextRef = useRef<SVGTextElement>(null);
  const displacementRef = useRef<SVGFEDisplacementMapElement>(null);
  const transitionCompleteRef = useRef(onTransitionComplete);

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (isFullscreen) return;

    const bounds = wordmarkContainerRef.current?.getBoundingClientRect();

    if (bounds) {
      setWordmarkPosition({
        top: bounds.top,
        width: bounds.width,
      });
    }
  }, [isFullscreen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    transitionCompleteRef.current = onTransitionComplete;
  }, [onTransitionComplete]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isTransitioning) {
      setWordmarkCentered(isResolving);
      return;
    }

    const text = wordmarkTextRef.current;
    const displacement = displacementRef.current;

    if (!text || !displacement) return;

    const wordmarkText = text;
    const displacementMap = displacement;

    let disposed = false;
    let frame = 0;
    let centerFrame = 0;
    let startedAt = 0;

    const HOLD_DURATION = 900;
    const DESTRUCTION_DURATION = 1500;

    const clamp = (value: number, min: number, max: number) =>
      Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value));

    const easeInOut = (value: number) =>
      value < 0.5
        ? 4 * value * value * value
        : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * value + 2, 3) / 2;

    centerFrame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      setWordmarkCentered(true);
    });

    async function startAnimation() {
      try {
        await document.fonts.load('400 100px Anton');
        await document.fonts.ready;
      } catch {
        // Continue with the available font.
      }

      if (disposed) return;

      wordmarkText.style.opacity = '1';
      displacementMap.setAttribute('scale', '0');

      function animate(time: number) {
        if (disposed) return;

        if (!startedAt) startedAt = time;

        const elapsed = time - startedAt;

        if (elapsed < HOLD_DURATION) {
          wordmarkText.style.opacity = '1';
          displacementMap.setAttribute('scale', '0');
        } else {
          const progress = clamp(
            (elapsed - HOLD_DURATION) / DESTRUCTION_DURATION,
            0,
            1,
          );

          const eased = easeInOut(progress);

          displacementMap.setAttribute(
            'scale',
            String(180 * eased),
          );

          wordmarkText.style.opacity = String(1 - eased);

          if (progress >= 1) {
            wordmarkText.style.opacity = '0';
            transitionCompleteRef.current();
            return;
          }
        }

        frame = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
      }

      frame = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    }

    void startAnimation();

    return () => {
      disposed = true;
      cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
      cancelAnimationFrame(centerFrame);
    };
  }, [isTransitioning, isResolving]);

  return (
    <main
      className={
        isFullscreen
          ? 'fixed inset-0 m-0 flex h-screen w-screen items-center justify-center overflow-hidden bg-white p-0'
          : 'flex min-h-screen flex-col items-center justify-center bg-white px-6 py-12'
      }
    >
      <div
        ref={wordmarkContainerRef}
        className={
          isFullscreen
            ? 'absolute left-1/2 z-10'
            : 'relative w-full max-w-[1120px]'
        }
        style={
          isFullscreen
            ? {
                top: isTransitioning && !wordmarkCentered
                  ? `${wordmarkPosition.top}px`
                  : '37.5vh',
                width: isTransitioning && wordmarkPosition.width
                  ? `${wordmarkPosition.width}px`
                  : 'min(calc(100vw - 48px), 1120px)',
                maxWidth: '1120px',
                transform: 'translateX(-50%)',
                transition: isTransitioning
                  ? 'top 700ms cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1)'
                  : 'none',
              }
            : undefined
        }
      >
        <div className="relative flex w-full items-center justify-center">
          {showAuthIndicator && (
            <>
              <style>{dotAnimation}</style>
              <div
              aria-hidden="true"
              className="pointer-events-none absolute left-1/2 top-[calc(50%-10rem)] z-20 flex -translate-x-1/2 items-center gap-2"
            >
              {[0, 1, 2].map((dot) => (
                <span
                  key={dot}
                  className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#8aa89b]"
                  style={{
                    animation: 'yotaabo-dot-size 900ms ease-in-out infinite',
                    animationDelay: `${dot * 180}ms`,
                  }}
                />
              ))}
              </div>
            </>
          )}
          <div className="w-full">
            <svg
              aria-hidden="true"
              viewBox="0 0 100 100"
              preserveAspectRatio="none"
              style={{
                display: 'block',
                width: '100%',
                height: '25vh',
              }}
            >
              <defs>
                <filter
                  id="wordmark-destruction"
                  x="-30%"
                  y="-30%"
                  width="160%"
                  height="160%"
                  colorInterpolationFilters="sRGB"
                >
                  <feTurbulence
                    type="fractalNoise"
                    baseFrequency="0.08"
                    numOctaves="2"
                    seed="8"
                    result="noise"
                  />
                  <feDisplacementMap
                    ref={displacementRef}
                    in="SourceGraphic"
                    in2="noise"
                    scale="0"
                    xChannelSelector="R"
                    yChannelSelector="G"
                  />
                </filter>
              </defs>

              <text
                ref={wordmarkTextRef}
                x="50"
                y="50%"
                dominantBaseline="middle"
                textAnchor="middle"
                textLength="100"
                lengthAdjust="spacingAndGlyphs"
                style={{
                  fontFamily: '"Anton", sans-serif',
                  fontWeight: 400,
                  filter: 'url(#wordmark-destruction)',
                  opacity: 1,
                }}
                fill="#8aa89b"
                fontSize="100"
              >
                yotaabo
              </text>
            </svg>
          </div>

          {!isFullscreen && (
            <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 z-10 flex items-center justify-center">
              <p className="font-accent text-[clamp(50px,10vw,130px)] tracking-[0.02em] text-[#4d4d4d]">
                Be Remembered
              </p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {!isFullscreen && (
        <>
          <div className="mt-16 w-full max-w-sm">
            <form onSubmit={onLogin} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border border-black/10 bg-[#f5f5f5] px-4 py-3.5">
                <input
                  type="email"
                  required
                  value={email}
                  onChange={(event) => onEmailChange(event.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Email address"
                  className="w-full border-0 bg-transparent text-sm font-medium text-black outline-none ring-0 placeholder:text-[#4d4d4d]/50 focus:outline-none focus:ring-0"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border border-black/10 bg-[#f5f5f5] px-4 py-3.5">
                <input
                  type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                  required
                  value={password}
                  onChange={(event) => onPasswordChange(event.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Password"
                  className="w-full border-0 bg-transparent text-sm font-medium text-black outline-none ring-0 placeholder:text-[#4d4d4d]/50 focus:outline-none focus:ring-0"
                />

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowPassword((value) => !value)}
                  className="flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center border-0 bg-transparent p-0 text-[#4d4d4d]/50 outline-none transition-colors hover:text-[#4d4d4d]"
                  aria-label={showPassword ? 'Hide password' : 'Show password'}
                >
                  {showPassword ? <FiEyeOff size={18} /> : <FiEye size={18} />}
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-2 flex justify-end">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setIsForgotMode(true)}
                  className="border-0 bg-transparent p-0 text-sm text-[#4d4d4d]/60 hover:text-[#4d4d4d]"
                >
                  Forgot password?
                </button>
              </div>

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={isLoggingIn}
                className="mt-4 flex h-12 w-full items-center justify-between border border-solid border-[#4d4d4d] bg-[#4d4d4d] px-5 text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide text-white transition-colors duration-300 hover:bg-transparent hover:text-[#4d4d4d] disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                <span>{isLoggingIn ? 'Signing in...' : 'Sign In'}</span>
                <span aria-hidden="true">↗</span>
              </button>
            </form>

            <p className="mt-8 text-center text-xs text-[#4d4d4d]/40">
              <Link to="/" className="hover:text-[#4d4d4d]">
                ← Back to site
              </Link>
            </p>
          </div>

          <ForgotPasswordModal
            isOpen={isForgotMode}
            onClose={() => setIsForgotMode(false)}
            initialEmail={email}
          />

          {loginError && (
            <div
              className="fixed inset-0 z-[60] flex items-center justify-center bg-white/40 p-4 backdrop-blur-sm"
              onClick={onClearLoginError}
            >
              <div
                className="relative w-full max-w-sm bg-white p-6 shadow-2xl"
                onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
              >
                <h2 className="font-heading text-2xl italic text-[#4d4d4d]">
                  Login Failed
                </h2>
                <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-relaxed text-[#4d4d4d]/70">
                  {loginError}
                </p>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={onClearLoginError}
                  className="mt-6 flex h-11 w-full items-center justify-center border border-solid border-[#4d4d4d] bg-[#4d4d4d] text-xs font-semibold text-white hover:bg-transparent hover:text-[#4d4d4d]"
                >
                  Try Again
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}

      {isTransitioning && (
        <span className="sr-only">Opening Yotaabo admin</span>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/components/AdminModals.tsx =====
import AdminProductForm, { type AdminProduct } from './AdminProductForm';
import AdminConfirmModal from './AdminConfirmModal';
import AdminOrderDetailsModal from './AdminOrderDetailsModal';
import AdminProductDetailsModal from './AdminProductDetailsModal';
import { invalidateProductsCache } from '../../../hooks/useProducts';
import type { AdminRow, Order, OrderStatus } from '../types/admin.types';

type AdminModalsProps = {
  viewingProduct: AdminRow | null;
  setViewingProduct: (v: AdminRow | null) => void;
  setEditing: (v: AdminProduct | null) => void;
  setFormOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  setConfirmDelete: (v: AdminRow | null) => void;
  setConfirmPermanentDelete: (v: AdminRow | null) => void;
  handleReactivateProduct: (p: AdminRow) => Promise<void>;

  formOpen: boolean;
  editing: AdminProduct | null;
  loadProducts: () => Promise<void>;
  loadActivities: () => Promise<void>;
  adminRole: 'super_admin' | 'store_admin' | null | undefined;
  adminStoreSlug: string | null | undefined;

  confirmDelete: AdminRow | null;
  isHidingProduct: boolean;
  handleHideProduct: (p: AdminRow) => Promise<void>;

  confirmPermanentDelete: AdminRow | null;
  isDeletingProduct: boolean;
  handlePermanentDeleteProduct: (p: AdminRow) => Promise<void>;

  viewingOrder: Order | null;
  setViewingOrder: (v: Order | null) => void;
  setConfirmDeleteOrder: (v: Order | null) => void;
  products: AdminRow[];
  handleOrderStatusChange: (o: Order, s: OrderStatus) => Promise<void>;

  confirmDeleteOrder: Order | null;
  handleDeleteOrder: (o: Order) => Promise<void>;
};

export default function AdminModals(props: AdminModalsProps) {
  return (
    <>
      <AdminProductDetailsModal
        product={props.viewingProduct}
        onClose={() => props.setViewingProduct(null)}
        onEdit={(product) => {
          props.setViewingProduct(null);
          props.setEditing(product);
          props.setFormOpen(true);
        }}
        onHide={props.setConfirmDelete}
        onShow={props.handleReactivateProduct}
        onDelete={(product) => {
          props.setViewingProduct(null);
          props.setConfirmPermanentDelete(product);
        }}
      />

      <AdminProductForm
        isOpen={props.formOpen}
        onClose={() => {
          props.setFormOpen(false);
          props.setEditing(null);
        }}
        onSaved={() => {
          invalidateProductsCache();
          void props.loadProducts();
          void props.loadActivities();
        }}
        productToEdit={props.editing}
        adminRole={props.adminRole ?? null}
        storeSlug={
          props.adminStoreSlug === 'yotaabo' || props.adminStoreSlug === 'poshies'
            ? props.adminStoreSlug
            : null
        }
      />

      <AdminConfirmModal
        isOpen={!!props.confirmDelete}
        title="Hide product?"
        message={
          props.confirmDelete
            ? `"${props.confirmDelete.name}" will be hidden from the site. You can reactivate it later.`
            : ''
        }
        confirmText="Hide"
        cancelText="Cancel"
        variant="grey"
        isLoading={props.isHidingProduct}
        onConfirm={() => {
          if (props.confirmDelete) void props.handleHideProduct(props.confirmDelete);
        }}
        onCancel={() => {
          if (!props.isHidingProduct) props.setConfirmDelete(null);
        }}
      />

      <AdminConfirmModal
        isOpen={!!props.confirmPermanentDelete}
        title="Delete product permanently?"
        message={
          props.confirmPermanentDelete
            ? `"${props.confirmPermanentDelete.name}" will be permanently deleted. This cannot be undone.`
            : ''
        }
        confirmText="Delete"
        cancelText="Cancel"
        variant="grey"
        isLoading={props.isDeletingProduct}
        onConfirm={() => {
          if (props.confirmPermanentDelete) void props.handlePermanentDeleteProduct(props.confirmPermanentDelete);
        }}
        onCancel={() => {
          if (!props.isDeletingProduct) props.setConfirmPermanentDelete(null);
        }}
      />

      <AdminOrderDetailsModal
        order={props.viewingOrder}
        products={props.products.filter(
          (product): product is AdminRow & { id: string } => typeof product.id === 'string',
        )}
        onClose={() => props.setViewingOrder(null)}
        onStatusChange={props.handleOrderStatusChange}
        onDelete={(order: Order) => {
          props.setViewingOrder(null);
          props.setConfirmDeleteOrder(order);
        }}
      />

      <AdminConfirmModal
        isOpen={!!props.confirmDeleteOrder}
        title="Delete order permanently?"
        message={
          props.confirmDeleteOrder
            ? `Order ${props.confirmDeleteOrder.order_number} will be permanently removed. This cannot be undone.`
            : ''
        }
        confirmText="Delete"
        cancelText="Cancel"
        variant="grey"
        onConfirm={() => {
          if (props.confirmDeleteOrder) void props.handleDeleteOrder(props.confirmDeleteOrder);
        }}
        onCancel={() => props.setConfirmDeleteOrder(null)}
      />
    </>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/components/AdminOrderDetailsModal.tsx =====
import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { FiTrash2, FiCheck, FiX, FiMenu } from 'react-icons/fi';
import { FaWhatsapp } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { formatPrice } from '../../../utils/formatPrice';
import Modal from '../../../components/Modal';
import CheckoutItemCard from '../../../components/CheckoutItemCard';
import ProductModal from '../../../components/ProductModal';
import { getCategoryTheme } from '../../../utils/categoryTheme';
import type { AdminProduct } from './AdminProductForm';
import type { Order } from '../types/admin.types';

type PreviewProduct = AdminProduct & { id: string };

type AdminOrderDetailsModalProps = {
  order: Order | null;
  products: PreviewProduct[];
  onClose: () => void;
  onStatusChange: (order: Order, newStatus: Order['status']) => void;
  onDelete: (order: Order) => void;
};

const STATUS_LABELS: Record<Order['status'], string> = {
  pending: 'Pending',
  confirmed: 'Confirmed',
  delivered: 'Delivered',
  cancelled: 'Cancelled',
};

const STATUS_COLORS: Record<Order['status'], string> = {
  pending: 'text-[var(--admin-status-amber)]',
  confirmed: 'text-[var(--admin-accent)]',
  delivered: 'text-[var(--admin-text-tertiary)]',
  cancelled: 'text-[var(--admin-status-red)]',
};

export default function AdminOrderDetailsModal({
  order,
  products,
  onClose,
  onStatusChange,
  onDelete,
}: AdminOrderDetailsModalProps) {
  const [isMenuOpen, setIsMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [selectedProduct, setSelectedProduct] = useState<PreviewProduct | null>(null);
  const menuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isMenuOpen) return;

    const handler = (event: MouseEvent) => {
      if (menuRef.current && !menuRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
        setIsMenuOpen(false);
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handler);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handler);
  }, [isMenuOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setIsMenuOpen(false);
  }, [order?.id]);

  if (!order) return null;

  const formattedDate = new Date(order.created_at).toLocaleDateString('en-ZA', {
    day: '2-digit',
    month: 'short',
    year: 'numeric',
    hour: '2-digit',
    minute: '2-digit',
  });

  const closeMenu = () => setIsMenuOpen(false);

  const actionClass =
    'flex min-h-12 w-full items-center gap-3 border-0 border-b border-[var(--admin-border-subtle)] bg-[var(--admin-surface)] px-4 py-3 text-left admin-type-body text-[var(--admin-text-secondary)] transition-colors hover:bg-[var(--admin-surface-subtle)] hover:text-[var(--admin-text-primary)]';

  return (
    <Modal isOpen={!!order} onClose={onClose} maxWidth="max-w-2xl" scrollable={false}>
      <div className="relative flex max-h-[85vh] flex-col overflow-hidden">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="relative z-20 flex shrink-0 items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b border-[var(--admin-border-subtle)] bg-[var(--admin-surface)] px-6 py-4 sm:px-8">
          <h2 className="min-w-0 truncate admin-type-card-title leading-snug text-[var(--admin-text-primary)] ">
            {order.customer_name}
          </h2>

<button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              aria-label="Close order details"
              className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center border-0 bg-transparent text-[var(--admin-text-primary)] transition-colors hover:bg-[var(--admin-surface-subtle)]"
            >
              <FiX size={22} />
            </button>
        </div>

        {/* Scrollable order details */}
        <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-6 py-6 sm:px-8 sm:py-8">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div>
                <span className="mb-1.5 block admin-type-micro font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-[var(--admin-charcoal-40)]">
                  Order number
                </span>
                <div className="w-full bg-[var(--admin-surface)] px-4 py-3 admin-type-body admin-color-primary">
                  {order.order_number}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <span className="mb-1.5 block admin-type-micro font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-[var(--admin-charcoal-40)]">
                  Order date
                </span>
                <div className="w-full bg-[var(--admin-surface)] px-4 py-3 admin-type-body admin-color-primary">
                  {formattedDate}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <span className="mb-1.5 block admin-type-micro font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-[var(--admin-charcoal-40)]">
                  Phone
                </span>
                <div className="w-full truncate bg-[var(--admin-surface)] px-4 py-3 admin-type-body admin-color-primary">
                  {order.customer_phone}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="min-w-0">
                <span className="mb-1.5 block admin-type-micro font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-[var(--admin-charcoal-40)]">
                  Status
                </span>
                <div className="w-full bg-[var(--admin-surface)] px-4 py-3 admin-type-body admin-color-primary">
                  <span className={STATUS_COLORS[order.status]}>
                    {STATUS_LABELS[order.status]}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Items */}
          <div className="mt-6">
            <h3 className="mb-3 admin-type-micro uppercase tracking-widest text-[var(--admin-text-tertiary)]">
              Items
            </h3>

            <div className="admin-order-items flex flex-col gap-2">
              {order.items.map((item, index) => {
                const matchedProduct = products.find(
                  (product) =>
                    product.name.trim().toLowerCase() ===
                    item.name.trim().toLowerCase(),
                );

                return (
                <CheckoutItemCard
                  key={`${order.id}-${index}`}
                  variant="admin"
                  name={item.name}
                  onClick={
                    matchedProduct
                      ? () => setSelectedProduct(matchedProduct)
                      : undefined
                  }
                  leftContent={
                    <>
                      <span className="admin-type-body">{item.qty}</span>
                      {' × '}
                      <span className="admin-type-body admin-color-primary">
                        {formatPrice(item.price)}
                      </span>
                    </>
                  }
                  rightContent={<span className="admin-type-body admin-color-primary">{formatPrice(item.price * item.qty)}</span>}
                />
                );
              })}
            </div>

            <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-between">
              <p className="admin-type-micro uppercase tracking-widest text-[var(--admin-text-tertiary)]">
                Subtotal
              </p>
              <p className="admin-type-body admin-color-primary">
                {formatPrice(order.subtotal)}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Sticky footer and upward-opening menu */}
        <div className="relative z-30 shrink-0 border-t border-[var(--admin-border-subtle)] bg-[var(--admin-surface)] px-6 py-4 sm:px-8">
          <div ref={menuRef} className="relative mx-auto w-full max-w-sm">
            {isMenuOpen && (
              <div
                role="menu"
                className="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 z-40 mb-2 w-64 -translate-x-1/2 border border-[var(--admin-border-strong)]/15 bg-[var(--admin-surface)] shadow-lg"
              >
                {order.status === 'pending' && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    role="menuitem"
                    onClick={() => {
                      closeMenu();
                      onStatusChange(order, 'confirmed');
                    }}
                    className={actionClass}
                  >
                    <FiCheck size={14} />
                    Confirm order
                  </button>
                )}

                {order.status === 'confirmed' && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    role="menuitem"
                    onClick={() => {
                      closeMenu();
                      onStatusChange(order, 'delivered');
                    }}
                    className={actionClass}
                  >
                    <FiCheck size={14} />
                    Mark delivered
                  </button>
                )}

                {(order.status === 'pending' || order.status === 'confirmed') && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    role="menuitem"
                    onClick={() => {
                      closeMenu();
                      onStatusChange(order, 'cancelled');
                    }}
                    className="flex min-h-12 w-full items-center gap-3 border-0 border-b border-[var(--admin-border-subtle)] bg-[var(--admin-surface)] px-4 py-3 text-left admin-type-body text-[var(--admin-status-red)]/80 transition-colors hover:bg-[var(--admin-surface-subtle)] hover:text-red-600"
                  >
                    <FiX size={14} />
                    Unable to complete
                  </button>
                )}

                <button
                  type="button"
                  role="menuitem"
                  onClick={() => {
                    closeMenu();
                    onDelete(order);
                  }}
                  className="flex min-h-12 w-full items-center gap-3 border-0 bg-[var(--admin-surface)] px-4 py-3 text-left admin-type-body text-[var(--admin-status-red)]/80 transition-colors hover:bg-[var(--admin-surface-subtle)] hover:text-red-600"
                >
                  <FiTrash2 size={14} />
                  Delete permanently
                </button>
              </div>
            )}

            <div className="flex w-full items-stretch gap-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIsMenuOpen((open) => !open)}
                className="flex h-12 min-w-0 flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-2 border-0 bg-[var(--admin-surface-subtle)] admin-type-body text-[var(--admin-text-primary)] transition-colors hover:bg-[var(--admin-hover)]"
                aria-expanded={isMenuOpen}
                aria-haspopup="menu"
                aria-label="Order actions menu"
              >
                <FiMenu size={18} />
                Menu
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() =>
                  window.open(
                    `https://wa.me/${order.customer_phone.replace(/\D/g, '')}`,
                    '_blank',
                    'noopener,noreferrer',
                  )
                }
                className="flex h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center border-0 bg-[var(--admin-surface-subtle)] text-[var(--admin-text-primary)] transition-colors hover:bg-[var(--admin-hover)]"
                aria-label="Contact customer on WhatsApp"
              >
                <FaWhatsapp size={22} />
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <ProductModal
        product={
          selectedProduct
            ? {
                id: selectedProduct.id,
                name: selectedProduct.name,
                price: String(selectedProduct.price),
                description: selectedProduct.description,
                image_url: selectedProduct.image_url,
              }
            : null
        }
        bgColor="bg-[var(--admin-surface)]"
        placeholderColor={
          selectedProduct
            ? getCategoryTheme(selectedProduct.category).placeholderColor
            : ''
        }
        accentColor={
          selectedProduct
            ? getCategoryTheme(selectedProduct.category).accentColor
            : 'text-[var(--admin-accent)]'
        }
        isDark={false}
        mode="preview"
        onClose={() => setSelectedProduct(null)}
      />
    </Modal>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/components/AdminOrders.tsx =====
import AdminItemCard from '../../../components/AdminItemCard';
import AdminPageHeader from './AdminPageHeader';
import { formatPrice } from '../../../utils/formatPrice';
import type {
  Order,
  OrderFilter,
} from '../types/admin.types';

type AdminOrdersProps = {
  orderSearch: string;
  orderFilter: OrderFilter;
  filteredOrders: Order[];
  paginatedOrders: Order[];
  isLoadingOrders: boolean;
  orderPage: number;
  totalOrderPages: number;
  onSearchChange: (value: string) => void;
  onFilterChange: (value: OrderFilter) => void;
  onPageChange: (page: number) => void;
  onSelectOrder: (order: Order) => void;
};

export default function AdminOrders({
  orderSearch,
  orderFilter,
  filteredOrders,
  paginatedOrders,
  isLoadingOrders,
  orderPage,
  totalOrderPages,
  onSearchChange,
  onFilterChange,
  onPageChange,
  onSelectOrder,
}: AdminOrdersProps) {
  return (
    <>
      <AdminPageHeader
        title="Orders"
        searchValue={orderSearch}
        searchPlaceholder="Search orders by number, name, or phone"
        onSearchChange={onSearchChange}
        filters={
          (['All', 'Pending', 'Confirmed', 'Delivered', 'Cancelled'] as OrderFilter[]).map((filter) => (
            <button
              key={filter}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onFilterChange(filter)}
              className={`border-0 bg-transparent px-0 py-1 admin-type-body text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${
                orderFilter === filter
                  ? 'text-[#4d4d4d] underline underline-offset-4 decoration-1'
                  : 'text-[#4d4d4d]/40 hover:text-[#4d4d4d]'
              }`}
            >
              {filter}
            </button>
          ))
        }
      />

      <div className="admin-order-list-region">
        {isLoadingOrders ? (
          <p className="admin-order-list-message admin-type-supporting text-center text-[#4d4d4d]/40">
            Loading orders...
          </p>
        ) : filteredOrders.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="admin-order-list-message admin-type-supporting text-center text-[#4d4d4d]/40">
            No orders match your filters.
          </p>
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="admin-order-mobile-list flex flex-col gap-6 md:hidden">
              {paginatedOrders.map((order) => {
                const orderDate = new Date(
                  order.created_at
                ).toLocaleDateString('en-ZA', {
                  day: 'numeric',
                  month: 'short',
                  year: 'numeric',
                });

                return (
                  <AdminItemCard
                    key={order.id}
                    layout="product"
                    title={order.customer_name}
                    status={order.status}
                    price={formatPrice(order.subtotal)}
                    meta={orderDate}
                    onClick={() => onSelectOrder(order)}
                    ariaLabel={`View order from ${order.customer_name}`}
                    leftClassName="admin-item-card-order-media"
                    statusClassName={
                      order.status === 'pending'
                        ? 'text-amber-600'
                        : order.status === 'cancelled'
                          ? 'text-red-500'
                          : 'text-green-700'
                    }
                  />
                );
              })}
            </div>

            <div className="hidden md:block admin-orders-table-scroll">
              <table className="admin-orders-table">
                <thead>
                  <tr>
                    <th scope="col">Order</th>
                    <th scope="col">Customer</th>
                    <th scope="col">Date</th>
                    <th scope="col" className="admin-orders-table-number">Items</th>
                    <th scope="col" className="admin-orders-table-number">Total</th>
                    <th scope="col">Status</th>
                  </tr>
