                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {paginatedOrders.map((order) => {
                    const totalQuantity = order.items.reduce(
                      (total, item) => total + item.qty,
                      0
                    );

                    return (
                      <tr
                        key={order.id}
                        onClick={() => onSelectOrder(order)}
                        onKeyDown={(event) => {
                          if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
                            event.preventDefault();
                            onSelectOrder(order);
                          }
                        }}
                        tabIndex={0}
                        role="button"
                        aria-label={`View order ${order.order_number} from ${order.customer_name}`}
                      >
                        <td className="admin-orders-table-order">{order.order_number}</td>
                        <td className="admin-orders-table-customer">{order.customer_name}</td>
                        <td>
                          {new Date(order.created_at).toLocaleDateString('en-ZA', {
                            day: 'numeric',
                            month: 'short',
                            year: 'numeric',
                          })}
                        </td>
                        <td className="admin-orders-table-number">
                          {totalQuantity} {totalQuantity === 1 ? 'item' : 'items'}
                        </td>
                        <td className="admin-orders-table-total">{formatPrice(order.subtotal)}</td>
                        <td>
                          <span className={`admin-order-status admin-order-status-${order.status}`}>
                            {order.status}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    );
                  })}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="mt-12 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-[#4d4d4d]/10 pt-6">
        <p className="admin-type-supporting text-sm font-medium text-[#4d4d4d]/50">
          Showing {Math.min((orderPage - 1) * 20 + 1, filteredOrders.length)}–{Math.min(orderPage * 20, filteredOrders.length)} of {filteredOrders.length}
        </p>

        {totalOrderPages > 1 && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
            <button
              onClick={() => onPageChange(Math.max(1, orderPage - 1))}
              disabled={orderPage === 1}
              className="border-0 bg-transparent px-2 py-1 admin-type-body text-sm font-medium text-[#4d4d4d]/60 transition-colors hover:text-[#4d4d4d] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-30"
            >
              ←
            </button>

            {Array.from({ length: totalOrderPages }, (_, i) => i + 1).map((page) => (
              <button
                key={page}
                onClick={() => onPageChange(page)}
                className={`min-w-[32px] border-0 bg-transparent px-2 py-1 admin-type-numeric text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${
                  page === orderPage
                    ? 'text-[#4d4d4d] underline underline-offset-4 decoration-1'
                    : 'text-[#4d4d4d]/40 hover:text-[#4d4d4d]'
                }`}
              >
                {page}
              </button>
            ))}

            <button
              onClick={() => onPageChange(Math.min(totalOrderPages, orderPage + 1))}
              disabled={orderPage === totalOrderPages}
              className="border-0 bg-transparent px-2 py-1 admin-type-body text-sm font-medium text-[#4d4d4d]/60 transition-colors hover:text-[#4d4d4d] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-30"
            >
              →
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/components/AdminOverview.tsx =====
import type { Order, AdminRow } from '../types/admin.types';
import OverviewHeader from './OverviewHeader';
import StatsRow from './StatsRow';
import StorePerformanceCard from './StorePerformanceCard';
import SectionHeader from './SectionHeader';

type AdminOverviewProps = {
  email: string;
  searchQuery: string;
  onSearchChange: (value: string) => void;
  onSearchFocus: () => void;
  onAddProduct: () => void;
  activeFilter?: string;
  onFilterSelect?: (filterId: string) => void;
  activeMonth?: string;
  onMonthSelect?: (month: string | undefined) => void;
  orders?: Order[];
  products?: AdminRow[];
  stats?: { total: number; active: number; hidden: number };
  orderStats?: {
    total: number;
    pending: number;
    confirmed: number;
    delivered: number;
    cancelled: number;
  };
};

export default function AdminOverview({
  email,
  searchQuery,
  onSearchChange,
  onSearchFocus,
  onAddProduct,
  activeFilter,
  onFilterSelect,
  activeMonth,
  onMonthSelect,
  orders = [],
  products = [],
  stats = { total: 0, active: 0, hidden: 0 },
  orderStats = { total: 0, pending: 0, confirmed: 0, delivered: 0, cancelled: 0 },
}: AdminOverviewProps) {
  return (
    <section
      className="admin-overview flex w-full flex-col"
      style={{ gap: 'var(--admin-space-xl)' }}
    >
      <OverviewHeader
        email={email}
        onAddProduct={onAddProduct}
        searchQuery={searchQuery}
        onSearchChange={onSearchChange}
        onSearchFocus={onSearchFocus}
        activeFilter={activeFilter}
        onFilterSelect={onFilterSelect}
      />
      <StatsRow
        totalOrders={orderStats.total}
        pendingOrders={orderStats.pending}
        confirmedOrders={orderStats.confirmed}
        deliveredOrders={orderStats.delivered}
        cancelledOrders={orderStats.cancelled}
        totalProducts={stats.total}
        activeProducts={stats.active}
        inactiveProducts={stats.hidden}
        activeFilter={activeFilter}
        orders={orders}
        products={products}
      />
      <div className="flex flex-col" style={{ gap: 'var(--admin-space-md)' }}>
        <SectionHeader
          title="Revenue performance"
          description="Where the money is going — monthly revenue by category."
        />
        <StorePerformanceCard orders={orders} products={products} activeFilter={activeFilter} />
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/components/AdminPageHeader.tsx =====
import { FiSearch } from 'react-icons/fi';
import type { AdminPageHeaderProps } from '../types/admin.types';

export default function AdminPageHeader({
  title,
  eyebrow,
  description,
  searchValue,
  searchPlaceholder,
  onSearchChange,
  onSearchFocus,
  action,
  filters,
}: AdminPageHeaderProps) {
  return (
    <div className="admin-section-header">
      <div>
        {eyebrow && (
          <p className="admin-type-supporting uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-[#4d4d4d]/45">
            {eyebrow}
          </p>
        )}
        <h1 className="admin-section-header-title admin-type-page-title text-[#4d4d4d]">
          {title}
        </h1>
        {description && (
          <p className="admin-type-body text-[#4d4d4d]/65">
            {description}
          </p>
        )}
      </div>

      {(searchPlaceholder || action) && (
        <div className="admin-section-header-row flex w-full items-center">
          {searchPlaceholder && (
            <div className="admin-search-control flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center border border-black/10 bg-[#f5f5f5]">
              <FiSearch size={16} className="shrink-0 text-[#4d4d4d]/40" />

              <input
                type="text"
                value={searchValue ?? ''}
                onFocus={onSearchFocus}
                onChange={(event) => onSearchChange?.(event.target.value)}
                placeholder={searchPlaceholder}
                aria-label={searchPlaceholder}
                className="admin-type-body w-full min-w-0 border-0 bg-transparent text-[#4d4d4d] outline-none placeholder:text-[#4d4d4d]/40 focus:outline-none focus:ring-0"
              />
            </div>
          )}

          {action}
        </div>
      )}

      {filters && (
        <div className="admin-section-header-filters flex flex-wrap">
          {filters}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/components/AdminProductDetailsModal.tsx =====
import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { FiEye, FiEyeOff, FiTrash2 } from 'react-icons/fi';
import MenuIcon from '../../../components/MenuIcon';
import { formatPrice } from '../../../utils/formatPrice';
import { getCategoryTheme } from '../../../utils/categoryTheme';
import Modal from '../../../components/Modal';
import type { AdminRow } from '../types/admin.types';

type AdminProductDetailsModalProps = {
  product: AdminRow | null;
  onClose: () => void;
  onEdit: (product: AdminRow) => void;
  onHide: (product: AdminRow) => void;
  onShow: (product: AdminRow) => void;
  onDelete: (product: AdminRow) => void;
};

export default function AdminProductDetailsModal({
  product,
  onClose,
  onEdit,
  onHide,
  onShow,
  onDelete,
}: AdminProductDetailsModalProps) {
  const [isMenuOpen, setIsMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [menuHovered, setMenuHovered] = useState(false);
  const menuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isMenuOpen) return;
    const handler = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (menuRef.current && !menuRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setIsMenuOpen(false);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handler);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handler);
  }, [isMenuOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setIsMenuOpen(false);
  }, [product?.id]);

  if (!product) return null;

  const theme = getCategoryTheme(product.category);

  return (
    <Modal isOpen={!!product} onClose={onClose}>
      <div className="relative">
        <div className="absolute right-4 top-4 z-20" ref={menuRef}>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setIsMenuOpen(!isMenuOpen)}
            onMouseEnter={() => setMenuHovered(true)}
            onMouseLeave={() => setMenuHovered(false)}
            className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center border-0 admin-color-primary backdrop-blur-sm transition-colors"
            aria-label="Product options"
            style={{
              WebkitAppearance: 'none',
              appearance: 'none',
              backgroundColor: 'color-mix(in srgb, var(--admin-surface) 80%, transparent)',
            }}
          >
            <MenuIcon isOpen={isMenuOpen} isHovered={menuHovered} size={22} />
          </button>

          {isMenuOpen && (
            <div
              className="absolute right-0 top-full z-30 mt-1 w-44 shadow-xl"
              style={{
                backgroundColor: 'var(--admin-surface)',
                borderWidth: '1px',
                borderStyle: 'solid',
                borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-subtle)',
              }}
            >
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setIsMenuOpen(false);
                  if (product.is_active) onHide(product);
                  else onShow(product);
                }}
                className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 border-0 bg-transparent px-4 py-3 text-left admin-type-body text-sm admin-color-primary transition-colors"
                style={{ WebkitAppearance: 'none', appearance: 'none' }}
                onMouseEnter={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = 'var(--admin-surface-subtle)'; }}
                onMouseLeave={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = 'transparent'; }}
              >
                {product.is_active ? (
                  <><FiEyeOff size={14} /> Hide from store</>
                ) : (
                  <><FiEye size={14} /> Show in store</>
                )}
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setIsMenuOpen(false);
                  onDelete(product);
                }}
                className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 border-0 border-t bg-transparent px-4 py-3 text-left admin-type-body text-sm transition-colors"
                style={{
                  WebkitAppearance: 'none',
                  appearance: 'none',
                  borderTopColor: 'var(--admin-border-subtle)',
                  color: 'var(--admin-status-red)',
                }}
                onMouseEnter={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = 'color-mix(in srgb, var(--admin-status-red) 8%, transparent)'; }}
                onMouseLeave={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = 'transparent'; }}
              >
                <FiTrash2 size={14} /> Delete permanently
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="p-6 sm:p-8">
          <div
            className={`h-48 w-full overflow-hidden ${
              product.is_active ? theme.placeholderColor : 'bg-[var(--admin-surface-subtle)]'
            }`}
          >
            {product.image_url && (
              <img
                src={product.image_url}
                alt={product.name}
                className="h-full w-full object-contain p-6"
              />
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col gap-4">
            <div>
              <span className="mb-1.5 block admin-type-micro font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-[var(--admin-charcoal-40)]">
                Name
              </span>
              <div className="w-full bg-[var(--admin-surface)] px-4 py-3 admin-type-body admin-color-primary">
                {product.name}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div>
                <span className="mb-1.5 block admin-type-micro font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-[var(--admin-charcoal-40)]">
                  Price (R)
                </span>
                <div className="w-full bg-[var(--admin-surface)] px-4 py-3 admin-type-body admin-color-primary">
                  {formatPrice(product.price)}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <span className="mb-1.5 block admin-type-micro font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-[var(--admin-charcoal-40)]">
                  Category
                </span>
                <div className="w-full bg-[var(--admin-surface)] px-4 py-3 admin-type-body admin-color-primary">
                  {product.category}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div>
                <span className="mb-1.5 block admin-type-micro font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-[var(--admin-charcoal-40)]">
                  Supplier Cost (R)
                </span>
                <div className="w-full bg-[var(--admin-surface)] px-4 py-3 admin-type-body admin-color-primary">
                  {product.supplier_cost != null
                    ? formatPrice(product.supplier_cost)
                    : 'Not recorded'}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <span className="mb-1.5 block admin-type-micro font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-[var(--admin-charcoal-40)]">
                  SKU
                </span>
                <div className="w-full bg-[var(--admin-surface)] px-4 py-3 admin-type-body admin-color-primary">
                  {product.sku || 'Not assigned'}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div>
                <span className="mb-1.5 block admin-type-micro font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-[var(--admin-charcoal-40)]">
                  Subcategory
                </span>
                <div className="w-full bg-[var(--admin-surface)] px-4 py-3 admin-type-body admin-color-primary">
                  {product.subcategory}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <span className="mb-1.5 block admin-type-micro font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-[var(--admin-charcoal-40)]">
                  Status
                </span>
                <div className={`w-full bg-[var(--admin-surface)] px-4 py-3 admin-type-body ${
                  product.is_active ? 'admin-color-primary' : 'text-[var(--admin-status-red)]'
                }`}>
                  {product.is_active ? 'Active' : 'Inactive'}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <span className="mb-1.5 block admin-type-micro font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-[var(--admin-charcoal-40)]">
                Description
              </span>
              <div className="w-full bg-[var(--admin-surface)] px-4 py-3 admin-type-body leading-[1.4] admin-color-primary">
                {product.description || 'No description for this product.'}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-6 flex items-center gap-3">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="flex h-12 flex-1 items-center justify-center border-0 bg-[var(--admin-surface)] admin-type-body admin-color-primary transition-colors"
              onMouseEnter={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = 'var(--admin-surface-subtle)'; }}
              onMouseLeave={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = 'var(--admin-surface)'; }}
            >
              Cancel
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => { onClose(); onEdit(product); }}
              className="flex h-12 flex-1 items-center justify-center border border-solid admin-type-body text-white transition-colors"
              style={{
                backgroundColor: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
                borderColor: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
                color: 'var(--admin-bg)',
              }}
            >
              Edit
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/components/AdminProductForm.tsx =====
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { supabase } from '../../../utils/supabase';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
import Modal from '../../../components/Modal';
import CustomDropdown from './AdminDropdown';

export type AdminProduct = {
  id?: string;
  storefront: 'yotaabo' | 'poshies';
  name: string;
  price: number;
  supplier_cost?: number | null;
  sku?: string | null;
  category: string;
  category_id: string | null;
  subcategory: string;
  subcategory_id: string | null;
  description: string | null;
  image_url: string | null;
  is_active: boolean;
  sort_order: number;
};

type AdminProductFormProps = {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSaved: () => void;
  productToEdit?: AdminProduct | null;
  adminRole?: 'super_admin' | 'store_admin' | null;
  storeSlug?: 'yotaabo' | 'poshies' | null;
};

type CatalogueSubcategory = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  slug: string;
  sort_order: number;
  is_active: boolean;
};

type CatalogueCategory = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  slug: string;
  sort_order: number;
  is_active: boolean;
  subcategories: CatalogueSubcategory[];
};

const DRAFT_STORAGE_KEY = 'yotaabo-admin-product-form-draft';

const getProductStoragePath = (imageUrl: string | null | undefined) => {
  if (!imageUrl) return null;

  try {
    const url = new URL(imageUrl);
    const marker = '/storage/v1/object/public/products/';
    const index = url.pathname.indexOf(marker);

    if (index === -1) return null;

    return decodeURIComponent(url.pathname.slice(index + marker.length));
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
};

const emptyProduct: AdminProduct = {
  storefront: 'yotaabo',
  name: '',
  price: 0,
  supplier_cost: null,
  sku: '',
  category: '',
  category_id: null,
  subcategory: '',
  subcategory_id: null,
  description: '',
  image_url: '',
  is_active: true,
  sort_order: 0,
};

export default function AdminProductForm({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  onSaved,
  productToEdit,
  adminRole,
  storeSlug,
}: AdminProductFormProps) {
  const [form, setForm] = useState<AdminProduct>(emptyProduct);
  const [statusValue, setStatusValue] = useState<string>('');
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);
  const [isUploading, setIsUploading] = useState(false);
  const [isDraftHydrated, setIsDraftHydrated] = useState(false);
  const [categories, setCategories] = useState<CatalogueCategory[]>([]);
  const [categoriesStorefront, setCategoriesStorefront] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isLoadingCategories, setIsLoadingCategories] = useState(false);
  const [categoryLoadError, setCategoryLoadError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) {
      setIsDraftHydrated(false);
      return;
    }

    setIsDraftHydrated(false);

    try {
      const raw = sessionStorage.getItem(DRAFT_STORAGE_KEY);
      const saved = raw
        ? JSON.parse(raw) as {
            productId: string | null;
            form: AdminProduct;
            statusValue: string;
          }
        : null;

      const currentProductId = productToEdit?.id ?? null;

      if (
        saved &&
        saved.productId === currentProductId &&
        saved.form
      ) {
        setForm({
          ...emptyProduct,
          ...saved.form,
          storefront: adminRole === 'store_admin' && storeSlug ? storeSlug : saved.form.storefront === 'poshies' ? 'poshies' : 'yotaabo',
        });
        setStatusValue(saved.statusValue ?? '');
      } else {
        setForm(productToEdit
          ? {
              ...emptyProduct,
              ...productToEdit,
              storefront: adminRole === 'store_admin' && storeSlug ? storeSlug : productToEdit.storefront === 'poshies' ? 'poshies' : 'yotaabo',
            }
          : {
            ...emptyProduct,
            storefront: adminRole === 'store_admin' && storeSlug ? storeSlug : emptyProduct.storefront,
          });
        setStatusValue(
          productToEdit
            ? productToEdit.is_active ? 'active' : 'hidden'
            : ''
        );
      }
    } catch {
      setForm(productToEdit
        ? {
            ...emptyProduct,
            ...productToEdit,
            storefront: adminRole === 'store_admin' && storeSlug ? storeSlug : productToEdit.storefront === 'poshies' ? 'poshies' : 'yotaabo',
          }
        : {
            ...emptyProduct,
            storefront: adminRole === 'store_admin' && storeSlug ? storeSlug : emptyProduct.storefront,
          });
      setStatusValue(
        productToEdit
          ? productToEdit.is_active ? 'active' : 'hidden'
          : ''
      );
    }

    setIsDraftHydrated(true);
  }, [isOpen, productToEdit, adminRole, storeSlug]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen || !isDraftHydrated) {
      setCategories([]);
      setCategoriesStorefront(null);
      setCategoryLoadError(null);
      return;
    }

    let cancelled = false;
    const storefront = form.storefront;

    setCategories([]);
    setCategoriesStorefront(null);
    setCategoryLoadError(null);
    setIsLoadingCategories(true);

    const loadCategories = async () => {
      try {
        const {
          data: { session },
        } = await supabase.auth.getSession();

        if (!session?.access_token) {
          throw new Error('Your session has expired. Please sign in again.');
        }

        const response = await fetch(
          `/api/categories?storefront=${encodeURIComponent(storefront)}&scope=admin`,
          {
            headers: {
              Authorization: `Bearer ${session.access_token}`,
            },
          }
        );

        const data = await response.json();

        if (!response.ok) {
          throw new Error(data.error || 'Unable to load categories.');
        }

        if (!Array.isArray(data.categories)) {
          throw new Error('The categories response is invalid.');
        }

        if (cancelled) return;

        setCategories(data.categories as CatalogueCategory[]);
        setCategoriesStorefront(storefront);
      } catch (error) {
        if (cancelled) return;

        setCategoryLoadError(
          error instanceof Error
            ? error.message
            : 'Unable to load categories.'
        );
      } finally {
        if (!cancelled) setIsLoadingCategories(false);
      }
    };

    void loadCategories();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [isOpen, isDraftHydrated, form.storefront]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (
      !isOpen ||
      !isDraftHydrated ||
      isLoadingCategories ||
      categoryLoadError ||
      categoriesStorefront !== form.storefront ||
      categories.length === 0
    ) {
      return;
    }

    setForm((prev) => {
      const legacyCategoryName =
        prev.storefront === 'poshies' && prev.category.toLowerCase() === 'spaces'
          ? 'Lounge'
          : prev.category;

      const category =
        categories.find((item) => item.id === prev.category_id) ||
        categories.find(
          (item) => item.name.toLowerCase() === legacyCategoryName.toLowerCase()
        ) ||
        (!prev.category && !prev.category_id
          ? categories.find((item) => item.is_active)
          : undefined);

      if (!category) return prev;

      const subcategory =
        category.subcategories.find(
          (item) => item.id === prev.subcategory_id
        ) ||
        category.subcategories.find(
          (item) => item.name.toLowerCase() === prev.subcategory.toLowerCase()
        ) ||
        (!prev.subcategory && !prev.subcategory_id
          ? category.subcategories.find((item) => item.is_active)
          : undefined);

      const nextSubcategory = subcategory ?? null;

      if (
        prev.category_id === category.id &&
        prev.category === category.name &&
        prev.subcategory_id === (nextSubcategory?.id ?? null) &&
        prev.subcategory === (nextSubcategory?.name ?? '')
      ) {
        return prev;
      }

      return {
        ...prev,
        category_id: category.id,
        category: category.name,
        subcategory_id: nextSubcategory?.id ?? null,
        subcategory: nextSubcategory?.name ?? '',
      };
    });
  }, [
    isOpen,
    isDraftHydrated,
    isLoadingCategories,
    categoryLoadError,
    categoriesStorefront,
    form.storefront,
    form.category,
    form.category_id,
    form.subcategory,
    form.subcategory_id,
    categories,
  ]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen || !isDraftHydrated) return;

    try {
      sessionStorage.setItem(
        DRAFT_STORAGE_KEY,
        JSON.stringify({
          productId: productToEdit?.id ?? null,
          form,
          statusValue,
        })
      );
    } catch {
      // Storage may be unavailable; the form remains usable.
    }
  }, [isOpen, isDraftHydrated, form, statusValue, productToEdit]);

  const handleClose = () => {
    try {
      sessionStorage.removeItem(DRAFT_STORAGE_KEY);
    } catch {
      // Ignore storage errors when closing.
    }

    setIsDraftHydrated(false);
    onClose();
  };

  const handleChange = (field: keyof AdminProduct, value: any) => {
    if (field === 'category') {
      const selected = categories.find((item) => item.id === value);
      if (!selected) return;

      const firstSubcategory =
        selected.subcategories.find((item) => item.is_active) ?? null;

      setForm((prev) => ({
        ...prev,
        category: selected.name,
        category_id: selected.id,
        subcategory: firstSubcategory?.name ?? '',
        subcategory_id: firstSubcategory?.id ?? null,
      }));
      return;
    }

    if (field === 'subcategory') {
      const selected = categories
        .find((item) => item.id === form.category_id)
        ?.subcategories.find((item) => item.id === value);

      if (!selected) return;

      setForm((prev) => ({
        ...prev,
        subcategory: selected.name,
        subcategory_id: selected.id,
      }));
      return;
    }

    if (field === 'storefront') {
      setForm((prev) => ({
        ...prev,
        storefront: value,
        category: '',
        category_id: null,
        subcategory: '',
        subcategory_id: null,
      }));
      return;
    }

    setForm((prev) => ({ ...prev, [field]: value }));
  };

  const handleImageUpload = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const input = e.currentTarget;
    const file = input.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;

    setIsUploading(true);

    try {
      const supportedTypes = ['image/jpeg', 'image/png', 'image/webp'];

      if (!supportedTypes.includes(file.type)) {
        throw new Error('Please select a JPEG, PNG, or WebP image.');
      }

      const optimizedFile = await new Promise<File>((resolve, reject) => {
        const objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file);
        const image = new Image();

        const cleanup = () => URL.revokeObjectURL(objectUrl);

        image.onload = () => {
          try {
            const maxDimension = 1600;
            const scale = Math.min(
              1,
              maxDimension / Math.max(image.naturalWidth, image.naturalHeight)
            );

            const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
            canvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(image.naturalWidth * scale));
            canvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(image.naturalHeight * scale));

            const context = canvas.getContext('2d');

            if (!context) {
              throw new Error('Could not process this image.');
            }

            context.drawImage(image, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

            canvas.toBlob(
              (blob) => {
                cleanup();

                if (!blob || blob.type !== 'image/webp') {
                  reject(new Error('Your browser could not convert this image to WebP.'));
                  return;
                }

                const filename = file.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '') || 'product';

                resolve(
                  new File([blob], `${filename}.webp`, {
                    type: 'image/webp',
                    lastModified: Date.now(),
                  })
                );
              },
              'image/webp',
              0.82
            );
          } catch (error) {
            cleanup();
            reject(error);
          }
        };

        image.onerror = () => {
          cleanup();
          reject(new Error('Could not read this image. Try another file.'));
        };

        image.src = objectUrl;
      });

      const path = `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2)}.webp`;

      const { error } = await supabase.storage
        .from('products')
        .upload(path, optimizedFile, {
          cacheControl: '31536000',
          contentType: 'image/webp',
          upsert: false,
        });

      if (error) {
        throw new Error(error.message);
      }

      const {
        data: { publicUrl },
      } = supabase.storage.from('products').getPublicUrl(path);

      setForm((prev) => ({ ...prev, image_url: publicUrl }));
      toast.success('Image optimised and uploaded');
    } catch (error) {
      const message =
        error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'An unexpected error occurred.';

      toast.error('Image upload failed: ' + message);
    } finally {
      setIsUploading(false);
      input.value = '';
    }
  };

  const handleSubmit = async () => {
    if (!form.name.trim()) return toast.error('Name is required');
    if (form.price <= 0) return toast.error('Price must be greater than 0');
    if (
      form.supplier_cost != null &&
      (!Number.isFinite(form.supplier_cost) || form.supplier_cost < 0)
    ) return toast.error('Supplier cost must be zero or greater');
    if (!statusValue) return toast.error('Status is required');
    if (isLoadingCategories) return toast.error('Categories are still loading');
    if (categoryLoadError) return toast.error('Categories could not be loaded');
    if (!form.category_id) return toast.error('Select a valid category');
    if (!form.subcategory_id) return toast.error('Select a valid subcategory');

    setIsSaving(true);
    const payload = {
      storefront: form.storefront,
      name: form.name.trim(),
      price: form.price,
      supplier_cost: form.supplier_cost ?? null,
      sku: form.sku?.trim().toUpperCase() || null,
      category: form.category,
      category_id: form.category_id,
      subcategory: form.subcategory,
      subcategory_id: form.subcategory_id,
      description: form.description?.trim() || null,
      image_url: form.image_url?.trim() || null,
      is_active: form.is_active,
      sort_order: form.sort_order,
    };

    try {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();

      if (!session?.access_token) {
        throw new Error('Your session has expired. Please sign in again.');
      }

      const response = await fetch('/api/products', {
        method: productToEdit?.id ? 'PATCH' : 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          Authorization: `Bearer ${session.access_token}`,
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          ...payload,
          ...(productToEdit?.id ? { id: productToEdit.id } : {}),
        }),
      });

      const responseText = await response.text();
      let data: { error?: string; product?: AdminProduct } = {};

      if (responseText.trim()) {
        try {
          data = JSON.parse(responseText);
        } catch {
          // Handle non-JSON server responses below.
        }
      }

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(data.error || 'Unable to save the product.');
      }

      if (!data.product) {
        throw new Error('The server did not confirm the saved product.');
      }

      if (
        productToEdit?.image_url &&
        productToEdit.image_url !== payload.image_url
      ) {
        const oldImagePath = getProductStoragePath(productToEdit.image_url);

        if (oldImagePath) {
          const { error: deleteError } = await supabase.storage
            .from('products')
            .remove([oldImagePath]);

          if (deleteError) {
            console.error('Previous product image could not be deleted:', deleteError);
            toast.error('Product saved, but the previous image could not be deleted.');
          }
        }
      }

      toast.success(productToEdit ? 'Product updated' : 'Product added');
      onSaved();
      handleClose();
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error(
        error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unable to save the product.'
      );
    } finally {
      setIsSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const selectedCategory = categories.find(
    (item) => item.id === form.category_id
  );

  const categoryOptions = categories
    .filter((item) => item.is_active || item.id === form.category_id)
    .map((item) => ({
      value: item.id,
      label: item.is_active ? item.name : `${item.name} (Inactive)`,
    }));

  const subcategoryOptions = (selectedCategory?.subcategories ?? [])
    .filter((item) => item.is_active || item.id === form.subcategory_id)
    .map((item) => ({
      value: item.id,
      label: item.is_active ? item.name : `${item.name} (Inactive)`,
    }));

  const inputClass =
    'w-full border-0 bg-[var(--admin-surface-subtle)] px-4 py-3 admin-type-body text-[var(--admin-text-primary)] outline-none ring-0 transition-colors placeholder:text-[var(--admin-text-tertiary)] focus:border-0 focus:outline-none focus:ring-0';

  return (
    <Modal isOpen={isOpen} onClose={handleClose}>
      <div className="p-6 sm:p-8">
        <h2 className="admin-type-section-title text-[var(--admin-text-primary)]">
          {productToEdit ? 'Edit Product' : 'Add Product'}
        </h2>

        <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col gap-4">
          <label className="block">
            <span className="mb-1.5 block admin-type-micro font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-[var(--admin-text-tertiary)]">Name</span>
            <input type="text" value={form.name} onChange={(e) => handleChange('name', e.target.value)} className={inputClass} placeholder="e.g. Noir Oud" />
          </label>

          {adminRole === 'super_admin' ? (
            <label className="block">
              <span className="mb-1.5 block admin-type-micro font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-[var(--admin-text-tertiary)]">Storefront</span>
              <CustomDropdown
                value={form.storefront}
                onChange={(value) => handleChange('storefront', value)}
                options={[
                  { value: 'yotaabo', label: 'Yotaabo' },
                  { value: 'poshies', label: 'Poshies' },
                ]}
                placeholder="Select storefront"
              />
            </label>
          ) : (
            <div>
              <span className="mb-1.5 block admin-type-micro font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-[var(--admin-text-tertiary)]">Storefront</span>
              <div className="border border-solid border-[var(--admin-border-strong)] px-3 py-2 text-sm text-[var(--admin-text-primary)]">
                {form.storefront === 'poshies' ? 'Poshies' : 'Yotaabo'}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <label className="block">
              <span className="mb-1.5 block admin-type-micro font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-[var(--admin-text-tertiary)]">Price (R)</span>
              <input type="number" value={form.price || ''} onChange={(e) => handleChange('price', Number(e.target.value))} className={`${inputClass} admin-type-price`} placeholder="0" />
            </label>

            <label className="block">
              <span className="mb-1.5 block admin-type-micro font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-[var(--admin-text-tertiary)]">Status</span>
              <CustomDropdown
                value={statusValue}
                onChange={(v) => { setStatusValue(v); handleChange('is_active', v === 'active'); }}
                options={[
                  { value: 'active', label: 'Active' },
                  { value: 'hidden', label: 'Hidden' },
                ]}
                placeholder="Select"
              />
            </label>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <label className="block">
              <span className="mb-1.5 block admin-type-micro font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-[var(--admin-text-tertiary)]">Supplier Cost (R)</span>
              <input
                type="number"
                min="0"
                step="0.01"
                value={form.supplier_cost ?? ''}
                onChange={(e) => handleChange(
                  'supplier_cost',
                  e.target.value === '' ? null : Number(e.target.value)
                )}
                className={`${inputClass} admin-type-price`}
                placeholder="Not recorded"
              />
            </label>

            <label className="block">
              <span className="mb-1.5 block admin-type-micro font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-[var(--admin-text-tertiary)]">SKU</span>
              <input
                type="text"
                maxLength={80}
                value={form.sku ?? ''}
                onChange={(e) => handleChange('sku', e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
                className={inputClass}
                placeholder="e.g. YOT-001"
              />
            </label>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <label className="block">
              <span className="mb-1.5 block admin-type-micro font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-[var(--admin-text-tertiary)]">Category</span>
              <CustomDropdown
                value={form.category_id ?? ''}
                onChange={(v) => handleChange('category', v)}
                options={categoryOptions}
                placeholder={
                  isLoadingCategories
                    ? 'Loading categories...'
                    : categoryLoadError
                      ? 'Categories unavailable'
                      : 'Select'
                }
              />
            </label>

            <label className="block">
              <span className="mb-1.5 block admin-type-micro font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-[var(--admin-text-tertiary)]">Subcategory</span>
              <CustomDropdown
                value={form.subcategory_id ?? ''}
                onChange={(v) => handleChange('subcategory', v)}
                options={subcategoryOptions}
                placeholder={
                  !form.category_id
                    ? 'Select category first'
                    : isLoadingCategories
                      ? 'Loading subcategories...'
                      : 'Select'
                }
              />
            </label>
          </div>

          <label className="block">
            <span className="mb-1.5 block admin-type-micro font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-[var(--admin-text-tertiary)]">Description</span>
            <textarea rows={3} value={form.description ?? ''} onChange={(e) => handleChange('description', e.target.value)} className={`resize-none ${inputClass}`} placeholder="Write a short product description..." />
          </label>

          <div>
            <span className="mb-1.5 block admin-type-micro font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-[var(--admin-text-tertiary)]">Product Image</span>

            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <input
                type="url"
                value={form.image_url ?? ''}
                onChange={(e) => handleChange('image_url', e.target.value)}
                className={inputClass}
                placeholder="https://..."
              />

              <label className={`flex h-[46px] shrink-0 cursor-pointer items-center justify-center border-0 bg-[var(--admin-text-primary)] px-4 admin-type-body text-white transition-colors hover:opacity-90 ${isUploading ? 'cursor-not-allowed opacity-50' : ''}`}>
                {isUploading ? '...' : form.image_url?.trim() ? 'Change' : 'Upload'}
                <input
                  type="file"
                  accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp"
                  className="hidden"
                  onChange={handleImageUpload}
                  disabled={isUploading}
                />
              </label>
            </div>

            {form.image_url?.trim() && (
              <div className="mt-3 flex h-40 w-full items-center justify-center overflow-hidden bg-[var(--admin-surface-subtle)] p-3">
                <img
                  src={form.image_url}
                  alt="Product preview"
                  className="h-full w-full object-contain"
                />
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="mt-6 flex items-center gap-3">
            <button type="button" onClick={handleClose} className="flex h-12 flex-1 items-center justify-center border-0 bg-[var(--admin-surface-subtle)] admin-type-body text-[var(--admin-text-primary)] transition-colors hover:bg-[var(--admin-hover)]">
              Cancel
            </button>
            <button type="button" onClick={handleSubmit} disabled={isSaving} className="flex h-12 flex-1 items-center justify-center border border-solid border-[var(--admin-border-strong)] bg-[var(--admin-text-primary)] admin-type-body text-white transition-colors hover:bg-transparent hover:text-[var(--admin-text-primary)] disabled:opacity-50">
              {isSaving ? (
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                  <span className="loading-dot"></span>
                  <span className="loading-dot"></span>
                  <span className="loading-dot"></span>
                </span>
              ) : productToEdit ? 'Save Changes' : 'Add Product'}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/components/AdminProducts.tsx =====
import { FiPlus } from 'react-icons/fi';
import AdminItemCard from '../../../components/AdminItemCard';
import AdminPageHeader from './AdminPageHeader';
import { formatPrice } from '../../../utils/formatPrice';
import { getCategoryTheme } from '../../../utils/categoryTheme';
import type {
  AdminRow,
  CategoryFilter,
} from '../types/admin.types';

type AdminProductsProps = {
  searchQuery: string;
  categoryFilter: CategoryFilter;
  filteredProducts: AdminRow[];
  paginatedProducts: AdminRow[];
  currentPage: number;
  totalPages: number;
  onSearchChange: (value: string) => void;
  onCategoryChange: (value: CategoryFilter) => void;
  onPageChange: (page: number) => void;
  onAddProduct: () => void;
  onSelectProduct: (product: AdminRow) => void;
};

export default function AdminProducts({
  searchQuery,
  categoryFilter,
  filteredProducts,
  paginatedProducts,
  currentPage,
  totalPages,
  onSearchChange,
  onCategoryChange,
  onPageChange,
  onAddProduct,
  onSelectProduct,
}: AdminProductsProps) {
  return (
    <>
      <AdminPageHeader
        title="Products"
        searchValue={searchQuery}
        searchPlaceholder="Search products"
        onSearchChange={onSearchChange}
        action={
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onAddProduct}
            className="admin-square-action inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center border border-solid border-[#4d4d4d] bg-[#4d4d4d] text-white transition-colors hover:bg-transparent hover:text-[#4d4d4d]"
          >
            <FiPlus size={18} />
          </button>
        }
        filters={
          (['All', 'His', 'Hers', 'Spaces', 'Lounge', 'Hidden'] as CategoryFilter[]).map((cat) => (
            <button
              key={cat}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onCategoryChange(cat)}
              className={`border-0 bg-transparent px-0 py-1 admin-type-body text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${
                categoryFilter === cat
                  ? 'text-[#4d4d4d] underline underline-offset-4 decoration-1'
                  : 'text-[#4d4d4d]/40 hover:text-[#4d4d4d]'
              }`}
            >
              {cat}
            </button>
          ))
        }
      />

      <div className="admin-product-list-region">
        {filteredProducts.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="admin-empty-state admin-type-supporting text-center text-[#4d4d4d]/40">
            No products match your filters.
          </p>
        ) : (
          <div className="admin-product-list flex flex-col">
            {paginatedProducts.map((product) => {
              const theme = getCategoryTheme(product.category);
              const isActive = product.is_active;

              return (
                <AdminItemCard
                  key={product.id}
                  layout="product"
                  title={product.name}
                  status={isActive ? 'Active' : 'Inactive'}
                  price={formatPrice(product.price)}
                  meta={`${product.category} · ${product.subcategory}`}
                  onClick={() => onSelectProduct(product)}
                  ariaLabel={`View product ${product.name}`}
                  leftClassName={
                    isActive ? theme.placeholderColor : 'bg-gray-300'
                  }
                  leftContent={
                    product.image_url ? (
                      <img
                        src={product.image_url}
                        alt={product.name}
                        loading="lazy"
                        className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full object-contain p-2"
                      />
                    ) : null
                  }
                  statusClassName={
                    isActive ? 'text-[#4d4d4d]/40' : 'text-red-500'
                  }
                  className={!isActive ? 'opacity-60' : ''}
                />
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="mt-12 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-[#4d4d4d]/10 pt-6">
        <p className="admin-type-supporting text-sm font-medium text-[#4d4d4d]/50">
          Showing {Math.min((currentPage - 1) * 20 + 1, filteredProducts.length)}–{Math.min(currentPage * 20, filteredProducts.length)} of {filteredProducts.length}
        </p>

        {totalPages > 1 && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
            <button
              onClick={() => onPageChange(Math.max(1, currentPage - 1))}
              disabled={currentPage === 1}
              className="border-0 bg-transparent px-2 py-1 admin-type-body text-sm font-medium text-[#4d4d4d]/60 transition-colors hover:text-[#4d4d4d] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-30"
            >
              ←
            </button>

            {Array.from({ length: totalPages }, (_, i) => i + 1)
              .filter((page) => {
                if (totalPages <= 7) return true;
                if (page === 1 || page === totalPages) return true;
                return Math.abs(page - currentPage) <= 1;
              })
              .map((page, index, pages) => {
                const previousPage = pages[index - 1];
                const showEllipsis =
                  previousPage && page - previousPage > 1;

                return (
                  <span key={page} className="flex items-center gap-1">
                    {showEllipsis && (
                      <span className="admin-type-numeric text-sm text-[#4d4d4d]/40">
                        …
                      </span>
                    )}
                    <button
                      onClick={() => onPageChange(page)}
                      className={`min-w-[32px] border-0 bg-transparent px-2 py-1 admin-type-numeric text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${
                        page === currentPage
                          ? 'text-[#4d4d4d] underline underline-offset-4 decoration-1'
                          : 'text-[#4d4d4d]/40 hover:text-[#4d4d4d]'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {page}
                    </button>
                  </span>
                );
              })}

            <button
              onClick={() => onPageChange(Math.min(totalPages, currentPage + 1))}
              disabled={currentPage === totalPages}
              className="border-0 bg-transparent px-2 py-1 admin-type-body text-sm font-medium text-[#4d4d4d]/60 transition-colors hover:text-[#4d4d4d] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-30"
            >
              →
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/components/AdminSettingsTab.tsx =====
import { useState } from 'react';
import { CARD_CHROME } from './shared/cardChrome';
import RequestPanel from './RequestPanel';
import NewRequestForm from './NewRequestForm';
import { useStoreRequests } from '../hooks/useStoreRequests';
import AdminCategories from './AdminCategories';
import AdminManagement from './AdminManagement';
import type { AdminTheme } from '../hooks/useAdminTheme';
import type { Storefront } from '../types/admin.types';

type AdminSettingsTabProps = {
  adminTheme: AdminTheme;
  toggleTheme: () => void;
  storefrontFilter: 'all' | Storefront;
  setStorefrontFilter: (v: 'all' | Storefront) => void;
  adminRole: string | undefined;
  adminStoreSlug: string | undefined;
  accessToken: string | undefined;
  onStorefrontChange: () => void;
};



export default function AdminSettingsTab({


  adminRole,
  adminStoreSlug,
  accessToken,
}: AdminSettingsTabProps) {
  const isSuperAdmin = adminRole === 'super_admin';

  // Store requests (general tickets to the technical team)
  const { requests, addRequest } = useStoreRequests();
  const [newRequestStore, setNewRequestStore] = useState<Storefront | null>(null);

  // For store admins: show only their assigned store.
  // For super admins: show both stores stacked.
  const storesToShow: { slug: Storefront; label: string; color: string }[] =
    adminStoreSlug === 'yotaabo' || adminStoreSlug === 'poshies'
      ? [{ slug: adminStoreSlug, label: adminStoreSlug === 'yotaabo' ? 'Yotaabo' : 'Poshies', color: adminStoreSlug === 'yotaabo' ? '#8aa89b' : '#ff1493' }]
      : [
          { slug: 'yotaabo' as Storefront, label: 'Yotaabo', color: '#8aa89b' },
          { slug: 'poshies' as Storefront, label: 'Poshies', color: '#ff1493' },
        ];

  return (
    <div className="flex w-full flex-col" style={{ gap: 'var(--admin-space-3xl)' }}>
      {/* Header — big title + theme toggle */}
      <header className="flex w-full flex-col" style={{ gap: 'var(--admin-space-xl)' }}>
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between" style={{ gap: 'var(--admin-space-md)' }}>
          <div className="flex flex-col" style={{ gap: 'var(--admin-space-xs)' }}>
            <h1
              style={{
                fontSize: 'var(--admin-greeting-size)',
                lineHeight: 'var(--admin-greeting-line)',
                fontWeight: 'var(--admin-greeting-weight)',
                color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
                margin: 0,
              }}
            >
              Settings
            </h1>
            <p
              style={{
                fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-supporting)',
                fontWeight: 'var(--admin-weight-normal)',
                color: 'var(--admin-text-secondary)',
                margin: 0,
                lineHeight: 1.5,
              }}
            >
              Manage your stores, categories, and admin access.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* Categories — one section per store */}
      {accessToken && adminRole && (
        <>
          {storesToShow.map((store) => (
            <section
              key={store.slug}
              className="flex w-full flex-col"
              style={{ gap: 'var(--admin-space-md)' }}
            >
              <div
                style={{
                  width: '100%',
                  backgroundColor: store.color,
                  borderRadius: 'var(--ds-radius-lg)',
                  paddingInline: 'var(--admin-space-md)',
                  paddingBlock: 'var(--admin-space-sm)',
                  display: 'flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  justifyContent: 'flex-start',
                }}
              >
                <h2
                  style={{
                    fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-heading-2)',
                    fontWeight: 'var(--admin-weight-semibold)',
                    color: 'var(--admin-on-accent)',
                    margin: 0,
                    lineHeight: 1.2,
                  }}
                >
                  {store.label} storefront
                </h2>
              </div>

              <AdminCategories
                accessToken={accessToken}
                adminRole={adminRole as 'super_admin' | 'store_admin'}
                assignedStorefront={store.slug}
              />

              <RequestPanel
                storeLabel={store.label}
                requests={requests.filter((r) => r.storefront === store.slug)}
                onNewRequest={() => setNewRequestStore(store.slug)}
              />
            </section>
          ))}
        </>
      )}

      {/* Admin Management — only for super admin */}
      {isSuperAdmin && accessToken && (
        <section className="flex w-full flex-col" style={{ gap: 'var(--admin-space-md)' }}>
          <div className="flex flex-col" style={{ gap: 'var(--admin-space-xs)' }}>
            <h2
              style={{
                fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-heading-2)',
                fontWeight: 'var(--admin-weight-semibold)',
                color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
                margin: 0,
              }}
            >
              Admin Management
            </h2>
            <p
              style={{
                fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-supporting)',
                color: 'var(--admin-text-secondary)',
                margin: 0,
                lineHeight: 1.5,
              }}
            >
              Manage administrator access and storefront assignments.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="admin-card-bg" style={CARD_CHROME}>
            <AdminManagement accessToken={accessToken} />
          </div>
        </section>
      )}

      {/* New request form — floats over the whole section */}
      {newRequestStore && (
        <NewRequestForm
          isOpen
          storefront={newRequestStore}
          storeLabel={newRequestStore === 'yotaabo' ? 'Yotaabo' : 'Poshies'}
          onClose={() => setNewRequestStore(null)}
          onSubmit={addRequest}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/components/AdminThemeShell.tsx =====
import type { CSSProperties, ReactNode } from 'react';
import { adminTypography, adminTypographyTokens, greetingTokens } from '../../../design-system/tokens/adminTypography';
import { adminColors } from '../../../design-system/tokens/adminColors';
import { getAdminThemeCssVariables } from '../../../design-system/themes/admin';
import { colors, adminSpacingVariables } from '../../../design-system/tokens';
import type { AdminTheme } from '../hooks/useAdminTheme';

type AdminThemeShellProps = {
  adminTheme: AdminTheme;
  children: ReactNode;
};

export default function AdminThemeShell({ adminTheme, children }: AdminThemeShellProps) {
  const themeColors = adminColors.themes[adminTheme];

  return (
    <main
      className="admin-scope min-h-screen bg-[var(--admin-bg)]"
      data-theme={adminTheme}
      style={
        {
          ...getAdminThemeCssVariables(adminTheme),
          '--admin-bg': themeColors.background,
          '--admin-surface': themeColors.surface,
          '--admin-surface-subtle': themeColors.surfaceSubtle,
          '--admin-surface-raised': themeColors.surfaceRaised,
          '--admin-text-primary': themeColors.text,
          '--admin-text-secondary': themeColors.textSecondary,
          '--admin-text-supporting': themeColors.textSecondary,
          '--admin-text-muted': themeColors.textMuted,
          '--admin-text-disabled': themeColors.textDisabled,
          '--admin-border-subtle': themeColors.border,
          '--admin-border-strong': themeColors.borderStrong,
          // HARDCODED — category header background.
          // Why: `mixHex()` produced an invalid value when used inside a CSS custom
          // property (empty string), and even a plain string variable failed to
          // apply after the first render cycle. A direct hex literal here is the
          // only form that reliably paints the header row in both themes.
          // Light: #E2E2E2 (grey header on white card)
          // Dark:  #3A3A3A (lighter grey header on #404040 card)
          '--admin-category-header-bg': adminTheme === 'dark' ? '#3A3A3A' : '#E2E2E2',

          '--admin-hover': themeColors.hover,
          '--admin-accent': adminColors.accent.primary,
          '--admin-accent-hover': adminColors.accent.hover,
          '--admin-accent-text': themeColors.accentText,
          '--admin-on-accent': adminColors.accent.onAccent,
          '--admin-focus': themeColors.focus,
          '--admin-shadow': themeColors.shadow,
          '--admin-charcoal-40':
            adminTheme === 'dark' ? 'rgba(244, 241, 236, 0.4)' : colors.opacity.charcoal40,
          '--admin-charcoal-50':
            adminTheme === 'dark' ? 'rgba(244, 241, 236, 0.5)' : colors.opacity.charcoal50,
          '--admin-charcoal-70':
            adminTheme === 'dark' ? 'rgba(244, 241, 236, 0.7)' : colors.opacity.charcoal70,
          '--admin-card-order-media-surface': themeColors.surfaceSubtle,
          '--admin-status-amber': colors.statusPalette.amber,
          '--admin-status-green': colors.statusPalette.green,
          '--admin-status-red': colors.statusPalette.red,

          ...adminSpacingVariables,

          '--admin-font-heading': adminTypography.heading,
          '--admin-font-body': adminTypography.body,

          '--admin-greeting-size': greetingTokens.fontSize,
          '--admin-greeting-line': greetingTokens.lineHeight,
          '--admin-greeting-weight': greetingTokens.fontWeight,
          '--admin-size-display': adminTypographyTokens.styles.display.fontSize,
          '--admin-line-display': adminTypographyTokens.styles.display.lineHeight,
          '--admin-weight-display': adminTypographyTokens.styles.display.fontWeight,
          '--admin-size-page-title': adminTypography.size.pageTitle,
          '--admin-size-section-title': adminTypography.size.sectionTitle,
          '--admin-size-card-title': adminTypography.size.cardTitle,
          '--admin-size-subsection-title': adminTypography.size.subsectionTitle,
          '--admin-size-body': adminTypography.size.body,
          '--admin-size-supporting': adminTypography.size.supporting,
          '--admin-size-micro': adminTypography.size.micro,

          '--admin-weight-thin': adminTypography.weight.thin,
          '--admin-weight-extra-light': adminTypography.weight.extraLight,
          '--admin-weight-normal': adminTypography.weight.normal,
          '--admin-weight-medium': adminTypography.weight.medium,
          '--admin-weight-semibold': adminTypography.weight.semibold,

          '--admin-line-tight': adminTypography.lineHeight.tight,
          '--admin-line-heading': adminTypography.lineHeight.heading,
          '--admin-line-body': adminTypography.lineHeight.body,
          '--admin-line-compact': adminTypography.lineHeight.compact,
        } as CSSProperties
      }
    >
      {children}
    </main>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/components/AdminTopBar.tsx =====
import { FiArrowLeft, FiBell, FiLogOut, FiMoon, FiSun } from 'react-icons/fi';
import AccentButton from './AccentButton';
import type { AdminTheme } from '../hooks/useAdminTheme';

type AdminTopBarProps = {
  email: string;
  onLogout: () => void;
  onBack?: () => void;
  onNotificationsClick?: () => void;
  isNotificationsActive?: boolean;
  adminTheme?: AdminTheme;
  onToggleTheme?: () => void;
};

export default function AdminTopBar({
  email,
  onLogout,
  onBack,
  onNotificationsClick,
  isNotificationsActive = false,
  adminTheme = 'light',
  onToggleTheme,
}: AdminTopBarProps) {
  return (
    <header
      className="sticky top-0 z-40 backdrop-blur"
      style={{ backgroundColor: 'var(--admin-surface, var(--admin-surface))' }}
    >
      <div
        className={`admin-topbar-inner mx-auto flex items-center ${
          onBack ? 'justify-between' : 'justify-end'
        }`}
      >
        {onBack && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onBack}
            aria-label="Back to overview"
            className="inline-flex min-h-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center border-0 bg-transparent px-2 opacity-70 transition-opacity hover:opacity-100"
            style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-secondary, rgba(77,77,77,0.60))' }}
          >
            <FiArrowLeft size={15} aria-hidden="true" />
          </button>
        )}
        <div
          className="flex shrink-0 items-center"
          style={{ gap: 'var(--admin-space-xs, 8px)' }}
        >
          <AccentButton
            variant={isNotificationsActive ? 'dark' : 'neutral'}
            shape="circle"
            ariaLabel="Notifications"
            onClick={onNotificationsClick}
          >
            <FiBell size={18} aria-hidden="true" />
          </AccentButton>
          {onToggleTheme && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onToggleTheme}
              aria-label={`Switch to ${adminTheme === 'light' ? 'dark' : 'light'} mode`}
              aria-pressed={adminTheme === 'dark'}
              style={{
                display: 'inline-flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
                width: '44px',
                height: '44px',
                borderRadius: '50%',
                borderWidth: '1px',
                borderStyle: 'solid',
                borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-strong)',
                backgroundColor: 'var(--admin-surface-subtle)',
                color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
                cursor: 'pointer',
                transition: 'background-color 200ms ease, transform 200ms ease',
              }}
              onMouseEnter={(e) => {
                e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = 'var(--admin-surface)';
              }}
              onMouseLeave={(e) => {
                e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = 'var(--admin-surface-subtle)';
              }}
              onMouseDown={(e) => {
                e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'scale(0.94)';
              }}
              onMouseUp={(e) => {
                e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'scale(1)';
              }}
            >
              <span
                style={{
                  display: 'inline-flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  justifyContent: 'center',
                  transition: 'transform 400ms cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.56, 0.64, 1)',
                  transform: adminTheme === 'light' ? 'rotate(0deg)' : 'rotate(180deg)',
                }}
              >
                {adminTheme === 'light' ? (
                  <FiSun size={18} aria-hidden="true" />
                ) : (
                  <FiMoon size={18} aria-hidden="true" />
                )}
              </span>
            </button>
          )}
          <AccentButton
            variant="dark"
            shape="circle"
            ariaLabel="Log out"
            onClick={onLogout}
          >
            <FiLogOut size={18} aria-hidden="true" />
          </AccentButton>
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/components/NewRequestForm.tsx =====
import { useState } from 'react';
import { FiX } from 'react-icons/fi';
import Modal from '../../../components/Modal';
import AccentButton from './AccentButton';
import AdminDropdown from './AdminDropdown';
import type {
  StoreRequestCategory,
  StoreRequestPriority,
  Storefront,
} from '../settings/categories/types';

type NewRequestFormProps = {
  isOpen: boolean;
  storefront: Storefront;
  storeLabel: string;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSubmit: (data: {
    storefront: Storefront;
    title: string;
    category: StoreRequestCategory;
    description: string;
    priority: StoreRequestPriority;
  }) => boolean;
};

const INPUT_STYLE = {
  width: '100%',
  paddingInline: 'var(--admin-space-sm)',
  paddingBlock: 'var(--admin-space-xs)',
  borderRadius: 'var(--ds-radius-lg)',
  borderWidth: '1px',
  borderStyle: 'solid',
  borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-strong)',
  backgroundColor: 'var(--admin-surface-subtle)',
  color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
  fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-body)',
  outline: 'none',
} as const;

const LABEL_STYLE = {
  fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-micro)',
  fontWeight: 'var(--admin-weight-medium)',
  color: 'var(--admin-text-secondary)',
  display: 'block',
  marginBottom: 'var(--admin-space-xxs)',
} as const;

const CATEGORY_OPTIONS: { value: StoreRequestCategory; label: string }[] = [
  { value: 'bug', label: 'Bug report' },
  { value: 'feature', label: 'Feature request' },
  { value: 'content', label: 'Content change' },
  { value: 'other', label: 'Other' },
];

const PRIORITY_OPTIONS: { value: StoreRequestPriority; label: string }[] = [
  { value: 'low', label: 'Low' },
  { value: 'medium', label: 'Medium' },
  { value: 'high', label: 'High' },
  { value: 'urgent', label: 'Urgent' },
];

export default function NewRequestForm({
  isOpen,
  storefront,
  storeLabel,
  onClose,
  onSubmit,
}: NewRequestFormProps) {
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [category, setCategory] = useState<StoreRequestCategory>('other');
  const [description, setDescription] = useState('');
  const [priority, setPriority] = useState<StoreRequestPriority>('medium');

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const reset = () => {
    setTitle('');
    setCategory('other');
    setDescription('');
    setPriority('medium');
  };

  const handleSubmit = () => {
    const success = onSubmit({
      storefront,
      title,
      category,
      description,
      priority,
    });
    if (success) {
      reset();
      onClose();
    }
  };

  return (
    <Modal isOpen onClose={onClose} maxWidth="max-w-lg">
      <div style={{ padding: 'var(--admin-space-lg)' }}>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between" style={{ marginBottom: 'var(--admin-space-md)' }}>
          <div className="flex flex-col" style={{ gap: 'var(--admin-space-xxs)' }}>
            <h3 style={{ fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-heading-3)', fontWeight: 'var(--admin-weight-semibold)', color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)', margin: 0 }}>
              New request
            </h3>
            <p style={{ fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-micro)', color: 'var(--admin-text-tertiary)', margin: 0 }}>
              For {storeLabel} — the technical team will review and respond.
            </p>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Close"
            style={{ width: '28px', height: '28px', borderRadius: '50%', border: 'none', background: 'transparent', cursor: 'pointer', color: 'var(--admin-text-tertiary)', display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}
          >
            <FiX size={16} aria-hidden="true" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Title */}
        <div style={{ marginBottom: 'var(--admin-space-md)' }}>
          <label style={LABEL_STYLE}>Title</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={title}
            onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Short summary of your request"
            maxLength={100}
            style={INPUT_STYLE}
          />
        </div>

        {/* Category + Priority row */}
        <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 'var(--admin-space-sm)', marginBottom: 'var(--admin-space-md)' }}>
          <div>
            <label style={LABEL_STYLE}>Category</label>
            <AdminDropdown
              value={category}
              onChange={(v) => setCategory(v as StoreRequestCategory)}
              options={CATEGORY_OPTIONS}
              align="left"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label style={LABEL_STYLE}>Priority</label>
            <AdminDropdown
              value={priority}
              onChange={(v) => setPriority(v as StoreRequestPriority)}
              options={PRIORITY_OPTIONS}
              align="left"
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Description */}
        <div style={{ marginBottom: 'var(--admin-space-md)' }}>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between" style={{ marginBottom: 'var(--admin-space-xxs)' }}>
            <label style={{ ...LABEL_STYLE, marginBottom: 0 }}>Description</label>
            <span style={{ fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-micro)', color: description.length > 450 ? 'var(--admin-status-amber)' : 'var(--admin-text-tertiary)', fontVariantNumeric: 'tabular-nums' }}>
              {description.length}/500
            </span>
          </div>
          <textarea
            value={description}
            onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)}
            maxLength={500}
            rows={4}
            placeholder="Describe what you need in as much detail as possible."
            style={{ ...INPUT_STYLE, resize: 'vertical', minHeight: '100px', fontFamily: 'inherit' }}
          />
        </div>

        {/* Actions */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end', gap: 'var(--admin-space-xs)' }}>
          <AccentButton variant="neutral" shape="pill" onClick={onClose} ariaLabel="Cancel">
            Cancel
          </AccentButton>
          <AccentButton variant="dark" shape="pill" onClick={handleSubmit} ariaLabel="Submit request">
            Submit request
          </AccentButton>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/components/OrderOverviewCard.tsx =====
import { FiActivity, FiArrowUpRight, FiCheckCircle, FiShoppingBag, FiTruck, FiXCircle } from 'react-icons/fi';

type OrderOverviewProps = {
  orderStats: {
    total: number;
    pending: number;
    confirmed: number;
    delivered: number;
    cancelled: number;
  };
  onViewOrders?: () => void;
};

export default function OrderOverviewCard({ orderStats, onViewOrders }: OrderOverviewProps) {
  const orderStatuses = [
    { label: 'Pending', value: orderStats.pending, icon: FiActivity, color: 'var(--ds-color-status-warning)', background: 'var(--ds-color-bg-status-warning)' },
    { label: 'Confirmed', value: orderStats.confirmed, icon: FiCheckCircle, color: 'var(--ds-color-status-success)', background: 'var(--ds-color-bg-status-success)' },
    { label: 'Delivered', value: orderStats.delivered, icon: FiTruck, color: 'var(--ds-color-status-success)', background: 'var(--ds-color-bg-status-success)' },
    { label: 'Cancelled', value: orderStats.cancelled, icon: FiXCircle, color: 'var(--ds-color-status-danger)', background: 'var(--ds-color-bg-status-danger)' },
  ];

  const totalOrders = Math.max(orderStats.total, 0);

  return (
    <section className="flex h-full min-w-0 flex-col gap-ds-4 rounded-ds-xl border border-design-border-secondary bg-design-surface p-ds-4 shadow-ds-xs sm:p-ds-5">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-ds-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-ds-2">
          <span className="flex size-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-ds-md bg-design-surface-secondary text-design-icon-primary">
            <FiShoppingBag size={18} aria-hidden="true" />
          </span>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-0">
            <h2 className="admin-font-heading text-ds-heading-3 text-design-text-primary">
              Order overview
            </h2>
            <p className="admin-font-body text-ds-caption text-design-text-secondary">
              Order status breakdown
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onViewOrders}
          disabled={!onViewOrders}
          className="inline-flex min-h-9 items-center gap-ds-1 rounded-ds-md px-ds-2 text-ds-caption font-medium text-design-text-accent transition-colors hover:bg-design-surface-secondary disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-design-focus-ring motion-reduce:transition-none"
        >
          View orders
          <FiArrowUpRight size={15} aria-hidden="true" />
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-end gap-ds-3 mt-auto">
        <p className="admin-font-heading text-ds-display tabular-nums text-design-text-primary">
          {orderStats.total}
        </p>
        <p className="admin-font-body pb-ds-2 text-ds-caption text-design-text-secondary">
          Total orders received
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="flex h-2 w-full overflow-hidden rounded-ds-full bg-design-surface-secondary">
        {orderStatuses.map((status) => {
          const percentage = totalOrders > 0 ? (status.value / totalOrders) * 100 : 0;
          return (
            <span
              key={status.label}
              className="h-full first:rounded-l-ds-full last:rounded-r-ds-full"
              style={{ width: `${percentage}%`, backgroundColor: status.color }}
              title={`${status.label}: ${status.value}`}
            />
          );
        })}
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-ds-4 gap-y-ds-3 border-t border-design-border-secondary pt-ds-4">
        {orderStatuses.map((status) => {
          const StatusIcon = status.icon;
          return (
            <div key={status.label} className="flex items-center gap-ds-2">
              <span
                className="flex size-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-ds-md"
                style={{ color: status.color, backgroundColor: status.background }}
              >
                <StatusIcon size={15} aria-hidden="true" />
              </span>
              <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-col gap-0">
                <span className="admin-font-body text-ds-caption text-design-text-secondary">
                  {status.label}
                </span>
                <span className="admin-font-heading text-ds-heading-3 tabular-nums text-design-text-primary">
                  {status.value}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
