===== src/features/admin/components/OverviewHeader.tsx =====
import { FiPlus } from 'react-icons/fi';
import { getCategoryTheme } from '../../../utils/categoryTheme';
import AccentButton from './AccentButton';
import TimeRangePicker from './TimeRangePicker';
import type { TimeRange } from '../types/admin.types';

type OverviewHeaderProps = {
  email: string;
  onAddProduct: () => void;
  searchQuery: string;
  onSearchChange: (value: string) => void;
  onSearchFocus: () => void;
  onFilterSelect?: (filterId: string) => void;
  activeFilter?: string;
  timeRange?: TimeRange;
  onTimeRangeChange?: (value: TimeRange) => void;
};

export default function OverviewHeader({
  email,
  onAddProduct,
  onFilterSelect,
  activeFilter = 'all',
  timeRange = 'this-year',
  onTimeRangeChange,
}: OverviewHeaderProps) {
  const rawName = email.split('@')[0] || email;
  const name = rawName.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + rawName.slice(1);

  const hour = new Date().getHours();
  const greeting =
    hour < 12 ? 'Good morning' : hour < 17 ? 'Good afternoon' : 'Good evening';

  const storeFilters: { id: string; label: string; category: string | null }[] = [
    { id: 'yotaabo', label: 'Yotaabo', category: 'His' },
    { id: 'poshies', label: 'Poshies', category: 'Hers' },
    { id: 'all', label: 'All', category: null },
  ];

  return (
    <header
      className="flex w-full flex-col"
      style={{ gap: 'var(--admin-space-xl)' }}
    >
      <h1
        style={{
          fontSize: 'var(--admin-greeting-size)',
          lineHeight: 'var(--admin-greeting-line)',
          fontWeight: 'var(--admin-greeting-weight)',
          color: 'var(--admin-border-strong)',
          margin: 0,
          whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
        }}
      >
        {greeting},{' '}
        <span
          style={{
            color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
            overflow: 'hidden',
            textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
            display: 'inline-block',
            maxWidth: '100%',
            verticalAlign: 'bottom',
          }}
        >
          {name}
        </span>
      </h1>

      <div
        className="admin-filter-row flex w-full items-center"
        style={{
          gap: 'var(--admin-space-xs)',
          justifyContent: 'flex-end',
          flexWrap: 'wrap',
        }}
      >
        {onTimeRangeChange && (
          <TimeRangePicker value={timeRange} onChange={onTimeRangeChange} />
        )}
        <div className="admin-segmented admin-filter-segmented">
          {storeFilters.map((filter, index) => {
            const isActive = activeFilter === filter.id;
            const isAll = filter.category === null;
            const theme = filter.category === null
              ? null
              : getCategoryTheme(filter.category);
            const isFirst = index === 0;
            const isLast = index === storeFilters.length - 1;
            const edgeClass = isFirst
              ? 'admin-segmented-left'
              : isLast
                ? 'admin-segmented-right'
                : '';
            return (
              <AccentButton
                key={filter.id}
                variant={isActive ? 'active' : 'neutral'}
                shape="pill"
                onClick={() => onFilterSelect?.(filter.id)}
                ariaLabel={`Filter: ${filter.label}`}
                ariaCurrent={isActive ? 'true' : undefined}
                storeColor={theme?.hexColor}
                activeColor={theme?.hexColor ?? 'var(--admin-accent)'}
                className={edgeClass}
                style={{ borderRadius: 0 }}
              >
                {filter.label}
              </AccentButton>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        <AccentButton
          variant="dark"
          shape="circle"
          ariaLabel="Add product"
          onClick={onAddProduct}
        >
          <FiPlus size={18} aria-hidden="true" />
        </AccentButton>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/components/QuickActionsCard.tsx =====
import { FiArrowUpRight, FiPlus, FiSearch, FiShoppingBag } from 'react-icons/fi';

type QuickActionsProps = {
  searchQuery: string;
  onSearchChange: (value: string) => void;
  onSearchFocus: () => void;
  onAddProduct: () => void;
  onViewOrders?: () => void;
};

export default function QuickActionsCard({
  searchQuery,
  onSearchChange,
  onSearchFocus,
  onAddProduct,
  onViewOrders,
}: QuickActionsProps) {
  return (
    <section className="flex h-full flex-col gap-ds-3 rounded-ds-xl border border-design-border-secondary bg-design-surface p-ds-4 shadow-ds-xs sm:p-ds-5">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-ds-2 mb-ds-2">
        <span className="flex size-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-ds-md bg-design-surface-secondary text-design-icon-primary">

          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={onAddProduct}
        className="flex min-h-12 items-center justify-between gap-ds-3 rounded-ds-md bg-design-fill-primary px-ds-3 text-left text-white transition-colors hover:bg-design-fill-primary-hover focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-design-focus-ring motion-reduce:transition-none mt-auto"

        <span className="flex items-center gap-ds-2">
          <FiPlus size={17} aria-hidden="true" />

        <input
          type="search"

          value={searchQuery}
          onChange={(event) => onSearchChange(event.target.value)}
          placeholder="Search products..."
          autoComplete="off"
          className="min-w-0 flex-1 border-0 bg-transparent py-ds-2 text-ds-body-sm text-design-text-primary outline-none placeholder:text-design-text-tertiary focus:border-0 focus:outline-none focus:ring-0"
        />
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onSearchFocus}
          aria-label="Open product search"
          className="flex size-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-ds-sm text-design-icon-secondary transition-colors hover:bg-design-surface-secondary-hover hover:text-design-icon-primary focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-design-focus-ring"
        >
          <FiArrowUpRight size={16} aria-hidden="true" />
        </button>
      </div>

      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={onViewOrders}
        disabled={!onViewOrders}
        className="flex min-h-11 items-center justify-between gap-ds-3 rounded-ds-md border border-design-border-secondary px-ds-3 text-left text-design-text-primary transition-colors hover:bg-design-surface-secondary disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-design-focus-ring motion-reduce:transition-none"
      >
        <span className="flex items-center gap-ds-2">
          <FiShoppingBag size={17} className="text-design-icon-secondary" aria-hidden="true" />
          <span className="text-ds-label font-medium">Manage orders</span>
        </span>
        <FiArrowUpRight size={16} className="text-design-icon-secondary" aria-hidden="true" />
      </button>
    </section>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/components/RecentActivityList.tsx =====
import { FiActivity, FiArrowUpRight, FiList, FiPackage, FiShoppingBag } from 'react-icons/fi';
import type { AdminActivity } from '../hooks/useAdminActivity';

type RecentActivityProps = {
  activities: AdminActivity[];
  isLoading: boolean;
  onViewAll: () => void;
};

export default function RecentActivityList({ activities, isLoading, onViewAll }: RecentActivityProps) {
  
  const getActivityDescription = (activity: AdminActivity) => {
    const metadata = activity.metadata ?? {};
    const productName = typeof metadata.product_name === 'string' ? metadata.product_name.trim() : '';
    const rawOrderNumber = typeof metadata.order_number === 'string' ? metadata.order_number.trim() : '';
    const orderNumber = rawOrderNumber.replace(/^#+/, '');

    if (activity.event_type === 'product.created' && productName) return `${productName} added`;
    if (activity.event_type === 'order.received' && orderNumber) return `Order #${orderNumber} received`;
    return activity.description.replace(/^(Order)\s*#+/i, '$1 #');
  };

  return (
    <section className="flex h-full min-w-0 flex-col rounded-ds-xl border border-design-border-secondary bg-design-surface p-ds-4 shadow-ds-xs sm:p-ds-5">
      <div className="mb-ds-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-ds-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-ds-2">
          <span className="flex size-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-ds-md bg-design-surface-secondary text-design-icon-primary">
            <FiActivity size={18} aria-hidden="true" />
          </span>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-0">
            <h2 className="admin-font-heading text-ds-heading-3 text-design-text-primary">
              Recent activity
            </h2>
            <p className="admin-font-body text-ds-caption text-design-text-secondary">
              Latest store updates
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onViewAll}
          className="inline-flex min-h-9 shrink-0 items-center gap-ds-1 rounded-ds-md px-ds-2 text-ds-caption font-medium text-design-text-accent transition-colors hover:bg-design-surface-secondary focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-design-focus-ring motion-reduce:transition-none"
        >
          View all
          <FiArrowUpRight size={15} aria-hidden="true" />
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col" aria-busy={isLoading && activities.length === 0}>
        {isLoading && activities.length === 0 ? (
          <div role="status" className="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center py-ds-8 text-ds-body-sm text-design-text-secondary">
            Loading recent activity...
          </div>
        ) : activities.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-ds-2 rounded-ds-md bg-design-surface-secondary px-ds-4 py-ds-6 text-center">
            <FiList size={22} className="text-design-icon-secondary" aria-hidden="true" />
            <p className="admin-font-body text-ds-body-sm text-design-text-primary">No activity to show yet</p>
            <p className="admin-font-body text-ds-caption text-design-text-secondary">Product and order updates will appear here.</p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <ul className="flex flex-col">
            {activities.slice(0, 4).map((activity, index) => (
              <li
                key={activity.id}
                className={`flex items-center gap-ds-3 py-ds-3 ${index > 0 ? 'border-t border-design-border-secondary' : ''}`}
              >
                <span className="flex size-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-ds-md bg-design-surface-secondary text-design-icon-secondary">
                  {activity.entity_type === 'product' ? <FiPackage size={16} /> : <FiShoppingBag size={16} />}
                </span>

                <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-0">
                  <p className="admin-font-body text-ds-body-sm text-design-text-primary truncate">
                    {getActivityDescription(activity)}
                  </p>
                  <p className="admin-font-body text-ds-caption text-design-text-secondary">
                    {activity.entity_type === 'product' ? 'Product activity' : 'Order activity'}
                  </p>
                </div>

                <time
                  dateTime={activity.created_at}
                  className="shrink-0 text-right text-ds-caption tabular-nums text-design-text-tertiary"
                >
                  {new Date(activity.created_at).toLocaleString('en-ZA', {
                    day: '2-digit', month: 'short', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit',
                  })}
                </time>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        )}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/components/RequestPanel.tsx =====
import { FiAlertCircle, FiCheck, FiClock, FiPlus } from 'react-icons/fi';
import AccentButton from './AccentButton';
import type { StoreRequest, StoreRequestStatus } from '../settings/categories/types';

type RequestPanelProps = {
  requests: StoreRequest[];
  onNewRequest: () => void;
  storeLabel: string;
};

function formatRelativeDate(dateStr: string): string {
  const date = new Date(dateStr);
  if (isNaN(date.getTime())) return 'recently';
  const now = new Date();
  const diffMs = now.getTime() - date.getTime();
  const diffDays = Math.floor(diffMs / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
  const diffHours = Math.floor(diffMs / (1000 * 60 * 60));
  if (diffHours < 1) return 'just now';
  if (diffHours < 24) return `${diffHours} hour${diffHours === 1 ? '' : 's'} ago`;
  if (diffDays < 7) return `${diffDays} day${diffDays === 1 ? '' : 's'} ago`;
  if (diffDays < 30) return `${Math.floor(diffDays / 7)} week${Math.floor(diffDays / 7) === 1 ? '' : 's'} ago`;
  return date.toLocaleDateString('en-ZA', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' });
}

const STATUS_META: Record<StoreRequestStatus, { label: string; color: string; bg: string }> = {
  open: {
    label: 'Open',
    color: 'var(--admin-status-amber)',
    bg: 'color-mix(in srgb, var(--admin-status-amber) 15%, transparent)',
  },
  in_progress: {
    label: 'In progress',
    color: 'var(--admin-accent)',
    bg: 'color-mix(in srgb, var(--admin-accent) 15%, transparent)',
  },
  resolved: {
    label: 'Resolved',
    color: 'var(--admin-status-green)',
    bg: 'color-mix(in srgb, var(--admin-status-green) 15%, transparent)',
  },
};

const CATEGORY_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {

  content: 'Content change',
  other: 'Other',
};

  low: 'Low',
  medium: 'Medium',
  high: 'High',
  urgent: 'Urgent',
};

export default function RequestPanel({
  requests,

}: RequestPanelProps) {

          >

          onClick={onNewRequest}

===== src/features/admin/components/RevenueChart.tsx =====
import {
  ResponsiveContainer,
  LineChart,
  Line,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
  CartesianGrid,
  Tooltip,
  Legend,
} from 'recharts';
import { getCategoryTheme } from '../../../utils/categoryTheme';

const MONTHS = [
  'Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun',
  'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec',
];

export type RevenueSeries = {
  key: string;
  label: string;
  color: string;
  data: (number | null)[];
  dashed?: boolean;
};

type RevenueChartProps = {
  series: RevenueSeries[];
  activeFilter?: string;
};

const formatRand = (value: number): string => {
  if (Math.abs(value) >= 1000) {
    return `R ${(value / 1000).toFixed(value % 1000 === 0 ? 0 : 1)}k`;
  }
  return `R ${Math.round(value).toLocaleString('en-ZA')}`;
};

export default function RevenueChart({ series, activeFilter = 'all' }: RevenueChartProps) {
  const accentColor =
    activeFilter === 'yotaabo'
      ? getCategoryTheme('His').hexColor
      : activeFilter === 'poshies'
        ? getCategoryTheme('Hers').hexColor
        : 'var(--admin-accent)';

  const currentMonthIndex = new Date().getMonth();

  // Build chart data: one row per month, each series contributes its value
  const chartData = MONTHS.map((month, idx) => {
    const row: Record<string, string | number | null> = { month };
    if (idx > currentMonthIndex) {
      for (const s of series) {
        row[s.key] = null;
      }
    } else {
      for (const s of series) {
        row[s.key] = s.data[idx] ?? 0;
      }
    }
    return row;
  });

  return (
    <div
      className="admin-revenue-chart w-full"
      style={{
        height: 'var(--admin-chart-height, 220px)',
        padding: '0 var(--admin-space-md)',
      }}
    >
      <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
        <LineChart data={chartData} margin={{ top: 8, right: 8, left: 0, bottom: 0 }}>
          <CartesianGrid
            strokeDasharray="4 4"
            stroke="color-mix(in srgb, var(--admin-border-strong) 40%, transparent)"
            vertical={false}
          />
          <XAxis
            dataKey="month"
            tickLine={false}
            axisLine={false}
            tick={{ fill: 'var(--admin-text-secondary)', fontSize: 10 }}
            style={{ outline: 'none' }}
          />
          <YAxis
            tickLine={false}
            axisLine={false}
            width={48}
            allowDecimals={false}
            tickFormatter={formatRand}
            tick={{ fill: 'var(--admin-text-secondary)', fontSize: 10 }}
            style={{ outline: 'none' }}
          />
          <Tooltip
            contentStyle={{
              backgroundColor: 'var(--admin-surface)',
              border: '1px solid var(--admin-border-strong)',
              borderRadius: 'var(--ds-radius-md)',
              fontSize: 12,
              color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
            }}
            labelStyle={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)' }}
            formatter={(value) => {
              if (value === null || value === undefined) return '';
              const num = typeof value === 'number' ? value : Number(value);
              if (Number.isNaN(num)) return '';
              return formatRand(num);
            }}
          />
          <Legend
            verticalAlign="top"
            align="right"
            iconType="circle"
            iconSize={8}
            wrapperStyle={{ fontSize: 10, color: 'var(--admin-text-secondary)' }}
          />
          {series.map((s) => (
            <Line
              key={s.key}
              type="monotone"
              dataKey={s.key}
              name={s.label}
              stroke={s.color}
              strokeWidth={s.dashed ? 1.5 : 2}
              strokeDasharray={s.dashed ? '4 4' : undefined}
              dot={false}
              activeDot={s.dashed ? false : { r: 4 }}
              connectNulls={false}
              pathLength={s.dashed ? undefined : 1}
              style={
                s.dashed
                  ? undefined
                  : {
                      animation: 'admin-trend-draw 1200ms ease-out forwards',
                      strokeDasharray: 1,
                    }
              }
            />
          ))}
        </LineChart>
      </ResponsiveContainer>
    </div>
  );
}
