===== src/features/admin/components/TimeRangePicker.tsx =====
import type { TimeRange } from '../types/admin.types';
import AccentButton from './AccentButton';

type TimeRangePickerProps = {
  value: TimeRange;
  onChange: (value: TimeRange) => void;
};

const OPTIONS: { id: TimeRange; label: string }[] = [
  { id: 'this-month', label: 'This month' },
  { id: 'last-month', label: 'Last month' },
  { id: 'last-quarter', label: 'Last quarter' },
  { id: 'ytd', label: 'YTD' },
  { id: 'this-year', label: 'This year' },
  { id: 'last-year', label: 'Last year' },
  { id: 'all', label: 'All' },
];

export default function TimeRangePicker({ value, onChange }: TimeRangePickerProps) {
  return (
    <div className="admin-filter-row flex items-center" style={{ gap: 'var(--admin-space-xs)', flexWrap: 'nowrap', overflowX: 'auto' }}>
      <div className="admin-segmented admin-filter-segmented" style={{ flexWrap: 'nowrap' }}>
        {OPTIONS.map((option, index) => {
          const isActive = value === option.id;
          const isFirst = index === 0;
          const isLast = index === OPTIONS.length - 1;
          const edgeClass = isFirst
            ? 'admin-segmented-left'
            : isLast
              ? 'admin-segmented-right'
              : '';
          return (
            <AccentButton
              key={option.id}
              variant={isActive ? 'active' : 'neutral'}
              shape="pill"
              onClick={() => onChange(option.id)}
              ariaLabel={`Time range: ${option.label}`}
              ariaCurrent={isActive ? 'true' : undefined}
              activeColor="var(--admin-text-primary)"
              className={edgeClass}
              style={{ borderRadius: 0, fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-caption)', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}
            >
              {option.label}
            </AccentButton>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/components/inventory/AdminInventory.tsx =====
import {
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useRef,
  useState,
  type FormEvent,
} from 'react';
import { ADMIN_STORAGE_KEYS } from '../../constants/admin.constants';
import {
  readAdminSnapshot,
  writeAdminSnapshot,
} from '../../utils/adminSnapshot';
import {
  FiActivity,
  FiArrowDownCircle,
  FiArrowUpCircle,
  FiFileText,
  FiPackage,
  FiPlus,
  FiSearch,
  FiX,
  FiShoppingCart,
  FiTrendingDown,
  FiTrendingUp,
} from 'react-icons/fi';

type InventorySection = 'overview' | 'stock' | 'sales' | 'receipts';
type StockAction = 'receive' | 'adjust' | null;
type StorefrontFilter = 'all' | 'yotaabo' | 'poshies';

type InventoryItem = {
  product_id: string;
  product_name: string;
  sku: string | null;
  price: number | string | null;
  supplier_cost: number | string | null;
  image_url: string | null;
  is_active: boolean;
  on_hand_quantity: number;
  reserved_quantity: number;
  available_quantity: number;
  is_initialized: boolean;
  updated_at: string | null;
};

type InventoryMovement = {
  id: string;
  product_id: string;
  product_name: string;
  product_sku: string | null;
  movement_type: string;
  quantity_delta: number;
  reserved_delta: number;
  source_type: string;
  reason: string | null;
  actor_email: string | null;
  created_at: string;
};

type InventoryAggregates = {
  received: number | string;
  adjusted: number | string;
};

type InventoryResponse = {
  inventory: InventoryItem[];
  movements: InventoryMovement[];
  aggregates: InventoryAggregates;
};

function isNumericValue(value: unknown): boolean {
  return (
    (typeof value === 'number' || typeof value === 'string') &&
    String(value).trim() !== '' &&
    Number.isFinite(Number(value))
  );
}

function isNullableString(value: unknown): boolean {
  return value === null || typeof value === 'string';
}

function isInventoryResponse(value: unknown): value is InventoryResponse {
  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object') return false;

  const data = value as Record<string, unknown>;

  if (
    !Array.isArray(data.inventory) ||
    !Array.isArray(data.movements) ||
    !data.aggregates ||
    typeof data.aggregates !== 'object'
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  const inventoryValid = data.inventory.every((entry: unknown) => {
    if (!entry || typeof entry !== 'object') return false;

    const item = entry as Record<string, unknown>;

    return (
      typeof item.product_id === 'string' &&
      typeof item.product_name === 'string' &&
      isNullableString(item.sku) &&
      (item.price === null || isNumericValue(item.price)) &&
      (item.supplier_cost === null || isNumericValue(item.supplier_cost)) &&
      isNullableString(item.image_url) &&
      typeof item.is_active === 'boolean' &&
      isNumericValue(item.on_hand_quantity) &&
      isNumericValue(item.reserved_quantity) &&
      isNumericValue(item.available_quantity) &&
      typeof item.is_initialized === 'boolean' &&
      isNullableString(item.updated_at)
    );
  });

  const movementsValid = data.movements.every((entry: unknown) => {
    if (!entry || typeof entry !== 'object') return false;

    const item = entry as Record<string, unknown>;

    return (
      typeof item.id === 'string' &&
      typeof item.product_id === 'string' &&
      typeof item.product_name === 'string' &&
      isNullableString(item.product_sku) &&
      typeof item.movement_type === 'string' &&
      isNumericValue(item.quantity_delta) &&
      isNumericValue(item.reserved_delta) &&
      typeof item.source_type === 'string' &&
      isNullableString(item.reason) &&
      isNullableString(item.actor_email) &&
      typeof item.created_at === 'string'
    );
  });

  const aggregates = data.aggregates as Record<string, unknown>;

  return (
    inventoryValid &&
    movementsValid &&
    isNumericValue(aggregates.received) &&
    isNumericValue(aggregates.adjusted)
  );
}

const sections: {
  id: InventorySection;
  label: string;
  description: string;
  Icon: typeof FiPackage;
}[] = [
  {
    id: 'overview',
    label: 'Overview',
    description: 'Inventory performance and stock health',
    Icon: FiActivity,
  },
  {
    id: 'stock',
    label: 'Stock management',
    description: 'Balances, receiving and adjustments',
    Icon: FiPackage,
  },
  {
    id: 'sales',
    label: 'Direct sales',
    description: 'Record sales outside the storefront',
    Icon: FiShoppingCart,
  },
  {
    id: 'receipts',
    label: 'Receipts',
    description: 'Review and manage customer receipts',
    Icon: FiFileText,
  },
];

function MetricCard({
  label,
  value,
  description,
  Icon,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  description: string;
  Icon: typeof FiPackage;
}) {
  return (
    <article
      className="min-w-0 rounded-xl border p-4"
      style={{
        background: 'var(--admin-surface)',
        borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-subtle)',
      }}
    >
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <span
          className="text-xs font-medium"
          style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-secondary)' }}
        >
          {label}
        </span>
        <Icon size={18} style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-secondary)' }} />
      </div>
      <p
        className="mt-3 text-2xl font-semibold"
        style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)' }}
      >
        {value}
      </p>
      <p
        className="mt-1 text-xs"
        style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-muted)' }}
      >
        {description}
      </p>
    </article>
  );
}

export default function AdminInventory({
  storefrontFilter,
  accessToken,
  userId,
}: {
  storefrontFilter: StorefrontFilter;
  accessToken: string;
  userId: string;
}) {
  const accessTokenRef = useRef(accessToken);
  accessTokenRef.current = accessToken;

  const [section, setSection] = useState<InventorySection>('overview');
  const [stockSearch, setStockSearch] = useState('');
  const [stockAction, setStockAction] = useState<StockAction>(null);
  const [inventory, setInventory] = useState<InventoryItem[]>([]);
  const [movements, setMovements] = useState<InventoryMovement[]>([]);
  const [movementSearch, setMovementSearch] = useState('');
  const [movementTypeFilter, setMovementTypeFilter] = useState('all');
  const [aggregates, setAggregates] = useState<InventoryAggregates>({
    received: 0,
    adjusted: 0,
  });
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [loadError, setLoadError] = useState('');
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [submitError, setSubmitError] = useState('');
  const [selectedProductId, setSelectedProductId] = useState('');
  const [quantity, setQuantity] = useState('1');
  const [reason, setReason] = useState('');
  const [adjustmentDirection, setAdjustmentDirection] =
    useState<'increase' | 'decrease'>('increase');
  const activeSection = sections.find((item) => item.id === section)!;

  const selectedProduct = inventory.find(
    (item) => item.product_id === selectedProductId
  );

  function applyInventoryResponse(
    data: InventoryResponse,
    persistSnapshot = true
  ) {
    setInventory(data.inventory);
    setMovements(data.movements);
    setAggregates({
      received: Number(data.aggregates.received) || 0,
      adjusted: Number(data.aggregates.adjusted) || 0,
    });

    if (persistSnapshot && userId && storefrontFilter !== 'all') {
      writeAdminSnapshot(
        `${ADMIN_STORAGE_KEYS.inventoryCache}:${storefrontFilter}`,
        userId,
        data
      );
    }
  }

  async function handleStockSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();

    if (
      !stockAction ||
      storefrontFilter === 'all' ||
      !accessToken ||
      !selectedProduct
    ) {
      setSubmitError('Select a storefront and a valid product.');
      return;
    }

    const parsedQuantity = Number(quantity);

    if (
      !Number.isInteger(parsedQuantity) ||
      parsedQuantity < 1 ||
      parsedQuantity > 1000000
    ) {
      setSubmitError('Quantity must be a whole number between 1 and 1,000,000.');
      return;
    }

    const action =
      stockAction === 'receive'
        ? selectedProduct.is_initialized
          ? 'replenishment'
          : 'opening-stock'
        : adjustmentDirection === 'increase'
          ? 'adjustment-increase'
          : 'adjustment-decrease';

    const requiresReason = action !== 'opening-stock';

    if (requiresReason && !reason.trim()) {
      setSubmitError('A reason is required for this stock movement.');
      return;
    }

    setIsSubmitting(true);
    setSubmitError('');

    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/inventory', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}`,
          Accept: 'application/json',
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          storefront: storefrontFilter,
          product_id: selectedProduct.product_id,
          action,
          quantity: parsedQuantity,
          reason: reason.trim(),
        }),
      });

      const data = await response.json();

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(data?.error || 'Unable to save the stock movement.');
      }

      // The stock movement is committed. Close the form before refreshing
      // so a refresh failure cannot lead to the same movement being submitted again.
      setStockAction(null);
      setSelectedProductId('');
      setQuantity('1');
      setReason('');
      setAdjustmentDirection('increase');

      try {
        const refreshResponse = await fetch(
          `/api/inventory?storefront=${encodeURIComponent(storefrontFilter)}`,
          {
            method: 'GET',
            headers: {
              Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}`,
              Accept: 'application/json',
            },
          }
        );

        const refreshData = await refreshResponse.json();

        if (!refreshResponse.ok) {
          throw new Error(
            refreshData?.error || 'The inventory refresh failed.'
          );
        }

        if (!isInventoryResponse(refreshData)) {
          throw new Error('The inventory response has an invalid format.');
        }

        applyInventoryResponse(refreshData);
        setLoadError('');
      } catch (refreshError) {
        setLoadError(
          `Stock was saved, but inventory could not be refreshed. Reload the page. ${
            refreshError instanceof Error
              ? refreshError.message
              : ''
          }`.trim()
        );
      }
    } catch (error) {
      setSubmitError(
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : 'Unable to save the stock movement.'
      );
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (storefrontFilter === 'all' || !userId) {
      setInventory([]);
      setMovements([]);
      setAggregates({ received: 0, adjusted: 0 });
      setLoadError('');
      setIsLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    const storageKey =
      `${ADMIN_STORAGE_KEYS.inventoryCache}:${storefrontFilter}`;

    const cachedInventory = readAdminSnapshot(
      storageKey,
      userId,
      isInventoryResponse
    );

    if (cachedInventory) {
      applyInventoryResponse(cachedInventory, false);
      setLoadError('');
      setIsLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    const token = accessTokenRef.current;

    if (!token) {
      setIsLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    const controller = new AbortController();
    setIsLoading(true);
    setLoadError('');

    async function loadInventory() {
      try {
        const response = await fetch(
          `/api/inventory?storefront=${encodeURIComponent(storefrontFilter)}`,
          {
            method: 'GET',
            headers: {
              Authorization: `Bearer ${accessTokenRef.current}`,
              Accept: 'application/json',
            },
            signal: controller.signal,
          }
        );

        const data = await response.json();

        if (!response.ok) {
          throw new Error(data?.error || 'Unable to load inventory.');
        }

        if (!isInventoryResponse(data)) {
          throw new Error('The inventory response has an invalid format.');
        }

        applyInventoryResponse(data);
      } catch (error) {
        if (error instanceof Error && error.name === 'AbortError') return;

        setLoadError(
          error instanceof Error
            ? error.message
            : 'Unable to load inventory.'
        );
      } finally {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) {
          setIsLoading(false);
        }
      }
    }

    void loadInventory();

    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [storefrontFilter, userId, Boolean(accessToken)]);

  const filteredInventory = useMemo(() => {
    const search = stockSearch.trim().toLowerCase();

    if (!search) return inventory;

    return inventory.filter((item) =>
      [item.product_name, item.sku || '']
        .some((value) => value.toLowerCase().includes(search))
    );
  }, [inventory, stockSearch]);

  const filteredMovements = useMemo(() => {
    const search = movementSearch.trim().toLowerCase();

    return movements.filter((movement) => {
      const matchesSearch =
        !search ||
        [movement.product_name, movement.product_sku || ''].some((value) =>
          value.toLowerCase().includes(search)
        );

      const matchesType =
        movementTypeFilter === 'all' ||
        movement.movement_type === movementTypeFilter;

      return matchesSearch && matchesType;
    });
  }, [movements, movementSearch, movementTypeFilter]);

  const stockSummary = useMemo(() => ({
    units: inventory.reduce(
      (total, item) => total + Number(item.on_hand_quantity || 0), 0
    ),
    reserved: inventory.reduce(
      (total, item) => total + Number(item.reserved_quantity || 0), 0
    ),
    available: inventory.reduce(
      (total, item) => total + Number(item.available_quantity || 0), 0
    ),
    outOfStock: inventory.filter(
      (item) =>
        item.is_initialized &&
        Number(item.available_quantity) <= 0
    ).length,
    uninitialized: inventory.filter(
      (item) => !item.is_initialized
    ).length,
    valuation: inventory.reduce((total, item) => {
      if (item.supplier_cost === null || item.supplier_cost === undefined) {
        return total;
      }

      return total +
        Number(item.supplier_cost) * Number(item.on_hand_quantity || 0);
    }, 0),
    unvaluedProducts: inventory.filter(
      (item) =>
        Number(item.on_hand_quantity) > 0 &&
        (item.supplier_cost === null || item.supplier_cost === undefined)
    ).length,
    received: Number(aggregates.received) || 0,
    adjusted: Number(aggregates.adjusted) || 0,
  }), [inventory, aggregates]);

  return (
    <section
      className="flex w-full flex-col"
      style={{ gap: 'var(--admin-space-xl)' }}
    >
      <header className="flex flex-col gap-2">
        <div
          className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider"
          style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-muted)' }}
        >
          Operations
        </div>
        <h1
          className="text-2xl font-semibold"
          style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)' }}
        >
          Inventory
        </h1>
        <p
          className="text-sm"
          style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-secondary)' }}
        >
          Manage stock, direct sales and receipts across your store.
        </p>
      </header>

      <nav
        aria-label="Inventory sections"
        className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-4"
      >
        {sections.map(({ id, label, Icon }) => {
          const selected = section === id;

          return (
            <button
              key={id}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setSection(id)}
              aria-current={selected ? 'page' : undefined}
              className="flex min-h-11 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-lg border px-3 py-3 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors"
              style={{
                background: selected
                  ? 'var(--admin-text-primary)'
                  : 'var(--admin-surface)',
                color: selected
                  ? 'var(--admin-surface)'
                  : 'var(--admin-text-secondary)',
                borderColor: selected
                  ? 'var(--admin-text-primary)'
                  : 'var(--admin-border-subtle)',
              }}
            >
              <Icon size={16} />
              <span>{label}</span>
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </nav>

      <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
        <h2
          className="text-lg font-semibold"
          style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)' }}
        >
          {activeSection.label}
        </h2>
        <p
          className="text-sm"
          style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-secondary)' }}
        >
          {activeSection.description}
        </p>
      </div>

      {loadError && (
        <p
          role="alert"
          className="rounded-lg border p-3 text-sm"
          style={{
            background: 'var(--admin-page)',
            borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-subtle)',
            color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
          }}
        >
          {loadError}
        </p>
      )}

      {section === 'overview' && (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-5">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 xl:grid-cols-4">
            <MetricCard
              label="Stock units"
              value={
                isLoading || loadError
                  ? '—'
                  : stockSummary.units.toLocaleString('en-ZA')
              }
              description="Physical units on hand"
              Icon={FiPackage}
            />
            <MetricCard
              label="Stock valuation"
              value={
                isLoading || loadError
                  ? '—'
                  : new Intl.NumberFormat('en-ZA', {
                      style: 'currency',
                      currency: 'ZAR',
                      maximumFractionDigits: 2,
                    }).format(stockSummary.valuation)
              }
              description={
                stockSummary.unvaluedProducts > 0
                  ? `Partial valuation: ${stockSummary.unvaluedProducts} stocked products missing supplier cost`
                  : 'Based on recorded supplier costs'
              }
              Icon={FiTrendingUp}
            />
            <MetricCard
              label="Out of stock"
              value={
                isLoading || loadError
                  ? '—'
                  : stockSummary.outOfStock.toLocaleString('en-ZA')
              }
              description="Products with no available units"
              Icon={FiTrendingDown}
            />
            <MetricCard
              label="Reserved units"
              value={
                isLoading || loadError
                  ? '—'
                  : stockSummary.reserved.toLocaleString('en-ZA')
              }
              description="Allocated to orders"
              Icon={FiShoppingCart}
            />
            <MetricCard
              label="Available units"
              value={
                isLoading || loadError
                  ? '—'
                  : stockSummary.available.toLocaleString('en-ZA')
              }
              description="Physical stock less reservations"
              Icon={FiPackage}
            />
            <MetricCard
              label="Uninitialised"
              value={
                isLoading || loadError
                  ? '—'
                  : stockSummary.uninitialized.toLocaleString('en-ZA')
              }
              description="Products awaiting opening stock"
              Icon={FiPackage}
            />
          </div>

          {!isLoading && !loadError && inventory.length === 0 && (
            <div
              className="rounded-xl border p-4 text-sm"
              style={{
                background: 'var(--admin-surface)',
                borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-subtle)',
                color: 'var(--admin-text-secondary)',
              }}
            >
              No products are available in this storefront yet. Inventory
              metrics will appear here once products have been added.
            </div>
          )}

          <div
            className="rounded-xl border p-4"
            style={{
              background: 'var(--admin-surface)',
              borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-subtle)',
            }}
          >
            <h3
              className="font-semibold"
              style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)' }}
            >
              Recent stock movements
            </h3>
            <div
              className="mt-4 flex min-h-32 flex-col gap-3"
              style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-muted)' }}
            >
              {isLoading ? (
                <p className="py-8 text-center text-sm">
                  Loading stock movements...
                </p>
              ) : movements.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 py-8 text-center">
                  <FiActivity size={22} />
                  <p className="text-sm">No stock movements recorded yet.</p>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
                    <div className="relative">
                      <FiSearch
                        className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2"
                        size={16}
                        style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-muted)' }}
                      />
                      <input
                        type="search"
                        value={movementSearch}
                        onChange={(event) => setMovementSearch(event.target.value)}
                        placeholder="Search product name or SKU"
                        aria-label="Search movements by product name or SKU"
                        className="w-full rounded-lg border py-2 pl-9 pr-3 text-sm"
                        style={{
                          background: 'var(--admin-surface)',
                          borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-subtle)',
                          color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
                        }}
                      />
                    </div>

                    <select
                      value={movementTypeFilter}
                      onChange={(event) => setMovementTypeFilter(event.target.value)}
                      aria-label="Filter movements by type"
                      className="w-full rounded-lg border px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                      style={{
                        background: 'var(--admin-surface)',
                        borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-subtle)',
                        color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
                      }}
                    >
                      <option value="all">All movement types</option>
                      <option value="opening_stock">Opening stock</option>
                      <option value="replenishment">Replenishment</option>
                      <option value="adjustment_increase">Adjustment increase</option>
                      <option value="adjustment_decrease">Adjustment decrease</option>
                    </select>
                  </div>

                  <p className="text-xs" aria-live="polite">
                    Showing {Math.min(filteredMovements.length, 8)} of{' '}
                    {filteredMovements.length} matching movements
                    {' '}from the latest {movements.length} loaded records.
                  </p>

                  {filteredMovements.length === 0 ? (
                    <p className="py-8 text-center text-sm">
                      No stock movements match these filters.
                    </p>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="w-full self-stretch text-left">
                      {filteredMovements.slice(0, 8).map((movement) => (
                        <div
                          key={movement.id}
                          className="flex flex-col gap-1 border-b py-3 last:border-b-0 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between"
                          style={{ borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-subtle)' }}
                        >
                          <div>
                            <p
                              className="text-sm font-medium"
                              style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)' }}
                            >
                              {movement.product_name}
                            </p>
                            <p
                              className="text-xs"
                              style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-muted)' }}
                            >
                              {movement.product_sku
                                ? `SKU: ${movement.product_sku} · `
                                : ''}
                              {movement.movement_type.replace(/_/g, ' ')}
                              {movement.reason ? ` · ${movement.reason}` : ''}
                            </p>
                          </div>
                          <div className="text-left sm:text-right">
                            <p
                              className="text-sm font-semibold"
                              style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)' }}
                            >
                              {movement.quantity_delta > 0 ? '+' : ''}
                              {movement.quantity_delta}
                            </p>
                            <p
                              className="text-xs"
                              style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-muted)' }}
                            >
                              {new Date(movement.created_at).toLocaleString('en-ZA')}
                            </p>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {section === 'stock' && (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-5">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <MetricCard
              label="Stock received"
              value={
                isLoading || loadError
                  ? '—'
                  : stockSummary.received.toLocaleString('en-ZA')
              }
              description="Opening stock and replenishment"
              Icon={FiArrowDownCircle}
            />
            <MetricCard
              label="Stock adjusted"
              value={
                isLoading || loadError
                  ? '—'
                  : stockSummary.adjusted.toLocaleString('en-ZA')
              }
              description="Units changed by adjustments"
              Icon={FiArrowUpCircle}
            />
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
            <div>
              <h3
                className="text-base font-semibold"
                style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)' }}
              >
                Stock balances
              </h3>
              <p
                className="mt-1 text-sm"
                style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-secondary)' }}
              >
                Track physical stock, reservations and available quantities.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setSubmitError('');
                  setSelectedProductId('');
                  setQuantity('1');
                  setReason('');
                  setStockAction('receive');
                }}
                className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm font-semibold"
                style={{
                  background: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
                  color: 'var(--admin-surface)',
                }}
              >
                <FiPlus size={16} />
                Receive stock
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setSubmitError('');
                  setSelectedProductId('');
                  setQuantity('1');
                  setReason('');
                  setAdjustmentDirection('increase');
                  setStockAction('adjust');
                }}
                className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-lg border px-3 py-2 text-sm font-semibold"
                style={{
                  background: 'var(--admin-surface)',
                  color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
                  borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-subtle)',
                }}
              >
                <FiActivity size={16} />
                Adjust stock
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div
            className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border"
            style={{
              background: 'var(--admin-surface)',
              borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-subtle)',
            }}
          >
            <div className="border-b p-3 sm:p-4" style={{ borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-subtle)' }}>
              <div className="relative">
                <FiSearch
                  size={17}
                  className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2"
                  style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-muted)' }}
                />
                <input
                  type="search"
                  value={stockSearch}
                  onChange={(event) => setStockSearch(event.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Search product name or SKU"
                  aria-label="Search stock balances"
                  className="min-h-11 w-full rounded-lg border py-2 pl-10 pr-3 text-sm outline-none"
                  style={{
                    background: 'var(--admin-surface)',
                    color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
                    borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-subtle)',
                  }}
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full min-w-[760px] text-left text-sm">
                <thead>
                  <tr
                    className="border-b text-xs font-semibold"
                    style={{
                      color: 'var(--admin-text-secondary)',
                      borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-subtle)',
                    }}
                  >
                    <th className="px-4 py-3">Product</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3">SKU</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3 text-right">On hand</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3 text-right">Reserved</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3 text-right">Available</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3 text-right">Supplier cost</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {isLoading ? (
                    <tr>
                      <td colSpan={6} className="px-4 py-12 text-center">
                        <p style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-secondary)' }}>
                          Loading stock balances...
                        </p>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ) : filteredInventory.length === 0 ? (
                    <tr>
                      <td colSpan={6} className="px-4 py-12 text-center">
                        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
                          <FiPackage
                            size={24}
                            style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-muted)' }}
                          />
                          <p
                            className="text-sm font-medium"
                            style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)' }}
                          >
                            {stockSearch
                              ? 'No matching stock balances'
                              : 'No products found'}
                          </p>
                          <p
                            className="max-w-md text-xs"
                            style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-muted)' }}
                          >
                            Products for this storefront will appear here.
                          </p>
                        </div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ) : (
                    filteredInventory.map((item) => (
                      <tr
                        key={item.product_id}
                        className="border-b last:border-b-0"
                        style={{ borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-subtle)' }}
                      >
                        <td className="px-4 py-3">
                          <div
                            className="font-medium"
                            style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)' }}
                          >
                            {item.product_name}
                          </div>
                          {!item.is_initialized && (
                            <span
                              className="text-xs"
                              style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-muted)' }}
                            >
                              Opening stock required
                            </span>
                          )}
                        </td>
                        <td
                          className="px-4 py-3"
                          style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-secondary)' }}
                        >
                          {item.sku || '—'}
                        </td>
                        <td
                          className="px-4 py-3 text-right tabular-nums"
                          style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)' }}
                        >
                          {Number(item.on_hand_quantity).toLocaleString('en-ZA')}
                        </td>
                        <td
                          className="px-4 py-3 text-right tabular-nums"
                          style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-secondary)' }}
                        >
                          {Number(item.reserved_quantity).toLocaleString('en-ZA')}
                        </td>
                        <td
                          className="px-4 py-3 text-right tabular-nums"
                          style={{
                            color: Number(item.available_quantity) <= 0
                              ? 'var(--admin-text-muted)'
                              : 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
                          }}
                        >
                          {Number(item.available_quantity).toLocaleString('en-ZA')}
                        </td>
                        <td
                          className="px-4 py-3 text-right tabular-nums"
                          style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-secondary)' }}
                        >
                          {item.supplier_cost === null
                            ? '—'
                            : new Intl.NumberFormat('en-ZA', {
                                style: 'currency',
                                currency: 'ZAR',
                              }).format(Number(item.supplier_cost))}
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    ))
                  )}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </div>

          {stockAction && (
            <div
              className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center overflow-y-auto p-4"
              role="presentation"
              style={{ background: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.55)' }}
              onClick={(event) => {
                if (
                  !isSubmitting &&
                  event.target === event.currentTarget
                ) {
                  setStockAction(null);
                }
              }}
            >
              <section
                role="dialog"
                aria-modal="true"
                aria-labelledby="stock-action-title"
                className="my-auto w-full max-w-lg rounded-xl border p-5 shadow-xl"
                style={{
                  background: 'var(--admin-surface)',
                  borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-subtle)',
                }}
              >
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
                  <div>
                    <h3
                      id="stock-action-title"
                      className="text-lg font-semibold"
                      style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)' }}
                    >
                      {stockAction === 'receive'
                        ? 'Receive stock'
                        : 'Adjust stock'}
                    </h3>
                    <p
                      className="mt-1 text-sm"
                      style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-secondary)' }}
                    >
                      {stockAction === 'receive'
                        ? 'Record new stock received from a supplier.'
                        : 'Record a verified correction to the physical stock balance.'}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setStockAction(null)}
                    disabled={isSubmitting}
                    aria-label="Close dialog"
                    className="rounded-lg p-2"
                    style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-secondary)' }}
                  >
                    <FiX size={19} />
                  </button>
                </div>

                <form onSubmit={handleStockSubmit} className="mt-5 flex flex-col gap-4">
                  <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                    <label
                      htmlFor="inventory-product"
                      className="text-sm font-medium"
                      style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)' }}
                    >
                      Product
                    </label>
                    <select
                      id="inventory-product"
                      value={selectedProductId}
                      onChange={(event) => setSelectedProductId(event.target.value)}
                      required
                      disabled={isSubmitting}
                      className="min-h-11 w-full rounded-lg border px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none"
                      style={{
                        background: 'var(--admin-surface)',
                        color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
                        borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-subtle)',
                      }}
                    >
                      <option value="">Select a product</option>
                      {inventory
                        .filter((item) => item.is_active)
                        .map((item) => (
                          <option key={item.product_id} value={item.product_id}>
                            {item.product_name}
                            {!item.is_initialized ? ' · Opening stock required' : ''}
                          </option>
                        ))}
                    </select>
                  </div>

                  {stockAction === 'adjust' && (
                    <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                      <label
                        htmlFor="inventory-adjustment-direction"
                        className="text-sm font-medium"
                        style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)' }}
                      >
                        Adjustment type
                      </label>
                      <select
                        id="inventory-adjustment-direction"
                        value={adjustmentDirection}
                        onChange={(event) =>
                          setAdjustmentDirection(
                            event.target.value as 'increase' | 'decrease'
                          )
                        }
                        disabled={isSubmitting}
                        className="min-h-11 w-full rounded-lg border px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none"
                        style={{
                          background: 'var(--admin-surface)',
                          color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
                          borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-subtle)',
                        }}
                      >
                        <option value="increase">Increase stock</option>
                        <option value="decrease">Decrease stock</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                    <label
                      htmlFor="inventory-quantity"
                      className="text-sm font-medium"
                      style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)' }}
                    >
                      Quantity
                    </label>
                    <input
                      id="inventory-quantity"
                      type="number"
                      min="1"
                      max="1000000"
                      step="1"
                      inputMode="numeric"
                      value={quantity}
                      onChange={(event) => setQuantity(event.target.value)}
                      required
                      disabled={isSubmitting}
                      className="min-h-11 w-full rounded-lg border px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none"
                      style={{
                        background: 'var(--admin-surface)',
                        color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
                        borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-subtle)',
                      }}
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                    <label
                      htmlFor="inventory-reason"
                      className="text-sm font-medium"
                      style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)' }}
                    >
                      Reason
                      {stockAction === 'receive' &&
                        selectedProduct &&
                        !selectedProduct.is_initialized
                        ? ' (optional for opening stock)'
                        : ''}
                    </label>
                    <textarea
                      id="inventory-reason"
                      value={reason}
                      onChange={(event) => setReason(event.target.value)}
                      required={
                        stockAction === 'adjust' ||
                        Boolean(selectedProduct?.is_initialized)
                      }
                      maxLength={500}
                      rows={3}
                      disabled={isSubmitting}
                      placeholder={
                        stockAction === 'receive'
                          ? 'Supplier, purchase reference or other details'
                          : 'Explain why this stock correction is required'
                      }
                      className="w-full rounded-lg border px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none"
                      style={{
                        background: 'var(--admin-surface)',
                        color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
                        borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-subtle)',
                      }}
                    />
                  </div>

                  {submitError && (
                    <p
                      role="alert"
                      className="rounded-lg border p-3 text-sm"
                      style={{
                        background: 'var(--admin-page)',
                        borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-subtle)',
                        color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
                      }}
                    >
                      {submitError}
                    </p>
                  )}

                  <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setStockAction(null)}
                      disabled={isSubmitting}
                      className="min-h-10 rounded-lg border px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold"
                      style={{
                        background: 'var(--admin-surface)',
                        color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
                        borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-subtle)',
                      }}
                    >
                      Cancel
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="submit"
                      disabled={
                        isSubmitting ||
                        inventory.filter((item) => item.is_active).length === 0
                      }
                      className="min-h-10 rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
                      style={{
                        background: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
                        color: 'var(--admin-surface)',
                      }}
                    >
                      {isSubmitting
                        ? 'Saving...'
                        : stockAction === 'receive'
                          ? 'Save received stock'
                          : 'Save adjustment'}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </form>
              </section>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {section === 'sales' && (
        <div
          className="rounded-xl border p-5"
          style={{
            background: 'var(--admin-surface)',
            borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-subtle)',
          }}
        >
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <FiShoppingCart
              size={18}
              style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-secondary)' }}
            />
            <h3
              className="font-semibold"
              style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)' }}
            >
              Direct sales
            </h3>
          </div>
          <p
            className="mt-2 text-sm"
            style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-secondary)' }}
          >
            Record offline transactions, select products, capture quantities
            and payment details, and update inventory through audited movements.
          </p>
        </div>
      )}

      {section === 'receipts' && (
        <div
          className="rounded-xl border p-5"
          style={{
            background: 'var(--admin-surface)',
            borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-subtle)',
          }}
        >
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <FiFileText
              size={18}
              style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-secondary)' }}
            />
            <h3
              className="font-semibold"
              style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)' }}
            >
              Receipt management
            </h3>
          </div>
          <p
            className="mt-2 text-sm"
            style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-secondary)' }}
          >
            Review receipt records, approve them, send them and track their
            status.
          </p>
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/components/overview/AdminOverview.tsx =====
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import {
  FiDownload,
  FiFilter,
  FiPlus,
  FiRefreshCw,
} from 'react-icons/fi';
import type { AdminRow, Order, Storefront } from '../../types/admin.types';
import type { AdminActivity } from '../../hooks/useAdminActivity';
import type { OverviewDateSelection } from '../../types/overview.types';
import { calculateOverviewAnalytics } from '../../analytics/overview/overviewCalculations';
import {
  getOverviewDateKey,
  getOverviewDateRange,
} from '../../utils/overview/dateRanges';
import { getOverviewQuickActionDates } from '../../utils/overview/quickActions';
import {
  readOverviewDateSelection,
  writeOverviewDateSelection,
} from '../../utils/overview/dateSelectionStorage';
import { getCategoryTheme } from '../../../../utils/categoryTheme';
import AccentButton from '../AccentButton';
import AdminDropdown from '../AdminDropdown';
import BusinessSummary from './sections/BusinessSummary';
import SalesPerformance from './sections/SalesPerformance';
import OrderOperations from './sections/OrderOperations';
import ProductPerformance from './sections/ProductPerformance';
import StoreComparison from './sections/StoreComparison';
import RecentActivity from './sections/RecentActivity';
import './overview.css';

type Props = {
  userId: string;
  userName: string;
  onAddProduct: () => void;
  orders: Order[];
  products: AdminRow[];
  storefrontFilter: 'all' | Storefront;
  onStorefrontChange: (value: 'all' | Storefront) => void;
  canSwitchStore: boolean;
  storeCreatedAt: Partial<Record<Storefront, string>>;
  onRefresh: () => Promise<void>;
  ordersLoadError: string | null;
  productsLoadError: string | null;
  isLoadingOrders: boolean;
  isLoadingProducts: boolean;
  activities: AdminActivity[];
  isLoadingActivities: boolean;
  onViewAllActivities: () => void;
};

const QUICK_ACTIONS = [
  { key: '7-days', label: 'Last 7 days' },
  { key: 'month', label: 'Last month' },
  { key: '3-months', label: 'Last 3 months' },
  { key: 'quarter', label: 'Previous quarter' },
  { key: '6-months', label: 'Last 6 months' },
  { key: '2026', label: '2026' },
] as const;

const QUICK_ACTION_OPTIONS = QUICK_ACTIONS.map((action) => ({
  value: action.key,
  label: action.label,
}));

function getFirstName(userName: string): string {
  const firstName = userName.trim().split(/[\s._-]+/)[0];

  if (!firstName) return 'Admin';

  return firstName.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + firstName.slice(1);
}

function getTimeBasedGreeting(): string {
  const hour = new Date().getHours();

  if (hour < 12) return 'Good morning';
  if (hour < 18) return 'Good afternoon';
  return 'Good evening';
}

function getSelectionLabel(selection: OverviewDateSelection): string {
  if (selection.type === 'preset') {
    return String(selection.preset)
      .replace(/-/g, ' ')
      .replace(/\b\w/g, (character) => character.toUpperCase());
  }

  const count = new Set(selection.dates).size;

  if (count === 0) return 'All available dates';

  return `${count} ${count === 1 ? 'day' : 'days'} selected`;
}

function getSelectionFileKey(selection: OverviewDateSelection): string {
  if (selection.type === 'preset') {
    return `preset-${selection.preset}`;
  }

  const dates = [...new Set(selection.dates)].sort();

  return dates.length > 0
    ? dates.join('_')
    : 'all-available-dates';
}

function get2026Dates(now = new Date()): string[] {
  const todayKey = getOverviewDateKey(now);
  const yearStart = Date.UTC(2026, 0, 1);
  const finalDateKey =
    todayKey < '2026-12-31' ? todayKey : '2026-12-31';

  const [year, month, day] = finalDateKey.split('-').map(Number);
  const yearEnd = Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day);

  const count = Math.max(
    0,
    Math.floor((yearEnd - yearStart) / 86400000) + 1,
  );

  return Array.from({ length: count }, (_, index) =>
    getOverviewDateKey(
      new Date(yearStart + index * 86400000),
    ),
  );
}

function getQuickActionDates(
  action: string,
  now = new Date(),
): string[] {
  return action === '2026'
    ? get2026Dates(now)
    : getOverviewQuickActionDates(action, now);
}

function getActiveQuickAction(
  selection: OverviewDateSelection,
): string {
  if (selection.type !== 'dates') return '';

  const selected = [...new Set(selection.dates)].sort();

  return QUICK_ACTIONS.find((action) => {
    const dates = getQuickActionDates(action.key);

    return (
      dates.length > 0 &&
      dates.length === selected.length &&
      dates.every((date, index) => date === selected[index])
    );
  })?.key ?? '';
}

function csvCell(value: string | number): string {
  const text = String(value);

  return '"' + text.replace(/"/g, '""') + '"';
}

function downloadOverviewReport(
  analytics: ReturnType<typeof calculateOverviewAnalytics>,
  storefront: 'all' | Storefront,
  dateRange: OverviewDateSelection,
) {
  const rows: (string | number)[][] = [
    ['SIMATU OVERVIEW REPORT'],
    [
      'Store',
      storefront === 'all'
        ? 'All stores'
        : storefront === 'yotaabo'
          ? 'Yotaabo'
          : 'Poshies',
    ],
    ['Date range', getSelectionLabel(dateRange)],
    [
      'Generated at',
      new Date().toLocaleString('en-ZA', {
        timeZone: 'Africa/Johannesburg',
      }),
    ],
    [],
    ['BUSINESS SUMMARY'],
    ['Metric', 'Value'],
    ['Orders received', analytics.businessSummary.ordersReceived],
    ['Outstanding orders', analytics.businessSummary.outstandingOrders],
    ['Pending orders', analytics.businessSummary.pendingOrders],
    ['Confirmed orders', analytics.businessSummary.confirmedOrders],
    ['Delivered orders', analytics.businessSummary.deliveredOrders],
    ['Cancelled orders', analytics.businessSummary.cancelledOrders],
    ['Total products', analytics.businessSummary.totalProducts],
    ['Active products', analytics.businessSummary.activeProducts],
    ['Hidden products', analytics.businessSummary.hiddenProducts],
    [],
    ['RECORDED SALES ACTIVITY'],
    ['Metric', 'Value'],
    [
      'Recorded sales value',
      analytics.salesPerformance.recordedSalesValue,
    ],
    ['Delivered orders', analytics.salesPerformance.deliveredOrders],
    [],
    ['ORDER OPERATIONS'],
    ['Status', 'Orders'],
    ['Pending', analytics.orderOperations.pending],
    ['Confirmed', analytics.orderOperations.confirmed],
    ['Delivered', analytics.orderOperations.delivered],
    ['Cancelled', analytics.orderOperations.cancelled],
    [],
    ['PRODUCT PERFORMANCE'],
    ['Product', 'Units sold', 'Recorded value'],
    ...analytics.productPerformance.map((item) => [
      item.productName,
      item.unitsSold,
      item.recordedValue,
    ]),
    [],
    ['STORE COMPARISON'],
    [
      'Store',
      'Orders',
      'Delivered orders',
      'Recorded sales value',
      'Units sold',
    ],
    ...analytics.storeComparison.map((item) => [
      item.storefront,
      item.orders,
      item.deliveredOrders,
      item.recordedSalesValue,
      item.unitsSold,
    ]),
  ];

  const csv = rows
    .map((row) => row.map(csvCell).join(','))
    .join('\r\n');

  const blob = new Blob(['\uFEFF', csv], {
    type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;',
  });

  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const link = document.createElement('a');
  const storeName =
    storefront === 'all' ? 'all-stores' : storefront;
  const date = getOverviewDateKey(new Date());

  link.href = url;
  link.download =
    `simatu-overview-${storeName}-${getSelectionFileKey(dateRange)}-${date}.csv`;

  document.body.appendChild(link);
  link.click();
  link.remove();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}

export default function AdminOverview({
  userId,
  userName,
  orders,
  products,
  storefrontFilter,
  onStorefrontChange,
  canSwitchStore,
  storeCreatedAt,
  onRefresh,
  ordersLoadError,
  productsLoadError,
  isLoadingOrders,
  isLoadingProducts,
  activities,
  isLoadingActivities,
  onViewAllActivities,
}: Props) {
  const [dateRange, setDateRange] = useState<OverviewDateSelection>(
    () => readOverviewDateSelection(userId),
  );

  const storeStartDate = useMemo(() => {
    const dates =
      storefrontFilter === 'all'
        ? Object.values(storeCreatedAt)
        : [storeCreatedAt[storefrontFilter]];

    return dates
      .filter((value): value is string => Boolean(value))
      .sort(
        (a, b) =>
          new Date(a).getTime() - new Date(b).getTime(),
      )[0];
  }, [storeCreatedAt, storefrontFilter]);

  const analytics = useMemo(
    () =>
      calculateOverviewAnalytics({
        orders,
        dateRange: getOverviewDateRange(dateRange, storeStartDate),
        catalogue: {
          total: products.length,
          active: products.filter((product) => product.is_active).length,
          hidden: products.filter((product) => !product.is_active).length,
        },
        selectedStore: storefrontFilter,
      }),
    [
      orders,
      products,
      dateRange,
      storefrontFilter,
      storeStartDate,
    ],
  );

  const hasLoadError = Boolean(
    ordersLoadError || productsLoadError,
  );

  const isLoading = isLoadingOrders || isLoadingProducts;
  const activeQuickAction = getActiveQuickAction(dateRange);
  const yotaaboColor = getCategoryTheme('His').hexColor;
  const poshiesColor = getCategoryTheme('Hers').hexColor;

  function handleDateFilterChange(value: string) {
    const dates = getQuickActionDates(value);

    if (dates.length === 0) return;

    const selection: OverviewDateSelection = {
      type: 'dates',
      dates,
    };

    writeOverviewDateSelection(userId, selection);
    setDateRange(selection);
  }

  return (
    <section
      className="admin-overview flex w-full flex-col"
      style={{ gap: 'var(--admin-space-2xl)' }}
    >
      <header
        className="flex w-full flex-col"
        style={{ gap: 'var(--admin-space-md)' }}
      >
        <div
          className="flex w-full flex-col"
          style={{ gap: 'var(--admin-space-xs)' }}
        >
          <h1
            style={{
              fontSize: 'var(--admin-greeting-size)',
              lineHeight: 'var(--admin-greeting-line)',
              fontWeight: 'var(--admin-greeting-weight)',
              color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
              margin: 0,
            }}
          >
            {getTimeBasedGreeting()}, {getFirstName(userName)}
          </h1>

          <p
            style={{
              fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-supporting)',
              lineHeight: 'var(--admin-line-compact)',
              fontWeight: 'var(--admin-weight-normal)',
              color: 'var(--admin-text-secondary)',
              margin: 0,
            }}
          >
            Here's your business overview for today.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div
          className="overview-toolbar-actions"
          aria-label="Overview actions"
        >
          <AccentButton
            variant="accent"
            shape="pill"
            className="overview-action-button"
            ariaLabel="Export overview report"
            onClick={() => {
              if (!hasLoadError) {
                downloadOverviewReport(
                  analytics,
                  storefrontFilter,
                  dateRange,
                );
              }
            }}
            style={{
              minWidth: 'auto',
              gap: 'var(--admin-space-xs)',
              paddingInline: 'var(--admin-space-md)',
            }}
          >
            <FiDownload size={16} aria-hidden="true" />
            <span className="overview-action-label">Export</span>
          </AccentButton>

          <AccentButton
            variant="accent"
            shape="pill"
            className="overview-action-button"
            ariaLabel={
              isLoading
                ? 'Refreshing overview'
                : 'Refresh overview'
            }
            onClick={() => {
              if (!isLoading) void onRefresh();
            }}
            style={{
              minWidth: 'auto',
              gap: 'var(--admin-space-xs)',
              paddingInline: 'var(--admin-space-md)',
            }}
          >
            <FiRefreshCw
              size={16}
              aria-hidden="true"
              className={isLoading ? 'animate-spin' : ''}
            />
            <span className="overview-action-label">
              {isLoading ? 'Refreshing' : 'Refresh'}
            </span>
          </AccentButton>

          <AdminDropdown
            value={activeQuickAction}
            onChange={handleDateFilterChange}
            options={QUICK_ACTION_OPTIONS}
            placeholder="Filter period"
            menuWidth="220px"
            compact
            iconOnly
            ariaLabel="Filter overview by date"
            triggerIcon={<FiFilter size={16} aria-hidden="true" />}
          />

          <AccentButton
            variant="accent"
            shape="pill"
            className="overview-action-button"
            ariaLabel="Add"
            style={{
              minWidth: 'auto',
              gap: 'var(--admin-space-xs)',
              paddingInline: 'var(--admin-space-md)',
            }}
          >
            <FiPlus size={16} aria-hidden="true" />
            <span className="overview-action-label">Add</span>
          </AccentButton>
        </div>

        {canSwitchStore && (
          <div className="overview-store-row">
            <div
              className="admin-segmented overview-store-selector"
              role="group"
              aria-label="Filter overview by storefront"
            >
              <AccentButton
                shape="pill"
                className="overview-store-option"
                variant={
                  storefrontFilter === 'poshies'
                    ? 'active'
                    : 'neutral'
                }
                storeColor={poshiesColor}
                activeColor={poshiesColor}
                ariaCurrent={
                  storefrontFilter === 'poshies'
                    ? 'page'
                    : undefined
                }
                onClick={() => onStorefrontChange('poshies')}
              >
                Poshies
              </AccentButton>

              <AccentButton
                shape="pill"
                className="overview-store-option"
                variant={
                  storefrontFilter === 'yotaabo'
                    ? 'active'
                    : 'neutral'
                }
                storeColor={yotaaboColor}
                activeColor={yotaaboColor}
                ariaCurrent={
                  storefrontFilter === 'yotaabo'
                    ? 'page'
                    : undefined
                }
                onClick={() => onStorefrontChange('yotaabo')}
              >
                Yotaabo
              </AccentButton>

              <AccentButton
                shape="pill"
                className="overview-store-option"
                variant={
                  storefrontFilter === 'all'
                    ? 'active'
                    : 'neutral'
                }
                activeColor="var(--admin-accent)"
                ariaCurrent={
                  storefrontFilter === 'all' ? 'page' : undefined
                }
                onClick={() => onStorefrontChange('all')}
              >
                All
              </AccentButton>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </header>

      {isLoading && (
        <p
          className="admin-type-supporting overview-text-muted"
          role="status"
          style={{ margin: 0 }}
        >
          Refreshing overview data…
        </p>
      )}

      {hasLoadError && (
        <div
          role="alert"
          className="admin-card-bg rounded-xl border"
          style={{
            borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-subtle)',
            borderRadius: 'var(--ds-radius-lg)',
            padding: 'var(--admin-space-md)',
            color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
          }}
        >
          <p
            className="admin-type-body"
            style={{
              fontWeight: 'var(--admin-weight-semibold)',
              margin: 0,
            }}
          >
            Some overview data could not be refreshed.
          </p>

          {ordersLoadError && (
            <p
              className="admin-type-supporting"
              style={{
                margin: 'var(--admin-space-xs) 0 0',
              }}
            >
              Orders: {ordersLoadError}
            </p>
          )}

          {productsLoadError && (
            <p
              className="admin-type-supporting"
              style={{
                margin: 'var(--admin-space-xs) 0 0',
              }}
            >
              Products: {productsLoadError}
            </p>
          )}

          <p
            className="admin-type-micro overview-text-muted"
            style={{
              margin: 'var(--admin-space-sm) 0 0',
            }}
          >
            Displayed figures may reflect the last successfully loaded snapshot.
          </p>
        </div>
      )}

      <BusinessSummary data={analytics.businessSummary} />
      <SalesPerformance data={analytics.salesPerformance} />
      <OrderOperations data={analytics.orderOperations} />
      <ProductPerformance data={analytics.productPerformance} />
      <StoreComparison data={analytics.storeComparison} />

      <RecentActivity
        activities={activities}
        isLoading={isLoadingActivities}
        onViewAll={onViewAllActivities}
      />
    </section>
  );
}

  );
}

===== src/features/admin/components/overview/sections/RecentActivity.tsx =====
import { FiArrowUpRight } from 'react-icons/fi';
import type { AdminActivity } from '../../../hooks/useAdminActivity';

type Props = {
  activities: AdminActivity[];
  isLoading: boolean;
  onViewAll: () => void;
};

function getDescription(activity: AdminActivity): string {
  const metadata = activity.metadata ?? {};

  const productName =
    typeof metadata.product_name === 'string'
      ? metadata.product_name.trim()
      : '';

  const rawOrderNumber =
    typeof metadata.order_number === 'string'
      ? metadata.order_number.trim()
      : '';

  const orderNumber = rawOrderNumber.replace(/^#+/, '');

  if (activity.event_type === 'product.created' && productName) {
    return `${productName} added`;
  }

  if (activity.event_type === 'order.received' && orderNumber) {
    return `Order #${orderNumber} received`;
  }

  return activity.description.replace(/^(Order)\s*#+/i, '$1 #');
}

function formatActivityDate(value: string): string {
  const date = new Date(value);

  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
    return 'Unknown date';
  }

  return date.toLocaleString('en-ZA', {
    day: '2-digit',
    month: 'short',
    hour: '2-digit',
    minute: '2-digit',
  });
}

export default function RecentActivity({
  activities,
  isLoading,
  onViewAll,
}: Props) {
  const recentActivities = [...activities]
    .filter((activity) => Number.isFinite(Date.parse(activity.created_at)))
    .sort(
      (a, b) =>
        new Date(b.created_at).getTime() -
        new Date(a.created_at).getTime()
    )
    .slice(0, 4);

  return (
    <section className="w-full" aria-labelledby="overview-recent-activity-title">
      <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h2
            id="overview-recent-activity-title"
            className="admin-section-header-title admin-type-page-title text-[#4d4d4d]"
          >
            Recent Activities
          </h2>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[#4d4d4d]/65">
            Latest store updates
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onViewAll}
          className="inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1 text-xs font-semibold text-[#4d4d4d] transition-opacity hover:opacity-65"
        >
          View all
          <FiArrowUpRight size={15} aria-hidden="true" />
        </button>
      </div>

      {isLoading && recentActivities.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="px-4 py-8 text-sm text-[#4d4d4d]/50">
          Loading activities...
        </p>
      ) : recentActivities.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="px-4 py-8 text-sm text-[#4d4d4d]/50">
          No activities found.
        </p>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {recentActivities.map((activity) => (
            <article
              key={activity.id}
              className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-[5px] border border-[#4d4d4d]/[0.10] bg-[#f5f5f5] px-4 py-2.5 shadow-[0_1px_3px_rgba(0,0,0,0.035)]"
            >
              <p className="min-w-0 text-sm leading-relaxed text-[#4d4d4d]/45">
                {getDescription(activity)}
              </p>

              <time
                dateTime={activity.created_at}
                className="shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap text-right text-xs text-[#4d4d4d]/45"
              >
                {formatActivityDate(activity.created_at)}
              </time>
            </article>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/components/overview/sections/SalesPerformance.tsx =====
import type { OverviewSalesPerformance } from '../../../types/overview.types';
import { formatOverviewCurrency } from '../../../utils/overview/currency';
import OverviewMetric from '../shared/OverviewMetric';
import OverviewSection from '../shared/OverviewSection';

type Props = {
  data: OverviewSalesPerformance;
};

export default function SalesPerformance({ data }: Props) {
  const maximum = Math.max(
    ...data.dailySales.map((point) => point.value),
    0,
  );

  return (
    <OverviewSection
      title="Sales performance"
      description="Recorded sales activity from delivered online orders. Delivery does not confirm payment."
    >
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <OverviewMetric
          label="Recorded sales value"
          value={formatOverviewCurrency(data.recordedSalesValue)}
          description="Delivered online orders only"
        />
        <OverviewMetric
          label="Delivered orders"
          value={data.deliveredOrders}
          description="Within selected period"
        />
      </div>

      <div
        className="flex flex-col rounded-xl border p-4"
        style={{
          borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-subtle)',
          borderRadius: 'var(--admin-radius-lg, 12px)',
          background: 'var(--admin-surface)',
          gap: 'var(--admin-space-md)',
        }}
      >
        <h3
          className="text-sm font-semibold"
          style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)' }}
        >
          Daily recorded sales
        </h3>

        {data.dailySales.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="py-6 text-center text-sm overview-text-muted">
            No delivered-order sales recorded in this period.
          </p>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex items-end gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-2">
            {data.dailySales.map((point) => (
              <div
                key={point.date}
                className="flex min-w-12 flex-1 flex-col items-center gap-2"
                title={`${point.date}: ${formatOverviewCurrency(point.value)}`}
              >
                <span className="text-[10px] overview-text-muted">
                  {formatOverviewCurrency(point.value)}
                </span>
                <div
                  className="flex h-28 w-full items-end overflow-hidden rounded-sm"
                  style={{ background: 'var(--admin-surface-subtle)' }}
                >
                  <div
                    className="w-full rounded-sm"
                    style={{
                      height: `${maximum > 0 ? Math.max(4, (point.value / maximum) * 100) : 0}%`,
                      background: 'var(--admin-color-text-primary, #404040)',
                    }}
                  />
                </div>
                <span className="text-[10px] overview-text-muted">
                  {point.label.slice(5)}
                </span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </OverviewSection>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/components/overview/sections/StoreComparison.tsx =====
import type { OverviewStorePerformance } from '../../../types/overview.types';

          style={{ color: 'var(--admin-text-secondary)' }}

          {description}
        </span>

  );
}

import type { CSSProperties } from 'react';

===== src/features/admin/components/skeleton/SkeletonBlock.tsx =====
import type { CSSProperties } from 'react';

type BlockProps = {
  height: string;
  width?: string;
  radius?: string;
  tone?: 'subtle' | 'strong' | 'accent' | 'dark' | 'light';
  className?: string;
  style?: CSSProperties;
};

/**
 * A raw skeleton block — no text, no icons, just a div with explicit
 * height, width, background and radius. All values come from tokens
 * or from the caller. Colors come from --admin-* variables (real
 * variables on the admin-scope element) with safe fallbacks.
 */
export function Block({
  height,
  width = '100%',
  radius = 'var(--ds-radius-md, 0.375rem)',
  tone = 'subtle',
  className = '',
  style,
}: BlockProps) {
  const bg: Record<string, string> = {
    subtle: 'var(--admin-surface-subtle, var(--admin-surface-subtle))',
    strong: 'var(--admin-border-subtle, rgba(0,0,0,0.1))',
    accent: 'var(--admin-accent, var(--admin-text-primary))',
    dark: 'var(--admin-text-primary, #2a2a2a)',
    light: 'var(--admin-surface, var(--admin-surface))',
  };

  return (
    <div
      aria-hidden="true"
      className={className}
      style={{
        height,
        width,
        borderRadius: radius,
        backgroundColor: bg[tone],
        ...style,
      }}
    />
  );
}

type CardProps = {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  height: string;
  variant?: 'default' | 'dark';
  className?: string;
};

/**
 * Raw card chrome — explicit height, token background, token border, no padding.
 * The height drives the layout; inner blocks are positioned by the caller.
 */
export function Card({ children, height, variant = 'default', className = '' }: CardProps) {
  const isDark = variant === 'dark';
  return (
    <div
      className={`flex min-w-0 flex-col overflow-hidden border ${className}`}
      style={{
        height,
        borderRadius: 'var(--ds-radius-xl, 0.75rem)',
        borderColor: isDark
          ? 'var(--admin-border-strong, rgba(0,0,0,0.2))'
          : 'var(--admin-border-subtle, rgba(0,0,0,0.1))',
        backgroundColor: isDark
          ? 'var(--admin-text-primary, #2a2a2a)'
          : 'var(--admin-surface, var(--admin-surface))',
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}
