import type { Order } from '../../types/admin.types';

type OrdersStatCardProps = {
  totalOrders: number;
  pendingOrders: number;
  deliveredOrders: number;
  orders: Order[];
  accentColor: string;
};

const CARD_STYLE = {
  minHeight: 'var(--admin-stat-card-min-height, 160px)',
  borderRadius: 'var(--ds-radius-lg)',
  borderWidth: '1px',
  borderStyle: 'solid' as const,
  borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-strong)',
  backgroundColor: 'var(--admin-surface)',
  backgroundImage: [
    'linear-gradient(to bottom, var(--admin-surface) 0%, transparent 30%)',
    'linear-gradient(to top, var(--admin-surface) 0%, transparent 30%)',
    'linear-gradient(to right, color-mix(in srgb, var(--admin-border-strong) 8%, transparent) 1px, transparent 1px)',
    'linear-gradient(to bottom, color-mix(in srgb, var(--admin-border-strong) 8%, transparent) 1px, transparent 1px)',
  ].join(','),
  backgroundSize: [
    '100% 100%',
    '100% 100%',
    'var(--admin-card-grid-size) var(--admin-card-grid-size)',
    'var(--admin-card-grid-size) var(--admin-card-grid-size)',
  ].join(','),
  backgroundRepeat: 'no-repeat, no-repeat, repeat, repeat',
  padding: 'var(--admin-space-md)',
  gap: 'var(--admin-space-xs)',
} as const;

export default function OrdersStatCard({
  totalOrders,
  pendingOrders,
  deliveredOrders,
  orders,
  accentColor,
}: OrdersStatCardProps) {
  // 30 daily bars
  const days = 30;
  const now = new Date();
  const dailyCounts: number[] = [];
  for (let i = days - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const d = new Date(now);
    d.setDate(d.getDate() - i);
    d.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    const next = new Date(d);
    next.setDate(next.getDate() + 1);
    const count = orders.filter((o) => {
      if (o.status === 'cancelled') return false;
      const t = new Date(o.created_at).getTime();
      return t >= d.getTime() && t < next.getTime();
    }).length;
    dailyCounts.push(count);
  }
  const dailyMax = Math.max(1, ...dailyCounts);
  const barAreaHeight = 32;

  return (
    <div className="admin-card-bg relative flex flex-col overflow-hidden" style={CARD_STYLE}>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between" style={{ gap: 'var(--admin-space-xs)' }}>
        <span
          style={{
            fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-supporting)',
            fontWeight: 'var(--admin-weight-normal)',
            color: 'var(--admin-text-tertiary)',
            lineHeight: 1,
          }}
        >
          Orders
        </span>
        <span style={{ fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-micro)', color: accentColor, lineHeight: 1 }}>
          Last 30 days
        </span>
      </div>
      <span
        style={{
          fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-page-title)',
          fontWeight: 'var(--admin-weight-semibold)',
          color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
          lineHeight: 1,
          fontVariantNumeric: 'tabular-nums',
        }}
      >
        {totalOrders.toLocaleString()}
      </span>

      <div
        className="w-full"
        style={{
          marginTop: 'auto',
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'flex-end',
          gap: '1px',
          height: `${barAreaHeight}px`,
        }}
        aria-label={`Daily order count over the last 30 days, peak ${dailyMax}`}
      >
        {dailyCounts.map((count, i) => {
          const h = count === 0 ? 2 : Math.max(4, (count / dailyMax) * barAreaHeight);
          return (
            <span
              key={i}
              title={`${count} order${count === 1 ? '' : 's'}`}
              style={{
                flex: 1,
                height: `${h}px`,
                backgroundColor: count === 0 ? 'var(--admin-border-subtle)' : accentColor,
                borderRadius: '1px',
                transition: 'height 200ms ease',
              }}
            />
          );
        })}
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center" style={{ gap: 'var(--admin-space-md)', marginTop: 'var(--admin-space-xs)' }}>
        <span style={{ fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-micro)', color: 'var(--admin-text-tertiary)', fontVariantNumeric: 'tabular-nums' }}>
          <span style={{ color: accentColor, fontWeight: 'var(--admin-weight-semibold)' }}>{pendingOrders}</span>{' '}
          pending
        </span>
        <span style={{ fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-micro)', color: 'var(--admin-text-tertiary)', fontVariantNumeric: 'tabular-nums' }}>
          <span style={{ color: accentColor, fontWeight: 'var(--admin-weight-semibold)' }}>{deliveredOrders}</span>{' '}
          delivered
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/components/stats/ProductsStatCard.tsx =====
import type { AdminRow } from '../../types/admin.types';

type ProductsStatCardProps = {
  totalProducts: number;
  activeProducts: number;
  inactiveProducts: number;
  products: AdminRow[];
  accentColor: string;
};

const CARD_STYLE = {
  minHeight: 'var(--admin-stat-card-min-height, 160px)',
  borderRadius: 'var(--ds-radius-lg)',
  borderWidth: '1px',
  borderStyle: 'solid' as const,
  borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-strong)',
  backgroundColor: 'var(--admin-surface)',
  backgroundImage: [
    'linear-gradient(to bottom, var(--admin-surface) 0%, transparent 30%)',
    'linear-gradient(to top, var(--admin-surface) 0%, transparent 30%)',
    'linear-gradient(to right, color-mix(in srgb, var(--admin-border-strong) 8%, transparent) 1px, transparent 1px)',
    'linear-gradient(to bottom, color-mix(in srgb, var(--admin-border-strong) 8%, transparent) 1px, transparent 1px)',
  ].join(','),
  backgroundSize: [
    '100% 100%',
    '100% 100%',
    'var(--admin-card-grid-size) var(--admin-card-grid-size)',
    'var(--admin-card-grid-size) var(--admin-card-grid-size)',
  ].join(','),
  backgroundRepeat: 'no-repeat, no-repeat, repeat, repeat',
  padding: 'var(--admin-space-md)',
  gap: 'var(--admin-space-xs)',
} as const;

export default function ProductsStatCard({
  totalProducts,
  activeProducts,
  inactiveProducts,
  products,
  accentColor,
}: ProductsStatCardProps) {
  // 8-week trend
  const weeks = 8;
  const weekMs = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
  const startOfThisWeek = new Date();
  startOfThisWeek.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  startOfThisWeek.setDate(startOfThisWeek.getDate() - startOfThisWeek.getDay());
  const weeklyCounts: number[] = [];
  for (let i = weeks - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const start = new Date(startOfThisWeek.getTime() - i * weekMs);
    const end = new Date(start.getTime() + weekMs);
    const count = products.filter((p) => {
      const t = new Date(p.created_at).getTime();
      return t >= start.getTime() && t < end.getTime();
    }).length;
    weeklyCounts.push(count);
  }
  const weeklyMax = Math.max(1, ...weeklyCounts);
  const barAreaHeight = 32;

  const w = barAreaHeight * 4;
  const h = barAreaHeight;
  const n = weeklyCounts.length;
  const denom = Math.max(1, n - 1);
  const pts = weeklyCounts.map((v, i) => {
    const x = (i / denom) * w;
    const y = h - (v / weeklyMax) * (h - 4) - 2;
    return `${x.toFixed(1)},${y.toFixed(1)}`;
  });
  const linePath = `M ${pts.join(' L ')}`;
  const areaPath = `${linePath} L ${w},${h} L 0,${h} Z`;

  return (
    <div className="admin-card-bg relative flex flex-col overflow-hidden" style={CARD_STYLE}>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between" style={{ gap: 'var(--admin-space-xs)' }}>
        <span
          style={{
            fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-supporting)',
            fontWeight: 'var(--admin-weight-normal)',
            color: 'var(--admin-text-tertiary)',
            lineHeight: 1,
          }}
        >
          Products
        </span>
        <span style={{ fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-micro)', color: accentColor, lineHeight: 1 }}>
          Last 8 weeks
        </span>
      </div>
      <span
        style={{
          fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-page-title)',
          fontWeight: 'var(--admin-weight-semibold)',
          color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
          lineHeight: 1,
          fontVariantNumeric: 'tabular-nums',
        }}
      >
        {totalProducts.toLocaleString()}
      </span>

      <div className="w-full" style={{ marginTop: 'auto', height: `${barAreaHeight}px` }}>
        <svg
          width="100%"
          height={barAreaHeight}
          viewBox={`0 0 ${w} ${h}`}
          preserveAspectRatio="none"
          style={{ display: 'block' }}
          aria-label={`Products added per week over the last 8 weeks, peak ${weeklyMax}`}
        >
          <path d={areaPath} fill={accentColor} fillOpacity={0.15} />
          <path
            d={linePath}
            fill="none"
            stroke={accentColor}
            strokeWidth={1.5}
            strokeLinejoin="round"
            strokeLinecap="round"
            vectorEffect="non-scaling-stroke"
            pathLength={1}
            strokeDasharray={1}
            style={{
              animation: 'admin-trend-draw 800ms ease-out forwards',
            }}
          />
          {weeklyCounts.map((v, i) => {
            const x = (i / denom) * w;
            const y = h - (v / weeklyMax) * (h - 4) - 2;
            return (
              <circle
                key={i}
                cx={x}
                cy={y}
                r={1.5}
                fill={accentColor}
                vectorEffect="non-scaling-stroke"
              />
            );
          })}
        </svg>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center" style={{ gap: 'var(--admin-space-md)', marginTop: 'var(--admin-space-xs)' }}>
        <span style={{ fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-micro)', color: 'var(--admin-text-tertiary)', fontVariantNumeric: 'tabular-nums' }}>
          <span style={{ color: accentColor, fontWeight: 'var(--admin-weight-semibold)' }}>{activeProducts}</span>{' '}
          live
        </span>
        <span style={{ fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-micro)', color: 'var(--admin-text-tertiary)', fontVariantNumeric: 'tabular-nums' }}>
          <span style={{ color: accentColor, fontWeight: 'var(--admin-weight-semibold)' }}>{inactiveProducts}</span>{' '}
          hidden
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/hooks/useAdminActions.ts =====
import type { AdminRow, Order, OrderStatus } from '../types/admin.types';

type Args = {
  hideProduct: (product: AdminRow) => Promise<boolean>;
  deleteProduct: (product: AdminRow) => Promise<boolean>;
  reactivateProduct: (product: AdminRow) => Promise<boolean>;
  changeOrderStatus: (order: Order, status: OrderStatus) => Promise<boolean>;
  deleteOrder: (order: Order) => Promise<boolean>;
  loadActivities: () => Promise<void>;
  setters: {
    setConfirmDelete: (v: AdminRow | null) => void;
    setConfirmPermanentDelete: (v: AdminRow | null) => void;
    setIsHidingProduct: (v: boolean) => void;
    isHidingProductRef: { current: boolean };
    setIsDeletingProduct: (v: boolean) => void;
    isDeletingProductRef: { current: boolean };
    setViewingProduct: (v: AdminRow | null) => void;
    setViewingOrder: (v: Order | null) => void;
    setConfirmDeleteOrder: (v: Order | null) => void;
    setCurrentPage: (v: number) => void;
    setActiveTab: (v: string) => void;
  };
};

export function useAdminActions({
  hideProduct,
  deleteProduct,
  reactivateProduct,
  changeOrderStatus,
  deleteOrder,
  loadActivities,
  setters,
}: Args) {
  async function handleHideProduct(product: AdminRow) {
    if (setters.isHidingProductRef.current) return;
    setters.isHidingProductRef.current = true;
    setters.setIsHidingProduct(true);
    try {
      const success = await hideProduct(product);
      if (success) {
        setters.setConfirmDelete(null);
        setters.setViewingProduct(null);
        void loadActivities();
      }
    } finally {
      setters.isHidingProductRef.current = false;
      setters.setIsHidingProduct(false);
    }
  }

  async function handlePermanentDeleteProduct(product: AdminRow) {
    if (setters.isDeletingProductRef.current) return;
    setters.isDeletingProductRef.current = true;
    setters.setIsDeletingProduct(true);
    try {
      const success = await deleteProduct(product);
      if (success) {
        setters.setConfirmPermanentDelete(null);
        setters.setViewingProduct(null);
        void loadActivities();
      }
    } finally {
      setters.isDeletingProductRef.current = false;
      setters.setIsDeletingProduct(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleReactivateProduct(product: AdminRow) {
    const success = await reactivateProduct(product);
    if (success) {
      setters.setViewingProduct(null);
      void loadActivities();
    }
  }

  async function handleOrderStatusChange(order: Order, newStatus: OrderStatus) {
    const success = await changeOrderStatus(order, newStatus);
    if (success) {
      setters.setViewingOrder(null);
      void loadActivities();
    }
  }

  async function handleDeleteOrder(order: Order) {
    const success = await deleteOrder(order);
    if (success) {
      setters.setConfirmDeleteOrder(null);
      setters.setViewingOrder(null);
      void loadActivities();
    }
  }

  function handleOverviewSearch() {
    setters.setCurrentPage(1);
    setters.setActiveTab('products');
  }

  function handleViewOrders() {
    setters.setActiveTab('orders');
  }

  return {
    handleHideProduct,
    handlePermanentDeleteProduct,
    handleReactivateProduct,
    handleOrderStatusChange,
    handleDeleteOrder,
    handleOverviewSearch,
    handleViewOrders,
  };
}

===== src/features/admin/hooks/useAdminActivity.ts =====
import {
  useCallback,
  useLayoutEffect,
  useRef,
  useState,
} from 'react';
import { supabase } from '../../../utils/supabase';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
import { ADMIN_STORAGE_KEYS } from '../constants/admin.constants';
import {
  readAdminSnapshot,
  writeAdminSnapshot,
} from '../utils/adminSnapshot';

export type AdminActivity = {
  id: string;
  event_type: string;
  entity_type: 'product' | 'order' | 'admin';
  entity_id: string | null;
  description: string;
  actor_email: string | null;
  metadata: Record<string, unknown>;
  created_at: string;
};

function isAdminActivities(value: unknown): value is AdminActivity[] {
  return (
    Array.isArray(value) &&
    value.every((entry) => {
      if (!entry || typeof entry !== 'object') return false;

      const item = entry as Record<string, unknown>;

      return (
        typeof item.id === 'string' &&
        typeof item.event_type === 'string' &&
        (
          item.entity_type === 'product' ||
          item.entity_type === 'order' ||
          item.entity_type === 'admin'
        ) &&
        (item.entity_id === null || typeof item.entity_id === 'string') &&
        typeof item.description === 'string' &&
        (item.actor_email === null || typeof item.actor_email === 'string') &&
        !!item.metadata &&
        typeof item.metadata === 'object' &&
        !Array.isArray(item.metadata) &&
        typeof item.created_at === 'string' &&
        Number.isFinite(Date.parse(item.created_at))
      );
    })
  );
}

export function useAdminActivity(
  isAdmin: boolean | null,
  userId: string | null
) {
  const [activities, setActivities] = useState<AdminActivity[]>([]);
  const [isLoadingActivities, setIsLoadingActivities] = useState(false);

  const adminRef = useRef(isAdmin);
  const userIdRef = useRef(userId);
  const generationRef = useRef(0);
  const previousScopeRef = useRef({ isAdmin, userId });

  if (
    previousScopeRef.current.isAdmin !== isAdmin ||
    previousScopeRef.current.userId !== userId
  ) {
    generationRef.current += 1;
    previousScopeRef.current = { isAdmin, userId };
  }

  adminRef.current = isAdmin;
  userIdRef.current = userId;

  const loadActivities = useCallback(async () => {
    if (isAdmin !== true || !userId) return;

    const requestGeneration = generationRef.current;
    const requestUserId = userId;

    const isCurrentRequest = () =>
      requestGeneration === generationRef.current &&
      adminRef.current === true &&
      userIdRef.current === requestUserId;

    try {
      const {
        data: { session },
        error: sessionError,
      } = await supabase.auth.getSession();

      if (
        sessionError ||
        !session?.access_token ||
        session.user.id !== requestUserId
      ) {
        throw new Error(
          'Your admin session has expired. Please sign in again.'
        );
      }

      const response = await fetch('/api/activity', {
        headers: {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${session.access_token}`,
          Accept: 'application/json',
        },
      });

      const data = await response.json();

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(data.error || 'Unable to load activities.');
      }

      if (!isAdminActivities(data.activities)) {
        throw new Error('The activities response has an invalid format.');
      }

      if (!isCurrentRequest()) return;

      setActivities(data.activities);
      writeAdminSnapshot(
        ADMIN_STORAGE_KEYS.activitiesCache,
        requestUserId,
        data.activities
      );
    } catch (error) {
      if (isCurrentRequest()) {
        toast.error(
          error instanceof Error
            ? error.message
            : 'Unable to load activities.'
        );
      }
    } finally {
      if (isCurrentRequest()) {
        setIsLoadingActivities(false);
      }
    }
  }, [isAdmin, userId]);

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (isAdmin !== true || !userId) {
      setActivities([]);
      setIsLoadingActivities(false);
      return;
    }

    const cachedActivities = readAdminSnapshot(
      ADMIN_STORAGE_KEYS.activitiesCache,
      userId,
      isAdminActivities
    );

    if (cachedActivities) {
      setActivities(cachedActivities);
      setIsLoadingActivities(false);
      return;
    }

    setActivities([]);
    setIsLoadingActivities(true);
    void loadActivities();
  }, [isAdmin, userId, loadActivities]);

  return {
    activities,
    isLoadingActivities,
    loadActivities,
  };
}

===== src/features/admin/hooks/useAdminAuth.ts =====
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { Session } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import { supabase } from '../../../utils/supabase';
import { ADMIN_STORAGE_KEYS } from '../constants/admin.constants';

type AdminContext = {
  id: string;
  email: string | null;
  role: 'super_admin' | 'store_admin';
  storeId: string | null;
  storeSlug: string | null;
  storeCreatedAt: Partial<Record<'yotaabo' | 'poshies', string>>;
};

type UseAdminAuthResult = {
  session: Session | null;
  admin: AdminContext | null;
  isAdmin: boolean | null;
  isChecking: boolean;
  isLoggingIn: boolean;
  loginError: string | null;
  authError: string | null;
  clearLoginError: () => void;
  retryAuthorization: () => void;
  login: (email: string, password: string) => Promise<boolean>;
  logout: () => Promise<void>;
};

function clearAdminStorage() {
  try {
    const storageKeys = Object.values(ADMIN_STORAGE_KEYS);

    for (let index = sessionStorage.length - 1; index >= 0; index--) {
      const storedKey = sessionStorage.key(index);

      if (
        storedKey &&
        storageKeys.some(
          (key) => storedKey === key || storedKey.startsWith(`${key}:`),
        )
      ) {
        sessionStorage.removeItem(storedKey);
      }
    }
  } catch {
    // Storage may be unavailable.
  }
}

// The session_id is used only to deduplicate client-side checks.
// It is never used as proof of authentication or authorization.
function getSessionIdentity(session: Session): string {
  try {
    const payload = session.access_token.split('.')[1];

    if (payload) {
      const claims = JSON.parse(
        window.atob(payload.replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/')),
      );

      if (typeof claims.session_id === 'string') {
        return `${session.user.id}:${claims.session_id}`;
      }
    }
  } catch {
    // Fall back to the authenticated user identity.
  }

  return session.user.id;
}

async function updateAppSession(
  currentSession: Session,
  action: 'heartbeat' | 'logout',
  signal?: AbortSignal,
) {
  const response = await fetch('/api/admin-session', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {
      Authorization: `Bearer ${currentSession.access_token}`,
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    },
    body: JSON.stringify({ action }),
    cache: 'no-store',
    signal,
  });

  if (!response.ok) {
    const payload = await response.json().catch(() => null);
    throw new Error(
      payload?.error || 'Unable to update administrator session.',
    );
  }
}

export function useAdminAuth(): UseAdminAuthResult {
  const [session, setSession] = useState<Session | null>(null);
  const [admin, setAdmin] = useState<AdminContext | null>(null);
  const [isAdmin, setIsAdmin] = useState<boolean | null>(null);
  const [isChecking, setIsChecking] = useState(true);
  const [isLoggingIn, setIsLoggingIn] = useState(false);
  const [loginError, setLoginError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [authError, setAuthError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const lastUserIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
  const authorizedUserIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
  const verifiedIdentityRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
  const inFlightIdentityRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
  const requestVersionRef = useRef(0);
  const requestControllerRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);
  const retryAuthorizationRef = useRef<() => void>(() => {});

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    async function checkAdmin(currentSession: Session) {
      if (!active) return;

      const identity = getSessionIdentity(currentSession);
      const userId = currentSession.user.id;

      // A previously verified session does not need another round trip.
      if (
        verifiedIdentityRef.current === identity &&
        authorizedUserIdRef.current === userId
      ) {
        setIsChecking(false);
        setAuthError(null);
        return;
      }

      // Prevent duplicate checks for the same session.
      if (inFlightIdentityRef.current === identity) return;

      // Invalidate and cancel any older request.
      requestVersionRef.current += 1;
      const requestVersion = requestVersionRef.current;

      requestControllerRef.current?.abort();
      requestControllerRef.current = null;
      inFlightIdentityRef.current = identity;

      // A different user must not inherit the previous user's admin context.
      if (
        authorizedUserIdRef.current !== null &&
        authorizedUserIdRef.current !== userId
      ) {
        authorizedUserIdRef.current = null;
        verifiedIdentityRef.current = null;
        setAdmin(null);
        setIsAdmin(null);
        clearAdminStorage();
      }

      setIsChecking(true);
      setAuthError(null);

      let lastError: unknown = null;

      try {
        // Two bounded attempts. A hung request cannot hold the UI indefinitely.
        for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 2; attempt++) {
          const controller = new AbortController();
          requestControllerRef.current = controller;

          const timeout = window.setTimeout(
            () => controller.abort(),
            10_000,
          );

          try {
            const response = await fetch('/api/admin-context', {
              method: 'GET',
              headers: {
                Authorization: `Bearer ${currentSession.access_token}`,
              },
              cache: 'no-store',
              signal: controller.signal,
            });

            if (!active || requestVersion !== requestVersionRef.current) {
              return;
            }

            if (response.status === 401 || response.status === 403) {
              clearAdminStorage();
              authorizedUserIdRef.current = null;
              verifiedIdentityRef.current = null;
              setAdmin(null);
              setIsAdmin(false);
              setAuthError(null);
              setIsChecking(false);
              return;
            }

            if (!response.ok) {
              throw new Error(
                `Admin verification failed with HTTP ${response.status}.`,
              );
            }

            const payload = await response.json();
            const verifiedAdmin = payload?.admin;

            const validAdmin =
              Boolean(verifiedAdmin?.id) &&
              ['super_admin', 'store_admin'].includes(verifiedAdmin.role) &&
              (
                verifiedAdmin.role !== 'store_admin' ||
                Boolean(verifiedAdmin.storeId && verifiedAdmin.storeSlug)
              ) &&
              (
                verifiedAdmin.role !== 'super_admin' ||
                verifiedAdmin.storeId === null
              );

            if (!validAdmin) {
              clearAdminStorage();
              authorizedUserIdRef.current = null;
              verifiedIdentityRef.current = null;
              setAdmin(null);
              setIsAdmin(false);
              setAuthError(null);
              setIsChecking(false);
              return;
            }

            if (!active || requestVersion !== requestVersionRef.current) {
              return;
            }

            setAdmin({
              ...verifiedAdmin,
              storeCreatedAt:
                payload?.storeCreatedAt &&
                typeof payload.storeCreatedAt === 'object'
                  ? payload.storeCreatedAt
                  : {},
            });

            authorizedUserIdRef.current = userId;
            verifiedIdentityRef.current = identity;

            setIsAdmin(true);
            setAuthError(null);
            setIsChecking(false);
            return;
          } catch (error) {
            if (!active || requestVersion !== requestVersionRef.current) {
              return;
            }

            lastError = error;

            if (attempt === 0) {
              await new Promise((resolve) =>
                window.setTimeout(resolve, 400),
              );
            }
          } finally {
            window.clearTimeout(timeout);

            if (requestControllerRef.current === controller) {
              requestControllerRef.current = null;
            }
          }
        }

        if (!active || requestVersion !== requestVersionRef.current) {
          return;
        }

        // A temporary service failure is not proof that access was revoked.
        setAuthError(
          lastError instanceof Error
            ? lastError.name === 'AbortError'
              ? 'Admin verification timed out. Check your connection and retry.'
              : lastError.message
            : 'Unable to verify admin access. Please try again.',
        );
        setIsChecking(false);
      } finally {
        if (inFlightIdentityRef.current === identity) {
          inFlightIdentityRef.current = null;
        }
      }
    }

    const { data: subscription } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange(
      (event, nextSession) => {
        if (!active) return;

        const nextUserId = nextSession?.user.id ?? null;
        const previousUserId = lastUserIdRef.current;

        lastUserIdRef.current = nextUserId;
        setSession(nextSession);

        if (!nextSession || event === 'SIGNED_OUT') {
          requestVersionRef.current += 1;
          requestControllerRef.current?.abort();
          requestControllerRef.current = null;
          inFlightIdentityRef.current = null;
          authorizedUserIdRef.current = null;
          verifiedIdentityRef.current = null;

          clearAdminStorage();
          setAdmin(null);
          setIsAdmin(false);
          setIsChecking(false);
          setAuthError(null);
          return;
        }

        if (event === 'PASSWORD_RECOVERY') {
          requestVersionRef.current += 1;
          requestControllerRef.current?.abort();
          requestControllerRef.current = null;
          inFlightIdentityRef.current = null;
          authorizedUserIdRef.current = null;
          verifiedIdentityRef.current = null;

          clearAdminStorage();
          setAdmin(null);
          setIsAdmin(false);
          setIsChecking(false);
          setAuthError(null);
          return;
        }

        // Refreshing an access token does not change the user's admin role.
        // Keep the new token in state without repeating authorization.
        if (event === 'TOKEN_REFRESHED') {
          return;
        }

        const relevantEvent = [
          'INITIAL_SESSION',
          'SIGNED_IN',
          'USER_UPDATED',
        ].includes(event);

        if (!relevantEvent) return;

        const identity = getSessionIdentity(nextSession);

        // Supabase can emit SIGNED_IN again for an existing session.
        if (
          verifiedIdentityRef.current === identity &&
          authorizedUserIdRef.current === nextUserId
        ) {
          setIsChecking(false);
          setAuthError(null);
          return;
        }

        if (inFlightIdentityRef.current === identity) return;

        if (previousUserId !== null && previousUserId !== nextUserId) {
          clearAdminStorage();
          authorizedUserIdRef.current = null;
          verifiedIdentityRef.current = null;
          setAdmin(null);
          setIsAdmin(null);
        }

        void checkAdmin(nextSession);
      },
    );

    retryAuthorizationRef.current = () => {
      const currentSession = lastUserIdRef.current
        ? sessionRef.current
        : null;

      if (currentSession) {
        void checkAdmin(currentSession);
      }
    };

    return () => {
      active = false;
      requestVersionRef.current += 1;
      requestControllerRef.current?.abort();
      requestControllerRef.current = null;
      retryAuthorizationRef.current = () => {};
      subscription.subscription.unsubscribe();
    };
  }, []);

  // Keep the latest session available to the retry callback without
  // recreating the Supabase auth subscription.
  const sessionRef = useRef<Session | null>(null);
  sessionRef.current = session;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!session || !admin || !isAdmin) return;

    const heartbeat = window.setInterval(() => {
      void updateAppSession(session, 'heartbeat').catch((error) => {
        console.error('Admin session heartbeat failed:', error);
      });
    }, 60_000);

    return () => window.clearInterval(heartbeat);
  }, [session, admin, isAdmin]);

  async function login(email: string, password: string) {
    setLoginError(null);
    setAuthError(null);
    setIsLoggingIn(true);

    try {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({
        email,
        password,
      });

      if (error) {
        setLoginError(
          error.message.toLowerCase().includes('invalid login')
            ? 'The email or password you entered is incorrect.'
            : error.message,
        );
        return false;
      }

      return Boolean(data.session);
    } catch (error) {
      setLoginError(
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : 'Unable to sign in. Please try again.',
      );
      return false;
    } finally {
      setIsLoggingIn(false);
    }
  }

  async function logout() {
    const currentSession = session;

    // Application-session logging is best effort. It must never prevent
    // the user's Supabase session from being cleared.
    if (currentSession) {
      const controller = new AbortController();
      const timeout = window.setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 2_500);

      try {
        await updateAppSession(currentSession, 'logout', controller.signal);
      } catch (error) {
        console.warn('Application session logout could not be recorded:', error);
      } finally {
        window.clearTimeout(timeout);
      }
    }

    const { error } = await supabase.auth.signOut({ scope: 'local' });

    if (error) throw error;

    requestVersionRef.current += 1;
    requestControllerRef.current?.abort();
    requestControllerRef.current = null;
    inFlightIdentityRef.current = null;
    authorizedUserIdRef.current = null;
    verifiedIdentityRef.current = null;

    clearAdminStorage();
    setSession(null);
    setAdmin(null);
    setIsAdmin(false);
    setIsChecking(false);
    setAuthError(null);
  }

  return {
    session,
    admin,
    isAdmin,
    isChecking,
    isLoggingIn,
    loginError,
    authError,
    clearLoginError: () => setLoginError(null),
    retryAuthorization: () => retryAuthorizationRef.current(),
    login,
    logout,
  };
}

===== src/features/admin/hooks/useAdminDashboardState.ts =====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import type { Tab, CategoryFilter, OrderFilter, Storefront, TimeRange } from '../types/admin.types';

export function useAdminDashboardState(
  adminRole: string | undefined,
  adminStoreSlug: string | undefined,
) {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<Tab>('overview');
  const [storefrontFilter, setStorefrontFilter] = useState<'all' | Storefront>('all');
  const [categoryFilter, setCategoryFilter] = useState<CategoryFilter>('All');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [notificationsOpen, setNotificationsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [overviewMonth, setOverviewMonth] = useState<string | undefined>('Nov');
  const [timeRange, setTimeRange] = useState<TimeRange>('this-year');
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1);

  const [orderFilter, setOrderFilter] = useState<OrderFilter>('All');
  const [orderSearch, setOrderSearch] = useState('');
  const [orderPage, setOrderPage] = useState(1);
  const [viewingOrder, setViewingOrder] = useState<import('../types/admin.types').Order | null>(null);
  const [confirmDeleteOrder, setConfirmDeleteOrder] = useState<import('../types/admin.types').Order | null>(null);

  const ITEMS_PER_PAGE = 20;
  const ORDERS_PER_PAGE = 20;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (adminRole === 'store_admin' && adminStoreSlug) {
      setStorefrontFilter(adminStoreSlug as Storefront);
    } else if (adminRole === 'super_admin') {
      setStorefrontFilter('all');
    }
  }, [adminRole, adminStoreSlug]);

  useEffect(() => {
    window.scrollTo(0, 0);
  }, [activeTab]);

  return {
    activeTab, setActiveTab,
    storefrontFilter, setStorefrontFilter,
    categoryFilter, setCategoryFilter,
    searchQuery, setSearchQuery,
    notificationsOpen, setNotificationsOpen,
    overviewMonth, setOverviewMonth,
    timeRange, setTimeRange,
    currentPage, setCurrentPage,
    orderFilter, setOrderFilter,
    orderSearch, setOrderSearch,
    orderPage, setOrderPage,
    viewingOrder, setViewingOrder,
    confirmDeleteOrder, setConfirmDeleteOrder,
    ITEMS_PER_PAGE,
    ORDERS_PER_PAGE,
  };
}

===== src/features/admin/hooks/useAdminDerivedData.ts =====
import { useMemo } from 'react';
import type { AdminRow, Order, CategoryFilter, OrderFilter } from '../types/admin.types';
import { inTimeRange } from '../utils/timeRange';
import type { TimeRange } from '../types/admin.types';

type Args = {
  products: AdminRow[];
  orders: Order[];
  storefrontFilter: 'all' | string;
  categoryFilter: CategoryFilter;
  searchQuery: string;
  currentPage: number;
  orderFilter: OrderFilter;
  orderSearch: string;
  orderPage: number;
  timeRange: TimeRange;
  ITEMS_PER_PAGE: number;
  ORDERS_PER_PAGE: number;
};

export function useAdminDerivedData({
  products,
  orders,
  storefrontFilter,
  categoryFilter,
  searchQuery,
  currentPage,
  orderFilter,
  orderSearch,
  orderPage,
  timeRange,
  ITEMS_PER_PAGE,
  ORDERS_PER_PAGE,
}: Args) {
  const storefrontProducts = useMemo(
    () => storefrontFilter === 'all' ? products : products.filter((p) => p.storefront === storefrontFilter),
    [products, storefrontFilter],
  );

  const storefrontOrders = useMemo(
    () => storefrontFilter === 'all' ? orders : orders.filter((o) => o.storefront === storefrontFilter),
    [orders, storefrontFilter],
  );

  const timeFilteredOrders = useMemo(
    () => storefrontOrders.filter((o) => inTimeRange(o.created_at, timeRange)),
    [storefrontOrders, timeRange],
  );

  const timeFilteredProducts = useMemo(
    () => storefrontProducts.filter((p) => inTimeRange(p.created_at, timeRange)),
    [storefrontProducts, timeRange],
  );

  const filteredProducts = useMemo(() => {
    return storefrontProducts
      .filter((product) => {
        if (categoryFilter === 'Hidden') {
          if (product.is_active) return false;
        } else if (categoryFilter !== 'All' && product.category !== categoryFilter) {
          return false;
        }
        if (searchQuery.trim() && !product.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase())) {
          return false;
        }
        return true;
      })
      .sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
  }, [storefrontProducts, categoryFilter, searchQuery]);

  const totalPages = Math.ceil(filteredProducts.length / ITEMS_PER_PAGE);

  const paginatedProducts = useMemo(() => {
    const start = (currentPage - 1) * ITEMS_PER_PAGE;
    return filteredProducts.slice(start, start + ITEMS_PER_PAGE);
  }, [filteredProducts, currentPage, ITEMS_PER_PAGE]);

  const filteredOrders = useMemo(() => {
    return storefrontOrders.filter((order) => {
      if (orderFilter !== 'All' && order.status !== orderFilter.toLowerCase()) return false;
      if (orderSearch.trim()) {
        const query = orderSearch.toLowerCase();
        const matches =
          order.order_number.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
          order.customer_name.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
          order.customer_phone.includes(orderSearch);
        if (!matches) return false;
      }
      return true;
    });
  }, [storefrontOrders, orderFilter, orderSearch]);

  const totalOrderPages = Math.ceil(filteredOrders.length / ORDERS_PER_PAGE);

  const paginatedOrders = useMemo(() => {
    const start = (orderPage - 1) * ORDERS_PER_PAGE;
    return filteredOrders.slice(start, start + ORDERS_PER_PAGE);
  }, [filteredOrders, orderPage, ORDERS_PER_PAGE]);

  const stats = useMemo(
    () => ({
      total: storefrontProducts.length,
      active: storefrontProducts.filter((p) => p.is_active).length,
      hidden: storefrontProducts.filter((p) => !p.is_active).length,
    }),
    [storefrontProducts],
  );

  const orderStats = useMemo(
    () => ({
      total: storefrontOrders.length,
      pending: storefrontOrders.filter((o) => o.status === 'pending').length,
      confirmed: storefrontOrders.filter((o) => o.status === 'confirmed').length,
      delivered: storefrontOrders.filter((o) => o.status === 'delivered').length,
      cancelled: storefrontOrders.filter((o) => o.status === 'cancelled').length,
    }),
    [storefrontOrders],
  );

  return {
    storefrontProducts,
    storefrontOrders,
    timeFilteredOrders,
    timeFilteredProducts,
    filteredProducts,
    paginatedProducts,
    totalPages,
    filteredOrders,
    paginatedOrders,
    totalOrderPages,
    stats,
    orderStats,
  };
}

===== src/features/admin/hooks/useAdminOrders.ts =====
import { useCallback, useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { supabase } from '../../../utils/supabase';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
import type {
  Order,
  OrderStatus,
} from '../types/admin.types';
import { ADMIN_STORAGE_KEYS } from '../constants/admin.constants';
import {
  readAdminSnapshot,
  writeAdminSnapshot,
} from '../utils/adminSnapshot';

function normalizeOrders(value: unknown): unknown {
  // Preserve API data exactly. Invalid storefronts must be rejected,
  // never silently assigned to another storefront.
  return value;
}

function isOrders(value: unknown): value is Order[] {
  const validStatuses: OrderStatus[] = [
    'pending',
    'confirmed',
    'delivered',
    'cancelled',
  ];

  return (
    Array.isArray(value) &&
    value.every((order) => {
      if (!order || typeof order !== 'object') return false;

      const item = order as Record<string, unknown>;

      return (
        typeof item.id === 'string' &&
        item.id.trim().length > 0 &&
        (item.storefront === 'yotaabo' || item.storefront === 'poshies') &&
        typeof item.order_number === 'string' &&
        item.order_number.trim().length > 0 &&
        typeof item.customer_name === 'string' &&
        typeof item.customer_phone === 'string' &&
        Array.isArray(item.items) &&
        item.items.every((orderItem: unknown) => {
          if (!orderItem || typeof orderItem !== 'object') return false;

          const line = orderItem as Record<string, unknown>;

          return (
            (
              line.product_id === null ||
              (
                typeof line.product_id === 'string' &&
                line.product_id.trim().length > 0
              )
            ) &&
            typeof line.name === 'string' &&
            line.name.trim().length > 0 &&
            typeof line.price === 'number' &&
            Number.isFinite(line.price) &&
            line.price >= 0 &&
            typeof line.qty === 'number' &&
            Number.isInteger(line.qty) &&
            line.qty > 0
          );
        }) &&
        typeof item.subtotal === 'number' &&
        Number.isFinite(item.subtotal) &&
        item.subtotal >= 0 &&
        validStatuses.includes(item.status as OrderStatus) &&
        typeof item.created_at === 'string' &&
        Number.isFinite(Date.parse(item.created_at)) &&
        typeof item.updated_at === 'string' &&
        Number.isFinite(Date.parse(item.updated_at))
      );
    })
  );
}

export function useAdminOrders(
  isAdmin: boolean | null,
  userId: string | null
) {
  const adminRef = useRef(isAdmin);
  const userIdRef = useRef(userId);
  const adminGenerationRef = useRef(0);
  const previousScopeRef = useRef({ isAdmin, userId });

  if (
    previousScopeRef.current.isAdmin !== isAdmin ||
    previousScopeRef.current.userId !== userId
  ) {
    adminGenerationRef.current += 1;
    previousScopeRef.current = { isAdmin, userId };
  }

  adminRef.current = isAdmin;
  userIdRef.current = userId;

  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<Order[]>([]);
  const [isLoadingOrders, setIsLoadingOrders] = useState(true);
  const [hasLoadedOrders, setHasLoadedOrders] = useState(false);
  const [ordersLoadError, setOrdersLoadError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const ordersApi = useCallback(
    async (
      method: 'GET' | 'PATCH' | 'DELETE',
      body?: Record<string, string>
    ) => {
      const {
        data: { session: currentSession },
        error: sessionError,
      } = await supabase.auth.getSession();

      if (
        sessionError ||
        !currentSession?.access_token ||
        currentSession.user.id !== userId
      ) {
        throw new Error(
          'Your admin session has expired. Please sign in again.'
        );
      }

      const response = await fetch('/api/orders', {
        method,
        headers: {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${currentSession.access_token}`,
          Accept: 'application/json',
          ...(body ? { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } : {}),
        },
        ...(body ? { body: JSON.stringify(body) } : {}),
      });

      const data = await response.json();

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(data.error || 'The order request failed.');
      }

      return data;
    },
    [userId]
  );

  const loadOrders = useCallback(async () => {
    if (isAdmin !== true || !userId) return;

    const requestGeneration = adminGenerationRef.current;
    const requestUserId = userId;

    const isCurrentRequest = () =>
      requestGeneration === adminGenerationRef.current &&
      adminRef.current === true &&
      userIdRef.current === requestUserId;

    setOrdersLoadError(null);

    try {
      const data = await ordersApi('GET');
      const latestOrders = normalizeOrders(data.orders ?? []);

      if (!isOrders(latestOrders)) {
        throw new Error('The orders response has an invalid format.');
      }

      if (!isCurrentRequest()) return;

      setOrders(latestOrders);

      writeAdminSnapshot(
        ADMIN_STORAGE_KEYS.ordersCache,
        requestUserId,
        latestOrders
      );
    } catch (error) {
      if (isCurrentRequest()) {
        const message =
          error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unable to load orders.';

        setOrdersLoadError(message);
        toast.error(message);
      }
    } finally {
      if (isCurrentRequest()) {
        setIsLoadingOrders(false);
        setHasLoadedOrders(true);
      }
    }
  }, [isAdmin, ordersApi, userId]);

  const changeOrderStatus = useCallback(
    async (order: Order, newStatus: OrderStatus): Promise<boolean> => {
      try {
        await ordersApi('PATCH', {
          id: order.id,
          status: newStatus,
        });

        if (adminRef.current && userIdRef.current === userId) {
          toast.success(`Order ${order.order_number} → ${newStatus}`);
          await loadOrders();
        }

        return true;
      } catch (error) {
        if (adminRef.current && userIdRef.current === userId) {
          toast.error(
            error instanceof Error
              ? error.message
              : 'Unable to update order.'
          );
        }

        return false;
      }
    },
    [loadOrders, ordersApi, userId]
  );

  const deleteOrder = useCallback(
    async (order: Order): Promise<boolean> => {
      try {
        await ordersApi('DELETE', { id: order.id });

        if (adminRef.current && userIdRef.current === userId) {
          toast.success(`Order ${order.order_number} deleted`);
          await loadOrders();
        }

        return true;
      } catch (error) {
        if (adminRef.current && userIdRef.current === userId) {
          toast.error(
            error instanceof Error
              ? error.message
              : 'Unable to delete order.'
          );
        }

        return false;
      }
    },
    [loadOrders, ordersApi, userId]
  );

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (isAdmin !== true || !userId) {
      setOrders([]);
      setIsLoadingOrders(false);
      setHasLoadedOrders(false);
      setOrdersLoadError(null);
      return;
    }

    const cachedOrders = readAdminSnapshot(
      ADMIN_STORAGE_KEYS.ordersCache,
      userId,
      (value): value is unknown[] =>
        Array.isArray(value) && isOrders(normalizeOrders(value))
    );

    if (cachedOrders) {
      setOrders(normalizeOrders(cachedOrders) as Order[]);
      setIsLoadingOrders(false);
      setHasLoadedOrders(true);
      return;
    }

    setOrders([]);
    setIsLoadingOrders(true);
    setHasLoadedOrders(false);
    void loadOrders();
  }, [isAdmin, userId, loadOrders]);

  return {
    orders,
    isLoadingOrders,
    hasLoadedOrders,
    ordersLoadError,
    loadOrders,
    changeOrderStatus,
    deleteOrder,
  };
}

===== src/features/admin/hooks/useAdminProductFormState.ts =====
import { useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { AdminProduct } from '../components/AdminProductForm';
import type { AdminRow } from '../types/admin.types';

export function useAdminProductFormState(isAdmin: boolean | null, userId: string | null | undefined) {
  const formUserId = isAdmin === true ? userId ?? null : null;

  const [formOpen, setFormOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<AdminProduct | null>(null);
  const [formStateReady, setFormStateReady] = useState(false);
  const restoredFormScopeRef = useRef<string | null>(null);

  const [confirmDelete, setConfirmDelete] = useState<AdminRow | null>(null);
  const [confirmPermanentDelete, setConfirmPermanentDelete] = useState<AdminRow | null>(null);
  const [isHidingProduct, setIsHidingProduct] = useState(false);
  const isHidingProductRef = useRef(false);
  const [isDeletingProduct, setIsDeletingProduct] = useState(false);
  const isDeletingProductRef = useRef(false);
  const [viewingProduct, setViewingProduct] = useState<AdminRow | null>(null);

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (!formUserId) {
      restoredFormScopeRef.current = null;
      setFormOpen(false);
      setEditing(null);
      setFormStateReady(false);
      setConfirmDelete(null);
      setConfirmPermanentDelete(null);
      setIsDeletingProduct(false);
      setViewingProduct(null);
      return;
    }

    setFormStateReady(false);
    try {
      const formOpenKey = `yotaabo-admin-product-form-open:${formUserId}`;
      const editingKey = `yotaabo-admin-product-editing:${formUserId}`;
      const savedOpen = sessionStorage.getItem(formOpenKey) === 'true';
      const savedEditing = sessionStorage.getItem(editingKey);
      let restoredEditing: AdminProduct | null = null;
      if (savedEditing) {
        try {
          restoredEditing = JSON.parse(savedEditing) as AdminProduct;
        } catch {
          sessionStorage.removeItem(editingKey);
        }
      }
      setFormOpen(savedOpen);
      setEditing(restoredEditing);
    } catch {
      setFormOpen(false);
      setEditing(null);
    }
    restoredFormScopeRef.current = formUserId;
    setFormStateReady(true);
  }, [formUserId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!formUserId || !formStateReady || restoredFormScopeRef.current !== formUserId) return;
    try {
      const formOpenKey = `yotaabo-admin-product-form-open:${formUserId}`;
      const editingKey = `yotaabo-admin-product-editing:${formUserId}`;
      if (formOpen) {
        sessionStorage.setItem(formOpenKey, 'true');
        if (editing) sessionStorage.setItem(editingKey, JSON.stringify(editing));
        else sessionStorage.removeItem(editingKey);
      } else {
        sessionStorage.removeItem(formOpenKey);
        sessionStorage.removeItem(editingKey);
      }
    } catch {
      // Storage may be unavailable; the admin form remains usable.
    }
  }, [formUserId, formStateReady, formOpen, editing]);

  return {
    formOpen,
    setFormOpen,
    editing,
    setEditing,
    confirmDelete,
    setConfirmDelete,
    confirmPermanentDelete,
    setConfirmPermanentDelete,
    isHidingProduct,
    setIsHidingProduct,
    isHidingProductRef,
    isDeletingProduct,
    setIsDeletingProduct,
    isDeletingProductRef,
    viewingProduct,
    setViewingProduct,
  };
}

===== src/features/admin/hooks/useAdminProducts.ts =====
import { useCallback, useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { supabase } from '../../../utils/supabase';
import { invalidateProductsCache } from '../../../hooks/useProducts';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
import { ADMIN_STORAGE_KEYS } from '../constants/admin.constants';
import {
  readAdminSnapshot,
  writeAdminSnapshot,
} from '../utils/adminSnapshot';
import type { AdminRow } from '../types/admin.types';

function getProductStoragePath(imageUrl: string | null | undefined) {
  if (!imageUrl) return null;

  try {
    const url = new URL(imageUrl);
    const marker = '/storage/v1/object/public/products/';
    const index = url.pathname.indexOf(marker);

    if (index === -1) return null;

    return decodeURIComponent(
      url.pathname.slice(index + marker.length)
    );
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function normalizeAdminRows(value: unknown): unknown {
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return value;

  return value.map((product) => {
    if (!product || typeof product !== 'object') return product;

    const item = product as Record<string, unknown>;

    const supplierCost =
      item.supplier_cost == null ||
      (typeof item.supplier_cost === 'string' &&
        item.supplier_cost.trim() === '')
        ? null
        : typeof item.supplier_cost === 'string'
          ? Number(item.supplier_cost)
          : item.supplier_cost;

    return {
      ...item,
      storefront: item.storefront === 'poshies' ? 'poshies' : 'yotaabo',
      price:
        typeof item.price === 'string' && item.price.trim() !== ''
          ? Number(item.price)
          : item.price,
      supplier_cost: supplierCost,
      sort_order:
        typeof item.sort_order === 'string' && item.sort_order.trim() !== ''
          ? Number(item.sort_order)
          : item.sort_order,
    };
  });
}

function isAdminRows(value: unknown): value is AdminRow[] {
  return (
    Array.isArray(value) &&
    value.every((product) => {
      if (!product || typeof product !== 'object') return false;

      const item = product as Record<string, unknown>;

      return (
        typeof item.id === 'string' &&
        (item.storefront === 'yotaabo' || item.storefront === 'poshies') &&
        typeof item.name === 'string' &&
        typeof item.price === 'number' &&
        (item.supplier_cost === undefined ||
          item.supplier_cost === null ||
          (typeof item.supplier_cost === 'number' &&
            Number.isFinite(item.supplier_cost) &&
            item.supplier_cost >= 0)) &&
        ['His', 'Hers', 'Spaces', 'Lounge'].includes(String(item.category)) &&
        typeof item.subcategory === 'string' &&
        (item.description === null || typeof item.description === 'string') &&
        (item.image_url === null || typeof item.image_url === 'string') &&
        typeof item.is_active === 'boolean' &&
        typeof item.sort_order === 'number' &&
        typeof item.created_at === 'string'
      );
    })
  );
}

export function useAdminProducts(
  isAdmin: boolean | null,
  userId: string | null
) {
  const adminRef = useRef(isAdmin);
  const userIdRef = useRef(userId);
  const adminGenerationRef = useRef(0);
  const loadRequestVersionRef = useRef(0);
  const productsRef = useRef<AdminRow[]>([]);
  const previousScopeRef = useRef({ isAdmin, userId });

  if (
    previousScopeRef.current.isAdmin !== isAdmin ||
    previousScopeRef.current.userId !== userId
  ) {
    adminGenerationRef.current += 1;
    loadRequestVersionRef.current += 1;
    previousScopeRef.current = { isAdmin, userId };
  }

  adminRef.current = isAdmin;
  userIdRef.current = userId;

  const [products, setProducts] = useState<AdminRow[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [hasLoadedProducts, setHasLoadedProducts] = useState(false);
  const [productsLoadError, setProductsLoadError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const updateProducts = useCallback((nextProducts: AdminRow[]) => {
    productsRef.current = nextProducts;
    setProducts(nextProducts);
  }, []);

  const productsApi = useCallback(
    async (
      method: 'GET' | 'PATCH' | 'DELETE',
      body?: Record<string, string | boolean>,
      adminScope = false
    ) => {
      const {
        data: { session: currentSession },
        error: sessionError,
      } = await supabase.auth.getSession();

      if (
        sessionError ||
        !currentSession?.access_token ||
        currentSession.user.id !== userId
      ) {
        throw new Error(
          'Your admin session has expired. Please sign in again.'
        );
      }

      const url = adminScope
        ? '/api/products?scope=admin'
        : '/api/products';

      const response = await fetch(url, {
        method,
        headers: {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${currentSession.access_token}`,
          Accept: 'application/json',
          ...(body ? { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } : {}),
        },
        ...(body ? { body: JSON.stringify(body) } : {}),
      });

      const data = await response.json();

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(data.error || 'The product request failed.');
      }

      return data;
    },
    [userId]
  );

  const loadProducts = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!isAdmin || !userId) return;

    const requestGeneration = adminGenerationRef.current;
    const requestVersion = ++loadRequestVersionRef.current;
    const requestUserId = userId;

    const isCurrentRequest = () =>
      requestGeneration === adminGenerationRef.current &&
      requestVersion === loadRequestVersionRef.current &&
      adminRef.current === true &&
      userIdRef.current === requestUserId;

    setProductsLoadError(null);

    try {
      const data = await productsApi('GET', undefined, true);
      const latestProducts = normalizeAdminRows(data.products ?? []);

      if (!isAdminRows(latestProducts)) {
        throw new Error('The products response has an invalid format.');
      }

      if (!isCurrentRequest()) return;

      updateProducts(latestProducts);

      writeAdminSnapshot(
        ADMIN_STORAGE_KEYS.productsCache,
        requestUserId,
        latestProducts
      );
    } catch (error) {
      if (isCurrentRequest()) {
        const message =
          error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unable to load products.';

        setProductsLoadError(message);
        toast.error(message);
      }
    } finally {
      if (isCurrentRequest()) {
        setIsLoading(false);
        setHasLoadedProducts(true);
      }
    }
  }, [isAdmin, productsApi, updateProducts, userId]);

  const upsertProduct = useCallback(
    (savedProduct: AdminRow) => {
      if (!adminRef.current || !userId || userIdRef.current !== userId) {
        console.error(
          '[AdminProducts] Ignored product upsert because the admin scope changed.'
        );
        return;
      }

      const normalized = normalizeAdminRows([savedProduct]);

      if (!isAdminRows(normalized) || normalized.length !== 1) {
        console.error(
          '[AdminProducts] Ignored product upsert because the saved product response is invalid.'
        );
        return;
      }

      const canonicalProduct = normalized[0];

      // Invalidate any GET request started before this successful save.
      loadRequestVersionRef.current += 1;

      const currentProducts = productsRef.current;
      const existingIndex = currentProducts.findIndex(
        (product) => product.id === canonicalProduct.id
      );

      let nextProducts: AdminRow[];

      if (existingIndex >= 0) {
        nextProducts = [...currentProducts];
        nextProducts[existingIndex] = canonicalProduct;
      } else {
        nextProducts = [canonicalProduct, ...currentProducts];
      }

      updateProducts(nextProducts);
      invalidateProductsCache();

      if (hasLoadedProducts) {
        writeAdminSnapshot(
          ADMIN_STORAGE_KEYS.productsCache,
          userId,
          nextProducts
        );
      } else {
        // The list has not been hydrated yet. Fetch the complete canonical list.
        void loadProducts();
      }

      console.info('[AdminProducts] Product collection reconciled.', {
        productId: canonicalProduct.id,
        operation: existingIndex >= 0 ? 'update' : 'insert',
        collectionSize: nextProducts.length,
      });
    },
    [hasLoadedProducts, loadProducts, updateProducts, userId]
  );

  const hideProduct = useCallback(
    async (product: AdminRow): Promise<boolean> => {
      try {
        await productsApi('PATCH', {
          action: 'set-status',
          id: product.id,
          is_active: false,
        });

        invalidateProductsCache();

        if (adminRef.current && userIdRef.current === userId) {
          toast.success(`${product.name} hidden`);
          await loadProducts();
        }

        return true;
      } catch (error) {
        if (adminRef.current && userIdRef.current === userId) {
          toast.error(
            error instanceof Error
              ? error.message
              : 'Unable to hide product.'
          );
        }

        return false;
      }
    },
    [loadProducts, productsApi, userId]
  );

  const reactivateProduct = useCallback(
    async (product: AdminRow): Promise<boolean> => {
      try {
        await productsApi('PATCH', {
          action: 'set-status',
          id: product.id,
          is_active: true,
        });

        invalidateProductsCache();

        if (adminRef.current && userIdRef.current === userId) {
          toast.success(`${product.name} reactivated`);
          await loadProducts();
        }

        return true;
      } catch (error) {
        if (adminRef.current && userIdRef.current === userId) {
          toast.error(
            error instanceof Error
              ? error.message
              : 'Unable to reactivate product.'
          );
        }

        return false;
      }
    },
    [loadProducts, productsApi, userId]
  );

  const deleteProduct = useCallback(
    async (product: AdminRow): Promise<boolean> => {
      try {
        const data = await productsApi('DELETE', { id: product.id });

        invalidateProductsCache();

        if (adminRef.current && userIdRef.current === userId) {
          toast.success(`${product.name} deleted`);

          const imagePath = getProductStoragePath(
            data.deleted?.image_url ?? product.image_url
          );

          if (imagePath) {
            void supabase.storage
              .from('products')
              .remove([imagePath])
              .then(({ error: storageError }) => {
                if (storageError) {
                  console.error(
                    'Deleted product image could not be removed:',
                    storageError
                  );
                  toast.error(
                    'Product deleted, but its image could not be removed.'
                  );
                }
              })
              .catch((error) => {
                console.error(
                  'Deleted product image cleanup failed:',
                  error
                );
                toast.error(
                  'Product deleted, but its image could not be removed.'
                );
              });
          }

          void loadProducts().catch((error) => {
            console.error('Product list refresh failed:', error);
          });
        }

        return true;
      } catch (error) {
        if (adminRef.current && userIdRef.current === userId) {
          toast.error(
            error instanceof Error
              ? error.message
              : 'Unable to delete product.'
          );
        }

        return false;
      }
    },
    [loadProducts, productsApi, userId]
  );

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (isAdmin !== true || !userId) {
      updateProducts([]);
      setIsLoading(false);
      setHasLoadedProducts(false);
      setProductsLoadError(null);
      return;
    }

    const cachedProducts = readAdminSnapshot(
      ADMIN_STORAGE_KEYS.productsCache,
      userId,
      (value): value is unknown[] =>
        Array.isArray(value) && isAdminRows(normalizeAdminRows(value))
    );

    if (cachedProducts) {
      const normalizedProducts = normalizeAdminRows(cachedProducts) as AdminRow[];

      updateProducts(normalizedProducts);
      setIsLoading(false);
      setHasLoadedProducts(true);
      return;
    }

    updateProducts([]);
    setIsLoading(true);
    setHasLoadedProducts(false);
    void loadProducts();
  }, [isAdmin, userId, loadProducts, updateProducts]);

  return {
    products,
    isLoading,
    hasLoadedProducts,
    productsLoadError,
    loadProducts,
    upsertProduct,
    hideProduct,
    reactivateProduct,
    deleteProduct,
  };
}

===== src/features/admin/hooks/useAdminTheme.ts =====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

export type AdminTheme = 'light' | 'dark';

export function useAdminTheme() {
  const [adminTheme, setAdminTheme] = useState<AdminTheme>(() => {
    try {
      return localStorage.getItem('simatu-admin-theme') === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light';
    } catch {
      return 'light';
    }
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem('simatu-admin-theme', adminTheme);
    } catch {
      // Theme remains usable when browser storage is unavailable.
    }
  }, [adminTheme]);

  const toggleTheme = () =>
    setAdminTheme((current) => (current === 'light' ? 'dark' : 'light'));

  return { adminTheme, setAdminTheme, toggleTheme };
}

===== src/features/admin/hooks/useRevenueSeries.ts =====
import { useMemo } from 'react';
import type { Order, AdminRow } from '../types/admin.types';
import type { RevenueSeries } from '../components/RevenueChart';
import { getCategoryTheme } from '../../../utils/categoryTheme';

type Args = {
  orders: Order[];
  products: AdminRow[];
  activeFilter: string;
};

export function useRevenueSeries({ orders, products, activeFilter }: Args): RevenueSeries[] {
  return useMemo(() => {
    const currentMonthIndex = new Date().getMonth();
    const elapsedMonths = currentMonthIndex + 1;

    // Build a name → category lookup per storefront
    const categoryByName = new Map<string, string>();
    for (const p of products) {
      const storefront = (p as { storefront?: string }).storefront;
      const key = `${storefront ?? ''}::${p.name}`;
      if (p.category) categoryByName.set(key, p.category);
    }

    const resolveCategory = (storefront: string, itemName: string): string | null =>
      categoryByName.get(`${storefront}::${itemName}`) ?? null;

    const computeMonthlyCounts = (
      storefrontPredicate: (storefront: string) => boolean,
      categoryPredicate: (category: string | null) => boolean,
    ): (number | null)[] => {
      const counts: (number | null)[] = [];
      for (let m = 0; m < 12; m++) {
        if (m > currentMonthIndex) {
          counts.push(null);
          continue;
        }
        let total = 0;
        for (const o of orders) {
          if (o.status === 'cancelled') continue;
          if (!storefrontPredicate(o.storefront)) continue;
          if (new Date(o.created_at).getMonth() !== m) continue;
          for (const item of o.items) {
            const cat = resolveCategory(o.storefront, item.name);
            if (categoryPredicate(cat)) total += 1;
          }
        }
        counts.push(total);
      }
      return counts;
    };

    const averageOf = (data: (number | null)[]): (number | null)[] => {
      const nums = data.slice(0, elapsedMonths).filter((v): v is number => v !== null);
      if (nums.length === 0) return data.map(() => null);
      const avg = nums.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / nums.length;
      const rounded = Math.round(avg * 10) / 10;
      return data.map((_, idx) => (idx <= currentMonthIndex ? rounded : null));
    };

    const his = getCategoryTheme('His');
    const hers = getCategoryTheme('Hers');
    const spaces = getCategoryTheme('Spaces');
    const lounge = getCategoryTheme('Lounge');

    if (activeFilter === 'yotaabo') {
      const isYotaabo = (s: string) => s === 'yotaabo';
      const hisData = computeMonthlyCounts(isYotaabo, (c) => c === 'His');
      const hersData = computeMonthlyCounts(isYotaabo, (c) => c === 'Hers');
      const spacesData = computeMonthlyCounts(isYotaabo, (c) => c === 'Spaces');
      const combined = hisData.map((v, i) => {
        const a = v ?? 0;
        const b = hersData[i] ?? 0;
        const c = spacesData[i] ?? 0;
        return v === null && hersData[i] === null && spacesData[i] === null ? null : a + b + c;
      });
      return [
        { key: 'his', label: 'His', color: his.hexColor, data: hisData },
        { key: 'hers', label: 'Hers', color: hers.hexColor, data: hersData },
        { key: 'spaces', label: 'Spaces', color: spaces.hexColor, data: spacesData },
        { key: 'avg', label: 'Average', color: 'var(--admin-accent)', data: averageOf(combined), dashed: true },
      ];
    }

    if (activeFilter === 'poshies') {
      const isPoshies = (s: string) => s === 'poshies';
      const hisData = computeMonthlyCounts(isPoshies, (c) => c === 'His');
      const hersData = computeMonthlyCounts(isPoshies, (c) => c === 'Hers');
      const loungeData = computeMonthlyCounts(isPoshies, (c) => c === 'Lounge');
      const combined = hisData.map((v, i) => {
        const a = v ?? 0;
        const b = hersData[i] ?? 0;
        const c = loungeData[i] ?? 0;
        return v === null && hersData[i] === null && loungeData[i] === null ? null : a + b + c;
      });
      return [
        { key: 'his', label: 'His', color: his.hexColor, data: hisData },
        { key: 'hers', label: 'Hers', color: hers.hexColor, data: hersData },
        { key: 'lounge', label: 'Lounge', color: lounge.hexColor, data: loungeData },
        { key: 'avg', label: 'Average', color: 'var(--admin-accent)', data: averageOf(combined), dashed: true },
      ];
    }

    const yotaaboData = computeMonthlyCounts((s) => s === 'yotaabo', () => true);
    const poshiesData = computeMonthlyCounts((s) => s === 'poshies', () => true);
    const combined = yotaaboData.map((v, i) => {
      const a = v ?? 0;
      const b = poshiesData[i] ?? 0;
      return v === null && poshiesData[i] === null ? null : a + b;
    });
    return [
      { key: 'yotaabo', label: 'Yotaabo', color: his.hexColor, data: yotaaboData },
      { key: 'poshies', label: 'Poshies', color: hers.hexColor, data: poshiesData },
      { key: 'avg', label: 'Average', color: 'var(--admin-accent)', data: averageOf(combined), dashed: true },
    ];
  }, [orders, products, activeFilter]);
}

===== src/features/admin/hooks/useStoreRequests.ts =====
import { useState } from 'react';
import type {
  StoreRequest,
  StoreRequestCategory,
  StoreRequestPriority,
  Storefront,
} from '../settings/categories/types';

type NewRequestInput = {
  storefront: Storefront;
  title: string;
  category: StoreRequestCategory;
  description: string;
  priority: StoreRequestPriority;
};

/**
 * Store requests — general tickets from store admins to the technical team.
 * Currently UI-only state. When backend lands, replace with API calls:
 *   - loadRequests(): GET /api/store-requests
 *   - addRequest(): POST /api/store-requests
 *   - updateRequestStatus(): PATCH /api/store-requests
 */
export function useStoreRequests() {
  const [requests, setRequests] = useState<StoreRequest[]>([]);
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const addRequest = (input: NewRequestInput) => {
    setError('');
    setNotice('');

    if (!input.title.trim() || !input.description.trim()) {
      setError('Title and description are required.');
      return false;
    }

    const newRequest: StoreRequest = {
      id: typeof crypto !== 'undefined' && 'randomUUID' in crypto
        ? crypto.randomUUID()
        : `req-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`,
      storefront: input.storefront,
      title: input.title.trim(),
      category: input.category,
      description: input.description.trim(),
      priority: input.priority,
      status: 'open',
      created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    };

    setRequests((prev) => [newRequest, ...prev]);
    setNotice('Request submitted');
    return true;
  };

  return { requests, addRequest, notice, error };
}

===== src/features/admin/settings/categories/CategoryForm.tsx =====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { FiPlus, FiX, FiEye, FiEyeOff, FiTrash2 } from 'react-icons/fi';
import Modal from '../../../../components/Modal';
import AccentButton from '../../components/AccentButton';
import AdminDropdown from '../../components/AdminDropdown';
import type { Category, Editor, Subcategory } from './types';

type NewSubcategory = { tempId: string; name: string };
type ExistingSubcategory = { tempId: string; id: string; name: string; originalName: string; isActive: boolean; originalIsActive: boolean; markedForDeletion: boolean };

type CategoryFormProps = {
  editor: Editor;
  category?: Category | null;
  totalPositions: number;
  initialSortOrder: number;
  isSaving: boolean;
  onSubmit: (data: {
    name: string;
    sort_order: number;
    note: string;
    newSubcategories: NewSubcategory[];
    existingSubcategories: ExistingSubcategory[];
  }) => Promise<void>;
  onCancel: () => void;
};

const INPUT_STYLE = {
  width: '100%',
  paddingInline: 'var(--admin-space-sm)',
  paddingBlock: 'var(--admin-space-xs)',
  borderRadius: 'var(--ds-radius-lg)',
  borderWidth: '1px',
  borderStyle: 'solid',
  borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-strong)',
  backgroundColor: 'var(--admin-surface-subtle)',
  color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
  fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-body)',
  outline: 'none',
  transition: 'border-color 150ms ease, box-shadow 150ms ease',
} as const;

const LABEL_STYLE = {
  fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-micro)',
  fontWeight: 'var(--admin-weight-medium)',
  color: 'var(--admin-text-secondary)',
  display: 'block',
  marginBottom: 'var(--admin-space-xxs)',
} as const;

export default function CategoryForm({
  editor,
  category,
  totalPositions,
  initialSortOrder,
  isSaving,
  onSubmit,
  onCancel,
}: CategoryFormProps) {
  const isNew = editor.kind === 'new-category';
  const isSubcategoryEditor = editor.kind === 'new-subcategory' || editor.kind === 'edit-subcategory';

  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [note, setNote] = useState('');
  const [sortOrder, setSortOrder] = useState(editor.kind === 'new-category' ? '' : String(initialSortOrder));
  const [newSubcategories, setNewSubcategories] = useState<NewSubcategory[]>([]);
  const [existingSubcategories, setExistingSubcategories] = useState<ExistingSubcategory[]>([]);

  // For subcategory editing — single name field only
  const [singleSubName, setSingleSubName] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isNew) {
      setName('');
      setNote('');
      setSortOrder('');
      setNewSubcategories([]);
      setExistingSubcategories([]);
    } else if (editor.kind === 'edit-category' && category) {
      setName(category.name);
      setNote('');
      setSortOrder(String(category.sort_order));
      setExistingSubcategories(
        category.subcategories.map((s) => ({
          tempId: s.id,
          id: s.id,
          name: s.name,
          originalName: s.name,
          isActive: s.is_active,
          originalIsActive: s.is_active,
          markedForDeletion: false,
        }))
      );
      setNewSubcategories([]);
    } else if (isSubcategoryEditor && category) {
      const editingId = editor.kind === 'edit-subcategory' ? editor.id : null;
      const existing = category.subcategories.find((s) => s.id === editingId);
      setSingleSubName(existing?.name ?? '');
    }
  }, [editor, category, initialSortOrder, isNew, isSubcategoryEditor]);

  const addSubcategoryRow = () => {
    setNewSubcategories((prev) => [...prev, { tempId: crypto.randomUUID(), name: '' }]);
  };

  const updateNewSub = (tempId: string, value: string) => {
    setNewSubcategories((prev) =>
      prev.map((s) => (s.tempId === tempId ? { ...s, name: value } : s))
    );
  };

  const removeNewSub = (tempId: string) => {
    setNewSubcategories((prev) => prev.filter((s) => s.tempId !== tempId));
  };

  const updateExistingSub = (tempId: string, value: string) => {
    setExistingSubcategories((prev) =>
      prev.map((s) => (s.tempId === tempId ? { ...s, name: value } : s))
    );
  };

  const toggleExistingSubStatus = (tempId: string) => {
    setExistingSubcategories((prev) =>
      prev.map((s) => (s.tempId === tempId ? { ...s, isActive: !s.isActive } : s))
    );
  };

  const markExistingSubForDeletion = (tempId: string) => {
    setExistingSubcategories((prev) =>
      prev.map((s) => (s.tempId === tempId ? { ...s, markedForDeletion: !s.markedForDeletion } : s))
    );
  };

  const handleSubmit = async () => {
    if (isSubcategoryEditor) {
      // single-name submit
      if (!singleSubName.trim()) return;
      await onSubmit({
        name: singleSubName.trim(),
        sort_order: initialSortOrder,
        note: '',
        newSubcategories: [],
        existingSubcategories: [],
      });
      return;
    }

    if (!name.trim()) return;

    await onSubmit({
      name: name.trim(),
      // If new category and user didn't pick an order → append at bottom
      sort_order: sortOrder ? Number(sortOrder) : totalPositions,
      note: note.trim(),
      newSubcategories: newSubcategories.filter((s) => s.name.trim()),
      existingSubcategories: existingSubcategories.map((s) => ({
        tempId: s.tempId,
        id: s.id,
        name: s.name,
        originalName: s.originalName,
        isActive: s.isActive,
        originalIsActive: s.originalIsActive,
        markedForDeletion: s.markedForDeletion,
      })),
    });
  };

  const title = isNew
    ? 'New category request'
    : editor.kind === 'edit-category'
      ? `Edit ${category?.name ?? 'category'}`
      : editor.kind === 'new-subcategory'
        ? 'New subcategory'
        : 'Edit subcategory';

  return (
    <Modal isOpen onClose={isSaving ? () => {} : onCancel} maxWidth="max-w-lg">
      <div style={{ padding: 'var(--admin-space-lg)' }}>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between" style={{ marginBottom: 'var(--admin-space-md)' }}>
          <h3 style={{ fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-heading-3)', fontWeight: 'var(--admin-weight-semibold)', color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)', margin: 0 }}>
            {title}
          </h3>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onCancel}
            aria-label="Close"
            style={{ width: '28px', height: '28px', borderRadius: '50%', border: 'none', background: 'transparent', cursor: 'pointer', color: 'var(--admin-text-tertiary)', display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}
          >
            <FiX size={16} aria-hidden="true" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Current state block — only for edit-category */}
        {editor.kind === 'edit-category' && category && (
          <div
            style={{
              padding: 'var(--admin-space-sm)',
              marginBottom: 'var(--admin-space-md)',
              borderRadius: 'var(--ds-radius-lg)',
              borderWidth: '1px',
              borderStyle: 'dashed',
              borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-strong)',
              backgroundColor: 'var(--admin-surface-subtle)',
            }}
          >
            <div style={{ fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-micro)', fontWeight: 'var(--admin-weight-semibold)', color: 'var(--admin-text-secondary)', marginBottom: 'var(--admin-space-xxs)', textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.08em' }}>
              Currently on storefront
            </div>
            <div style={{ fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-body)', color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)', fontWeight: 'var(--admin-weight-medium)' }}>
              {category.name}
            </div>
            <div style={{ fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-caption)', color: 'var(--admin-text-tertiary)', marginTop: '2px' }}>
              {category.subcategories.length} subcategor{category.subcategories.length === 1 ? 'y' : 'ies'} · Order {category.sort_order}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Name field (hidden for subcategory editors) */}
        {!isSubcategoryEditor && (
          <div style={{ marginBottom: 'var(--admin-space-md)' }}>
            <label style={LABEL_STYLE}>Category name</label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={name}
              onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="e.g., His"
              required
              maxLength={60}
              style={INPUT_STYLE}
              onFocus={(e) => {
                e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = 'var(--admin-focus)';
                e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = '0 0 0 2px color-mix(in srgb, var(--admin-focus) 20%, transparent)';
              }}
              onBlur={(e) => {
                e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = 'var(--admin-border-strong)';
                e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = 'none';
              }}
            />
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Subcategory editor — single name field */}
        {isSubcategoryEditor && (
          <div style={{ marginBottom: 'var(--admin-space-md)' }}>
            <label style={LABEL_STYLE}>Subcategory name</label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={singleSubName}
              onChange={(e) => setSingleSubName(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="e.g., Amber Oud"
