              required
              maxLength={60}
              style={INPUT_STYLE}
              autoFocus
            />
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Category-level fields (not for subcategory editors) */}
        {!isSubcategoryEditor && (
          <>
            {/* Existing subcategories — only for edit-category */}
            {editor.kind === 'edit-category' && existingSubcategories.length > 0 && (
              <div style={{ marginBottom: 'var(--admin-space-md)' }}>
                <label style={LABEL_STYLE}>Existing subcategories</label>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 'var(--admin-space-xs)' }}>
                  {existingSubcategories.map((sub) => {
                    const isMarked = sub.markedForDeletion;
                    return (
                      <div
                        key={sub.tempId}
                        style={{
                          display: 'flex',
                          gap: 'var(--admin-space-xs)',
                          alignItems: 'center',
                          opacity: isMarked ? 0.4 : 1,
                          textDecoration: isMarked ? 'line-through' : 'none',
                        }}
                      >
                        <input
                          type="text"
                          value={sub.name}
                          onChange={(e) => updateExistingSub(sub.tempId, e.target.value)}
                          disabled={isMarked}
                          style={{ ...INPUT_STYLE, flex: 1 }}
                        />
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => toggleExistingSubStatus(sub.tempId)}
                          disabled={isMarked}
                          aria-label={sub.isActive ? 'Hide subcategory' : 'Show subcategory'}
                          title={sub.isActive ? 'Visible — click to hide' : 'Hidden — click to show'}
                          style={{
                            width: '36px',
                            height: '36px',
                            borderRadius: 'var(--ds-radius-lg)',
                            border: '1px solid var(--admin-border-strong)',
                            background: 'transparent',
                            cursor: isMarked ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                            color: sub.isActive ? 'var(--admin-status-green)' : 'var(--admin-text-tertiary)',
                            display: 'flex',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            justifyContent: 'center',
                          }}
                        >
                          {sub.isActive ? <FiEye size={14} aria-hidden="true" /> : <FiEyeOff size={14} aria-hidden="true" />}
                        </button>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => markExistingSubForDeletion(sub.tempId)}
                          aria-label={isMarked ? 'Undo delete' : 'Delete subcategory'}
                          title={isMarked ? 'Undo delete' : 'Mark for deletion'}
                          style={{
                            width: '36px',
                            height: '36px',
                            borderRadius: 'var(--ds-radius-lg)',
                            border: '1px solid var(--admin-border-strong)',
                            background: isMarked ? 'var(--admin-status-red)' : 'transparent',
                            cursor: 'pointer',
                            color: isMarked ? 'var(--admin-on-accent)' : 'var(--admin-status-red)',
                            display: 'flex',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            justifyContent: 'center',
                          }}
                        >
                          <FiTrash2 size={14} aria-hidden="true" />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
                <p style={{ fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-micro)', color: 'var(--admin-text-tertiary)', margin: 'var(--admin-space-xs) 0 0', lineHeight: 1.3 }}>
                  Click <FiEye size={10} style={{ display: 'inline', verticalAlign: 'middle' }} /> to toggle visibility. Click <FiTrash2 size={10} style={{ display: 'inline', verticalAlign: 'middle' }} /> to mark for deletion.
                </p>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* New subcategories */}
            {isNew && newSubcategories.length > 0 && (
              <div style={{ marginBottom: 'var(--admin-space-md)' }}>
                <label style={LABEL_STYLE}>Subcategories</label>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 'var(--admin-space-xs)' }}>
                  {newSubcategories.map((sub) => (
                    <div key={sub.tempId} style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 'var(--admin-space-xs)' }}>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={sub.name}
                        onChange={(e) => updateNewSub(sub.tempId, e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="Subcategory name"
                        style={{ ...INPUT_STYLE, flex: 1 }}
                      />
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => removeNewSub(sub.tempId)}
                        aria-label="Remove subcategory"
                        style={{ width: '36px', height: 'auto', borderRadius: 'var(--ds-radius-lg)', border: '1px solid var(--admin-border-strong)', background: 'transparent', cursor: 'pointer', color: 'var(--admin-status-red)', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}
                      >
                        <FiX size={14} aria-hidden="true" />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Add subcategory button */}
            {isNew && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={addSubcategoryRow}
                style={{
                  width: '100%',
                  paddingBlock: 'var(--admin-space-xs)',
                  marginBottom: 'var(--admin-space-md)',
                  borderRadius: 'var(--ds-radius-lg)',
                  borderWidth: '1px',
                  borderStyle: 'dashed',
                  borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-strong)',
                  backgroundColor: 'transparent',
                  color: 'var(--admin-text-secondary)',
                  fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-caption)',
                  fontWeight: 'var(--admin-weight-medium)',
                  cursor: 'pointer',
                  display: 'flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  justifyContent: 'center',
                  gap: 'var(--admin-space-xs)',
                }}
              >
                <FiPlus size={12} aria-hidden="true" />
                Add subcategory
              </button>
            )}

            {/* Position */}
            <div style={{ marginBottom: 'var(--admin-space-md)' }}>
              <label style={LABEL_STYLE}>Display order</label>
              <AdminDropdown
                value={sortOrder}
                onChange={setSortOrder}
                placeholder="Select display order…"
                options={Array.from({ length: totalPositions }, (_, i) => {
                  const pos = i + 1;
                  return {
                    value: String(pos),
                    label: `${pos}${pos === 1 ? ' (top)' : pos === totalPositions ? ' (bottom)' : ''}`,
                  };
                })}
                align="left"
              />
            </div>

            {/* Note to technical team */}
            <div style={{ marginBottom: 'var(--admin-space-md)' }}>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between" style={{ marginBottom: 'var(--admin-space-xxs)' }}>
                <label style={{ ...LABEL_STYLE, marginBottom: 0 }}>Note to technical team</label>
                <span style={{ fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-micro)', color: note.length > 180 ? 'var(--admin-status-amber)' : 'var(--admin-text-tertiary)', fontVariantNumeric: 'tabular-nums' }}>
                  {note.length}/200
                </span>
              </div>
              <textarea
                value={note}
                onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)}
                maxLength={200}
                rows={2}
                placeholder="What would you like us to build on the storefront?"
                style={{ ...INPUT_STYLE, resize: 'vertical', minHeight: '60px', fontFamily: 'inherit' }}
              />
            </div>
          </>
        )}

        {/* Actions */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end', gap: 'var(--admin-space-xs)', marginTop: 'var(--admin-space-md)' }}>
          <AccentButton variant="neutral" shape="pill" onClick={onCancel} ariaLabel="Cancel">
            Cancel
          </AccentButton>
          <AccentButton
            variant="dark"
            shape="pill"
            onClick={() => void handleSubmit()}
            ariaLabel={isSaving ? 'Saving' : 'Request change'}
          >
            {isSaving ? 'Saving…' : 'Request change'}
          </AccentButton>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/settings/categories/CategoryRow.tsx =====
import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { FiEdit2, FiEye, FiEyeOff } from 'react-icons/fi';
import AccentButton from '../../components/AccentButton';
import { getCategoryTheme } from '../../../../utils/categoryTheme';
import type { Category } from './types';
import { CARD_CHROME } from '../../components/shared/cardChrome';
import AdminConfirmModal from '../../components/AdminConfirmModal';

type CategoryRowProps = {
  category: Category;
  onChangeStatus: (kind: 'category' | 'subcategory', id: string, isActive: boolean, categoryId?: string) => Promise<void>;
  onEditCategory: (c: Category) => void;

  onMoveToPosition: (category: Category, position: number) => Promise<void>;
  groupPosition: number;
  groupSize: number;
};

const ICON_BTN_STYLE = {
  width: '32px',
  height: '32px',
  minWidth: '32px',
  padding: 0,
} as const;

const CATEGORY_GRID = 'minmax(0, 1fr) auto';
const SUBCATEGORY_GRID = 'minmax(0, 1fr) auto';

const ROW_STYLE = {
  display: 'grid',
  alignItems: 'center',
  padding: 'var(--admin-space-sm) var(--admin-space-md)',
  gap: 'var(--admin-space-sm)',
} as const;

export default function CategoryRow({
  category,
  onChangeStatus,
  onEditCategory,
  onMoveToPosition,
  groupPosition,
  groupSize,
}: CategoryRowProps) {
  const categoryTheme = getCategoryTheme(category.name);
  const sortCircleColor = categoryTheme?.hexColor ?? 'var(--admin-accent)';

  const [isSortOpen, setIsSortOpen] = useState(false);
  const [pendingToggle, setPendingToggle] = useState<
    | { kind: 'category'; name: string; isActive: boolean; subcategoryCount: number }
    | { kind: 'subcategory'; id: string; categoryId: string; name: string; isActive: boolean }
    | null
  >(null);
  const sortRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isSortOpen) return;
    const mouseHandler = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (sortRef.current && !sortRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setIsSortOpen(false);
      }
    };
    const keyHandler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') setIsSortOpen(false);
    };
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', mouseHandler);
    document.addEventListener('keydown', keyHandler);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('mousedown', mouseHandler);
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', keyHandler);
    };
  }, [isSortOpen]);



  return (
    <div
      className="admin-card-bg overflow-hidden"
      style={{
        ...CARD_CHROME,
        opacity: category.is_active ? 1 : 0.5,
        transition: 'opacity 200ms ease',
      }}
    >
      <div
        className="admin-category-header border-b border-[var(--admin-border-subtle)]"
        style={{
          ...ROW_STYLE,
          gridTemplateColumns: CATEGORY_GRID,
        }}
      >
        <div className="flex items-center" style={{ gap: 'var(--admin-space-sm)', minWidth: 0 }}>
          <span
            style={{
              fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-body)',
              fontWeight: 'var(--admin-weight-medium)',
              color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
              overflow: 'hidden',
              textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
              whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
              textDecoration: category.is_active ? 'none' : 'line-through',
            }}
          >
            {category.name}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-end" style={{ gap: 'var(--admin-space-xs)' }}>
          {/* Sort button — circular with orange position number */}
          <div className="relative" ref={sortRef}>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setIsSortOpen((v) => !v)}
              aria-label={`Change position of ${category.name}`}
              style={{
                width: '32px',
                height: '32px',
                borderRadius: '50%',
                border: '1px solid var(--admin-border-strong)',
                backgroundColor: 'var(--admin-surface)',
                cursor: 'pointer',
                display: 'inline-flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
                padding: 0,
              }}
            >
              <span
                style={{
                  fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-caption)',
                  fontWeight: 'var(--admin-weight-semibold)',
                  fontVariantNumeric: 'tabular-nums',
                  color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
                  lineHeight: 1,
                }}
              >
                {groupPosition}
              </span>
            </button>

            {isSortOpen && (
              <div
                style={{
                  position: 'absolute',
                  top: 'calc(100% + 4px)',
                  right: 0,
                  width: '180px',
                  zIndex: 100,
                  maxHeight: '240px',
                  overflowY: 'auto',
                  backgroundColor: 'var(--admin-surface)',
                  boxShadow: 'var(--admin-shadow)',
                  borderWidth: '1px',
                  borderStyle: 'solid',
                  borderColor: 'var(--admin-border-subtle)',
                  borderRadius: 'var(--ds-radius-lg)',
                }}
              >
                <div
                  style={{
                    padding: '8px 12px',
                    borderBottom: '1px solid var(--admin-border-subtle)',
                  }}
                >
                  <div
                    style={{
                      fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-micro)',
                      fontWeight: 'var(--admin-weight-semibold)',
                      color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
                      lineHeight: 1.2,
                    }}
                  >
                    Sorting
                  </div>
                  <div
                    style={{
                      fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-micro)',
                      color: 'var(--admin-text-tertiary)',
                      lineHeight: 1.3,
                      marginTop: '2px',
                    }}
                  >
                    Determines how this section is positioned on the storefront.
                  </div>
                </div>
                {Array.from({ length: groupSize }, (_, i) => {
                  const position = i + 1;
                  const isCurrent = position === groupPosition;
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={position}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setIsSortOpen(false);
                        if (!isCurrent) void onMoveToPosition(category, position);
                      }}
                      style={{
                        width: '100%',
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        justifyContent: 'space-between',
                        gap: 'var(--admin-space-sm)',
                        padding: '8px 12px',
                        border: 'none',
                        background: 'transparent',
                        color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
                        fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-caption)',
                        textAlign: 'left',
                        cursor: 'pointer',
                        fontVariantNumeric: 'tabular-nums',
                      }}
                      onMouseEnter={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = 'var(--admin-surface-subtle)'; }}
                      onMouseLeave={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = 'transparent'; }}
                    >
                      <span>{position}</span>
                      <span
                        style={{
                          display: 'inline-flex',
                          alignItems: 'center',
                          justifyContent: 'center',
                          width: '14px',
                          height: '14px',
                          borderRadius: '50%',
                          border: isCurrent
                            ? `4px solid ${sortCircleColor}`
                            : '1.5px solid var(--admin-border-strong)',
                          flexShrink: 0,
                          transition: 'border 200ms ease',
                          boxSizing: 'border-box',
                        }}
                      />
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <AccentButton
            variant="neutral"
            shape="circle"
            onClick={() => onEditCategory(category)}
            ariaLabel={`Edit ${category.name}`}
            style={ICON_BTN_STYLE}
          >
            <FiEdit2 size={14} aria-hidden="true" />
          </AccentButton>
          <AccentButton
            variant="neutral"
            shape="circle"
            onClick={() =>
              setPendingToggle({
                kind: 'category',
                name: category.name,
                isActive: category.is_active,
                subcategoryCount: category.subcategories.length,
              })
            }
            ariaLabel={`${category.is_active ? 'Hide' : 'Show'} ${category.name}`}
            style={ICON_BTN_STYLE}
          >
            {category.is_active ? <FiEyeOff size={14} aria-hidden="true" /> : <FiEye size={14} aria-hidden="true" />}
          </AccentButton>
        </div>
      </div>


      {category.subcategories.length > 0 && (
        <ul style={{ listStyle: 'none', margin: 0, padding: 0 }}>
          {category.subcategories.map((sub, index) => {
            return (
              <li
                key={sub.id}
                style={{
                  borderTop:
                    index > 0
                      ? '1px solid var(--admin-border-subtle)'
                      : 'none',
                }}
              >
                <div style={{ ...ROW_STYLE, gridTemplateColumns: SUBCATEGORY_GRID }}>
                  <span
                    style={{
                      fontSize: 'var(--admin-size-body)',
                      color: 'var(--admin-text-primary)',
                      overflow: 'hidden',
                      textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
                      whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                      paddingLeft: 'var(--admin-space-md)',
                    }}
                  >
                    {sub.name}
                  </span>

                  <div className="flex items-center" style={{ gap: 'var(--admin-space-xs)' }}>
                  </div>

                </div>

              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ul>
      )}




      {/* Confirmation modal for hide/show */}
      <AdminConfirmModal
        isOpen={pendingToggle !== null}
        title={
          pendingToggle?.isActive
            ? `Hide ${pendingToggle.kind === 'category' ? 'category' : 'subcategory'}?`
            : `Show ${pendingToggle?.kind === 'category' ? 'category' : 'subcategory'}?`
        }
        message={
          pendingToggle
            ? pendingToggle.isActive
              ? pendingToggle.kind === 'category'
                ? `"${pendingToggle.name}" and its ${pendingToggle.subcategoryCount} subcategor${pendingToggle.subcategoryCount === 1 ? 'y' : 'ies'} will be hidden from the storefront. You can show it again at any time.`
                : `"${pendingToggle.name}" will be hidden from the storefront. You can show it again at any time.`
              : pendingToggle.kind === 'category'
                ? `"${pendingToggle.name}" and its subcategories will be visible on the storefront again.`
                : `"${pendingToggle.name}" will be visible on the storefront again.`
            : ''
        }
        confirmText={pendingToggle?.isActive ? 'Hide' : 'Show'}
        cancelText="Cancel"
        variant="grey"
        onConfirm={() => {
          if (!pendingToggle) return;
          if (pendingToggle.kind === 'category') {
            void onChangeStatus('category', category.id, pendingToggle.isActive);
          } else {
            void onChangeStatus('subcategory', pendingToggle.id, pendingToggle.isActive, pendingToggle.categoryId);
          }
          setPendingToggle(null);
        }}
        onCancel={() => setPendingToggle(null)}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/settings/categories/types.ts =====
export type Storefront = 'yotaabo' | 'poshies';

export type Subcategory = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  slug: string;
  sort_order: number;
  is_active: boolean;
};

export type Category = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  slug: string;
  sort_order: number;
  is_active: boolean;
  description?: string;
  created_at?: string;
  subcategories: Subcategory[];
};

export type Editor =
  | { kind: 'new-category' }
  | { kind: 'new-subcategory'; categoryId: string }
  | { kind: 'edit-category'; id: string }
  | { kind: 'edit-subcategory'; id: string; categoryId: string };

export type CategoriesProps = {
  accessToken: string;
  adminRole: string;
  assignedStorefront: Storefront | null;
};


export type StoreRequestStatus = 'open' | 'in_progress' | 'resolved';
export type StoreRequestCategory = 'bug' | 'feature' | 'content' | 'other';
export type StoreRequestPriority = 'low' | 'medium' | 'high' | 'urgent';

export type StoreRequest = {
  id: string;
  storefront: Storefront;
  title: string;
  category: StoreRequestCategory;
  description: string;
  priority: StoreRequestPriority;
  status: StoreRequestStatus;
  created_at: string;
};

===== src/features/admin/settings/categories/useAdminCategories.ts =====
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import type { FormEvent } from 'react';
import type { Category, Editor, Storefront, Subcategory } from './types';

type Args = {
  accessToken: string;
  adminRole: string;
  assignedStorefront: Storefront | null;
};

export function useAdminCategories({ accessToken, adminRole, assignedStorefront }: Args) {
  const isSuperAdmin = adminRole === 'super_admin';
  const [storefront, setStorefront] = useState<Storefront>(assignedStorefront ?? 'yotaabo');
  const [categories, setCategories] = useState<Category[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState('');
  const [editor, setEditor] = useState<Editor | null>(null);
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [sortOrder, setSortOrder] = useState('0');

  useEffect(() => {
    if (assignedStorefront) setStorefront(assignedStorefront);
  }, [assignedStorefront]);

  const loadCategories = useCallback(async () => {
    setIsLoading(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`/api/categories?storefront=${storefront}`, {
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}` },
      });
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to load categories');
      const data = await response.json();
      setCategories(Array.isArray(data.categories) ? data.categories : []);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to load categories');
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }, [accessToken, storefront]);

  useEffect(() => {
    void loadCategories();
  }, [loadCategories]);

  function openEditor(nextEditor: Editor, currentName = '', currentSort = 0) {
    setEditor(nextEditor);
    setName(currentName);
    setSortOrder(String(currentSort));
    setError('');
    setNotice('');
  }

  function closeEditor() {
    setEditor(null);
    setName('');
    setSortOrder('0');
  }

  async function submitEditor(payload: {
    name: string;
    sort_order: number;
    note: string;
    newSubcategories: { tempId: string; name: string }[];
    existingSubcategories: { tempId: string; id: string; name: string }[];
  }) {
    if (!editor) return;
    setIsSaving(true);
    setError('');
    setNotice('');

    try {
      const headers = {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}`,
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      };

      // For new-subcategory or edit-subcategory editors, we're editing a single subcategory
      if (editor.kind === 'new-subcategory' || editor.kind === 'edit-subcategory') {
        const isEdit = editor.kind === 'edit-subcategory';
        const response = await fetch('/api/categories/subcategories', {
          method: isEdit ? 'PATCH' : 'POST',
          headers,
          body: JSON.stringify({
            ...(isEdit ? { id: editor.id } : {}),
            category_id: editor.categoryId,
            name: payload.name,
          }),
        });
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to save subcategory');
        setNotice('Saved successfully');
        closeEditor();
        await loadCategories();
        return;
      }

      // Category creation or editing
      if (editor.kind === 'new-category') {
        // 1. Create the category (hidden pending super admin)
        const catResponse = await fetch('/api/categories', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers,
          body: JSON.stringify({
            name: payload.name,
            sort_order: payload.sort_order,
            storefront,
            is_active: false,
            note: payload.note,
          }),
        });
        if (!catResponse.ok) throw new Error('Failed to create category');
        const catData = await catResponse.json();
        const newCategoryId = catData.category?.id ?? catData.id;
        if (!newCategoryId) throw new Error('Category created but no ID returned');

        // 2. Create each new subcategory
        for (const sub of payload.newSubcategories) {
          const subResponse = await fetch('/api/categories/subcategories', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers,
            body: JSON.stringify({
              category_id: newCategoryId,
              name: sub.name,
            }),
          });
          if (!subResponse.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to add subcategory "${sub.name}"`);
        }

        setNotice('Request submitted');
        closeEditor();
        await loadCategories();
        return;
      }

      if (editor.kind === 'edit-category') {
        // 1. Update the category name + sort_order
        const catResponse = await fetch('/api/categories', {
          method: 'PATCH',
          headers,
          body: JSON.stringify({
            id: editor.id,
            name: payload.name,
            sort_order: payload.sort_order,
          }),
        });
        if (!catResponse.ok) throw new Error('Failed to update category');

        // 2. Update each existing subcategory (rename)
        const original = categories.find((c) => c.id === editor.id);
        for (const sub of payload.existingSubcategories) {
          const originalSub = original?.subcategories.find((s) => s.id === sub.id);
          if (originalSub && originalSub.name !== sub.name) {
            const subResponse = await fetch('/api/categories/subcategories', {
              method: 'PATCH',
              headers,
              body: JSON.stringify({ id: sub.id, name: sub.name }),
            });
            if (!subResponse.ok) throw new Error('Failed to update subcategory');
          }
        }

        setNotice('Saved successfully');
        closeEditor();
        await loadCategories();
        return;
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to save');
    } finally {
      setIsSaving(false);
    }
  }

  async function changeStatus(
    kind: 'category' | 'subcategory',
    id: string,
    isActive: boolean,
    categoryId?: string,
  ) {
    setError('');
    setNotice('');
    try {
      const url = kind === 'category' ? '/api/categories' : '/api/categories/subcategories';
      const body: Record<string, unknown> = { id, is_active: !isActive };
      if (categoryId) body.category_id = categoryId;
      const response = await fetch(url, {
        method: 'PATCH',
        headers: {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}`,
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        },
        body: JSON.stringify(body),
      });
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to update');
      setNotice('Updated');
      await loadCategories();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to update');
    }
  }

  async function moveCategoryToPosition(category: Category, targetPosition: number) {
    setError('');
    setNotice('');

    // Get categories in the same group (active vs inactive), sorted by sort_order
    const sameGroup = categories
      .filter((c) => c.is_active === category.is_active)
      .sort((a, b) => a.sort_order - b.sort_order);

    const currentIndex = sameGroup.findIndex((c) => c.id === category.id);
    if (currentIndex === -1) return;

    const targetIndex = targetPosition - 1; // 1-based → 0-based
    if (targetIndex === currentIndex) return;
    if (targetIndex < 0 || targetIndex >= sameGroup.length) return;

    try {
      const headers = {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}`,
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      };

      // Reorder the array locally: remove the category and insert at target
      const reordered = [...sameGroup];
      const [moved] = reordered.splice(currentIndex, 1);
      reordered.splice(targetIndex, 0, moved);

      // Now assign sequential sort_order values (starting from the group's min sort_order)
      const baseSortOrder = Math.min(...sameGroup.map((c) => c.sort_order));

      // Send PATCH for every category whose sort_order changed
      for (let i = 0; i < reordered.length; i++) {
        const newSortOrder = baseSortOrder + i;
        if (reordered[i].sort_order !== newSortOrder) {
          const response = await fetch('/api/categories', {
            method: 'PATCH',
            headers,
            body: JSON.stringify({ id: reordered[i].id, sort_order: newSortOrder }),
          });
          if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to reorder');
        }
      }

      setNotice('Order updated');
      await loadCategories();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to reorder');
    }
  }

  function editCategory(category: Category) {
    openEditor({ kind: 'edit-category', id: category.id }, category.name, category.sort_order);
  }

  function editSubcategory(categoryId: string, subcategory: Subcategory) {
    openEditor(
      { kind: 'edit-subcategory', id: subcategory.id, categoryId },
      subcategory.name,
      subcategory.sort_order,
    );
  }

  return {
    isSuperAdmin,
    storefront, setStorefront,
    categories, isLoading, isSaving,
    error, notice,
    editor,
    name, setName,
    sortOrder, setSortOrder,
    openEditor, closeEditor, submitEditor, changeStatus,
    moveCategoryToPosition,
    editCategory, editSubcategory,
  };
}

===== src/features/admin/settings/management/useAdminManagement.ts =====
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
import type { AdminRecord } from '../../components/AdminList';

type UseAdminManagementArgs = {
  accessToken: string;
};

export function useAdminManagement({ accessToken }: UseAdminManagementArgs) {
  const [admins, setAdmins] = useState<AdminRecord[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [isInviting, setIsInviting] = useState(false);
  const [actionLoadingId, setActionLoadingId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [storeSlug, setStoreSlug] = useState<'yotaabo' | 'poshies'>('yotaabo');
  const [loadError, setLoadError] = useState('');

  const loadAdmins = useCallback(async () => {
    setIsLoading(true);
    setLoadError('');
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/admins', {
        method: 'GET',
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}` },
      });
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to load admins');
      const data = await response.json();
      setAdmins(Array.isArray(data.admins) ? data.admins : []);
    } catch (err) {
      setLoadError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to load admins');
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }, [accessToken]);

  useEffect(() => {
    void loadAdmins();
  }, [loadAdmins]);

  const handleInvite = async (e: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsInviting(true);
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/admins/invite', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}`,
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({ email, store_slug: storeSlug }),
      });
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to invite');
      toast.success('Invitation sent');
      setEmail('');
      await loadAdmins();
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to invite');
    } finally {
      setIsInviting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteAdmin = async (admin: AdminRecord) => {
    setActionLoadingId(admin.id);
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`/api/admins?id=${admin.id}`, {
        method: 'DELETE',
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}` },
      });
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to delete admin');
      toast.success('Admin removed');
      await loadAdmins();
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to delete admin');
    } finally {
      setActionLoadingId(null);
    }
  };

  const handleResend = async (admin: AdminRecord) => {
    setActionLoadingId(admin.id);
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/admins/invitations', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}`,
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({ id: admin.id, action: 'resend' }),
      });
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to resend');
      toast.success('Invitation resent');
      await loadAdmins();
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to resend');
    } finally {
      setActionLoadingId(null);
    }
  };

  const handleRevoke = async (admin: AdminRecord) => {
    setActionLoadingId(admin.id);
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/admins/invitations', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}`,
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({ id: admin.id, action: 'revoke' }),
      });
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to revoke');
      toast.success('Invitation revoked');
      await loadAdmins();
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to revoke');
    } finally {
      setActionLoadingId(null);
    }
  };

  return {
    admins, isLoading, isInviting, actionLoadingId,
    email, setEmail,
    storeSlug, setStoreSlug,
    loadError,
    loadAdmins,
    handleInvite,
    handleDeleteAdmin,
    handleResend,
    handleRevoke,
  };
}

===== src/features/admin/utils/adminSnapshot.ts =====
type Snapshot<T> = {
  userId: string;
  savedAt: number;
  data: T;
};

const SNAPSHOT_MAX_AGE_MS = 10 * 60 * 1000;

export function adminSnapshotKey(
  storageKey: string,
  userId: string
): string {
  return `${storageKey}:${userId}`;
}

export function readAdminSnapshot<T>(
  storageKey: string,
  userId: string,
  validate: (value: unknown) => value is T
): T | null {
  const key = adminSnapshotKey(storageKey, userId);

  try {
    const raw = sessionStorage.getItem(key);

    if (!raw) return null;

    const snapshot: unknown = JSON.parse(raw);

    if (!snapshot || typeof snapshot !== 'object') {
      sessionStorage.removeItem(key);
      return null;
    }

    const candidate = snapshot as Partial<Snapshot<unknown>>;

    if (
      typeof candidate.userId !== 'string' ||
      typeof candidate.savedAt !== 'number' ||
      !('data' in candidate)
    ) {
      sessionStorage.removeItem(key);
      return null;
    }

    const age = Date.now() - candidate.savedAt;

    if (
      candidate.userId !== userId ||
      !Number.isFinite(candidate.savedAt) ||
      age < 0 ||
      age > SNAPSHOT_MAX_AGE_MS ||
      !validate(candidate.data)
    ) {
      sessionStorage.removeItem(key);
      return null;
    }

    return candidate.data;
  } catch {
    try {
      sessionStorage.removeItem(key);
    } catch {
      // Storage may be unavailable.
    }

    return null;
  }
}

export function writeAdminSnapshot<T>(
  storageKey: string,
  userId: string,
  data: T
): void {
  try {
    const snapshot: Snapshot<T> = {
      userId,
      savedAt: Date.now(),
      data,
    };

    sessionStorage.setItem(
      adminSnapshotKey(storageKey, userId),
      JSON.stringify(snapshot)
    );
  } catch {
    // The dashboard must remain usable if storage is unavailable.
  }
}

===== src/features/admin/utils/colorMix.ts =====
/**
 * Mix two hex colors at a given weight.
 * Used to compute values that CSS color-mix() can't reliably resolve inside
 * custom properties on all browsers (especially mobile).
 */

function hexToRgb(hex: string): [number, number, number] {
  const clean = hex.replace('#', '');
  const full = clean.length === 3
    ? clean.split('').map((c) => c + c).join('')
    : clean;
  return [
    parseInt(full.slice(0, 2), 16),
    parseInt(full.slice(2, 4), 16),
    parseInt(full.slice(4, 6), 16),
  ];
}

function rgbToHex(r: number, g: number, b: number): string {
  const toHex = (n: number) =>
    Math.round(Math.max(0, Math.min(255, n))).toString(16).padStart(2, '0');
  return `#${toHex(r)}${toHex(g)}${toHex(b)}`;
}

export function mixHex(color1: string, color2: string, weight1: number): string {
  const w1 = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, weight1));
  const w2 = 1 - w1;

  if (!color1.startsWith('#') || !color2.startsWith('#')) {
    // If either isn't hex, return color1 as fallback
    return color1;
  }

  const [r1, g1, b1] = hexToRgb(color1);
  const [r2, g2, b2] = hexToRgb(color2);

  return rgbToHex(
    r1 * w1 + r2 * w2,
    g1 * w1 + g2 * w2,
    b1 * w1 + b2 * w2,
  );
}

===== src/features/admin/utils/overview/currency.ts =====
const zarFormatter = new Intl.NumberFormat('en-ZA', {
  style: 'currency',
  currency: 'ZAR',
  minimumFractionDigits: 2,
  maximumFractionDigits: 2,
});

export function formatOverviewCurrency(value: number): string {
  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) return 'Unavailable';

  return zarFormatter.format(value);
}

export function formatOverviewNumber(value: number): string {
  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) return 'Unavailable';

  return new Intl.NumberFormat('en-ZA').format(value);
}

===== src/features/admin/utils/overview/dateRanges.ts =====
import type {
  OverviewDateSelection,
  OverviewDateRangeValue,
} from '../../types/overview.types';

export const OVERVIEW_TIME_ZONE = 'Africa/Johannesburg';

type LocalDateTime = {
  year: number;
  month: number;
  day: number;
  hour: number;
  minute: number;
  second: number;
};

function getLocalDateParts(date: Date): {
  year: number;
  month: number;
  day: number;
} {
  const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', {
    timeZone: OVERVIEW_TIME_ZONE,
    year: 'numeric',
    month: '2-digit',
    day: '2-digit',
  }).formatToParts(date);

  const values = Object.fromEntries(
    parts.map((part) => [part.type, part.value]),
  );

  return {
    year: Number(values.year),
    month: Number(values.month),
    day: Number(values.day),
  };
}

/**
 * Converts a Johannesburg wall-clock date/time into its UTC timestamp.
 * The iterative offset calculation avoids assuming that local time is UTC.
 */
function localDateTimeToUtc(value: LocalDateTime): Date {
  const targetUtc = Date.UTC(
    value.year,
    value.month - 1,
    value.day,
    value.hour,
    value.minute,
    value.second,
  );

  let timestamp = targetUtc;

  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt += 1) {
    const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', {
      timeZone: OVERVIEW_TIME_ZONE,
      year: 'numeric',
      month: '2-digit',
      day: '2-digit',
      hour: '2-digit',
      minute: '2-digit',
      second: '2-digit',
      hourCycle: 'h23',
    }).formatToParts(new Date(timestamp));

    const values = Object.fromEntries(
      parts.map((part) => [part.type, part.value]),
    );

    const representedUtc = Date.UTC(
      Number(values.year),
      Number(values.month) - 1,
      Number(values.day),
      Number(values.hour),
      Number(values.minute),
      Number(values.second),
    );

    const difference = targetUtc - representedUtc;

    if (difference === 0) break;

    timestamp += difference;
  }

  return new Date(timestamp);
}

function addCalendarDays(date: Date, amount: number): Date {
  const result = new Date(date);
  result.setUTCDate(result.getUTCDate() + amount);
  return result;
}

function getLocalMidnight(parts: {
  year: number;
  month: number;
  day: number;
}): Date {
  return localDateTimeToUtc({
    ...parts,
    hour: 0,
    minute: 0,
    second: 0,
  });
}

function getLocalEndOfDay(parts: {
  year: number;
  month: number;
  day: number;
}): Date {
  return new Date(
    localDateTimeToUtc({
      ...parts,
      hour: 23,
      minute: 59,
      second: 59,
    }).getTime() + 999,
  );
}

export function getOverviewDateRange(
  selection: OverviewDateSelection,
  storeCreatedAt?: string,
  now = new Date(),
): OverviewDateRangeValue {
  const today = getLocalDateParts(now);
  const todayEnd = getLocalEndOfDay(today);
  const createdAt = storeCreatedAt ? new Date(storeCreatedAt) : null;

  if (createdAt && !Number.isFinite(createdAt.getTime())) {
    throw new Error('Invalid store creation date supplied to Overview.');
  }

  let start: Date | null = null;
  let end = todayEnd;

  const makeStart = (year: number, month: number, day: number) =>
    getLocalMidnight({ year, month, day });

  const makeEnd = (year: number, month: number, day: number) =>
    getLocalEndOfDay({ year, month, day });

  if (selection.type === 'preset') {
    switch (selection.preset) {
      case 'today':
        start = makeStart(today.year, today.month, today.day);
        break;

      case 'yesterday': {
        const day = addCalendarDays(
          new Date(Date.UTC(today.year, today.month - 1, today.day)),
          -1,
        );
        start = makeStart(
          day.getUTCFullYear(),
          day.getUTCMonth() + 1,
          day.getUTCDate(),
        );
        end = makeEnd(
          day.getUTCFullYear(),
          day.getUTCMonth() + 1,
          day.getUTCDate(),
        );
        break;
      }

      case 'last-7-days':
      case 'last-30-days': {
        const count = selection.preset === 'last-7-days' ? 7 : 30;
        const first = addCalendarDays(
          new Date(Date.UTC(today.year, today.month - 1, today.day)),
          -(count - 1),
        );
        start = makeStart(
          first.getUTCFullYear(),
          first.getUTCMonth() + 1,
          first.getUTCDate(),
        );
        break;
      }

      case 'this-month':
        start = makeStart(today.year, today.month, 1);
        break;

      case 'last-month': {
        const previousMonth = new Date(Date.UTC(today.year, today.month - 2, 1));
        const year = previousMonth.getUTCFullYear();
        const month = previousMonth.getUTCMonth() + 1;
        const lastDay = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month, 0)).getUTCDate();
        start = makeStart(year, month, 1);
        end = makeEnd(year, month, lastDay);
        break;
      }

      case 'this-year':
        start = makeStart(today.year, 1, 1);
        break;

      case 'last-year':
        start = makeStart(today.year - 1, 1, 1);
        end = makeEnd(today.year - 1, 12, 31);
        break;

      case 'all-time':
        start = createdAt;
        break;
    }
  } else {
    if (selection.dates.length === 0) {
      return {
        start: null,
        end: todayEnd,
      };
    }

    const ranges = [...new Set(selection.dates)]
      .filter((dateKey) => {
        if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(dateKey)) return false;

        const [year, month, day] = dateKey.split('-').map(Number);
        const date = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day));

        return (
          date.getUTCFullYear() === year &&
          date.getUTCMonth() + 1 === month &&
          date.getUTCDate() === day
        );
      })
      .sort()
      .map((dateKey) => {
        const [year, month, day] = dateKey.split('-').map(Number);
        let rangeStart = makeStart(year, month, day);
        let rangeEnd = makeEnd(year, month, day);

        if (rangeEnd.getTime() > todayEnd.getTime()) {
          rangeEnd = todayEnd;
        }

        return { start: rangeStart, end: rangeEnd };
      })
      .filter((range) => range.start.getTime() <= range.end.getTime());

    return {
      start: ranges[0]?.start ?? null,
      end: ranges[ranges.length - 1]?.end ?? todayEnd,
      ranges,
    };
  }

  if (start && createdAt && createdAt.getTime() > start.getTime()) {
    start = createdAt;
  }

  if (end.getTime() > todayEnd.getTime()) {
    end = todayEnd;
  }

  return { start, end };
}

export function getOverviewDateKey(value: string | Date): string {
  const date = value instanceof Date ? value : new Date(value);

  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
    throw new Error('Invalid date supplied to Overview.');
  }

  const { year, month, day } = getLocalDateParts(date);

  return [
    String(year),
    String(month).padStart(2, '0'),
    String(day).padStart(2, '0'),
  ].join('-');
}

export function isWithinOverviewRange(
  value: string,
  range: OverviewDateRangeValue,
): boolean {
  const timestamp = new Date(value).getTime();

  if (!Number.isFinite(timestamp)) {
    return false;
  }

  if (range.ranges) {
    return range.ranges.some(
      (part) =>
        timestamp >= part.start.getTime() &&
        timestamp <= part.end.getTime(),
    );
  }

  if (range.start && timestamp < range.start.getTime()) {
    return false;
  }

  return timestamp <= range.end.getTime();
}

===== src/features/admin/utils/overview/dateSelectionStorage.ts =====
import { ADMIN_STORAGE_KEYS } from '../../constants/admin.constants';
import type {
  OverviewDatePreset,
  OverviewDateSelection,
} from '../../types/overview.types';

const STORAGE_VERSION = 1;

const PRESETS: OverviewDatePreset[] = [
  'today',
  'yesterday',
  'last-7-days',
  'last-30-days',
  'this-month',
  'last-month',
  'this-year',
  'last-year',
  'all-time',
];

function isValidDateKey(value: unknown): value is string {
  if (
    typeof value !== 'string' ||
    !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value)
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  const [year, month, day] = value.split('-').map(Number);
  const date = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day));

  return (
    date.getUTCFullYear() === year &&
    date.getUTCMonth() === month - 1 &&
    date.getUTCDate() === day
  );
}

function isValidSelection(
  value: unknown
): value is OverviewDateSelection {
  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object') return false;

  const selection = value as Record<string, unknown>;

  if (selection.type === 'dates') {
    return (
      Array.isArray(selection.dates) &&
      selection.dates.every(isValidDateKey)
    );
  }

  return (
    selection.type === 'preset' &&
    typeof selection.preset === 'string' &&
    PRESETS.includes(selection.preset as OverviewDatePreset)
  );
}

function storageKey(userId: string): string {
  return `${ADMIN_STORAGE_KEYS.overviewDateFilter}:${userId}`;
}

export function readOverviewDateSelection(
  userId: string
): OverviewDateSelection {
  const emptySelection: OverviewDateSelection = {
    type: 'dates',
    dates: [],
  };

  if (!userId) return emptySelection;

  try {
    const raw = sessionStorage.getItem(storageKey(userId));

    if (!raw) return emptySelection;

    const saved: unknown = JSON.parse(raw);

    if (!saved || typeof saved !== 'object') {
      sessionStorage.removeItem(storageKey(userId));
      return emptySelection;
    }

    const record = saved as Record<string, unknown>;

    if (
      record.version !== STORAGE_VERSION ||
      record.userId !== userId ||
      !isValidSelection(record.selection)
    ) {
      sessionStorage.removeItem(storageKey(userId));
      return emptySelection;
    }

    if (record.selection.type === 'dates') {
      return {
        type: 'dates',
        dates: [...new Set(record.selection.dates)].sort(),
      };
    }

    return record.selection;
  } catch {
    return emptySelection;
  }
}

export function writeOverviewDateSelection(
  userId: string,
  selection: OverviewDateSelection
): void {
  if (!userId || !isValidSelection(selection)) return;

  try {
    const normalized: OverviewDateSelection =
      selection.type === 'dates'
        ? {
            type: 'dates',
            dates: [...new Set(selection.dates)].sort(),
          }
        : selection;

    sessionStorage.setItem(
      storageKey(userId),
      JSON.stringify({
        version: STORAGE_VERSION,
        userId,
        selection: normalized,
      })
    );
  } catch {
    // The Overview remains usable if browser storage is unavailable.
  }
}

===== src/features/admin/utils/overview/quickActions.ts =====
import { getOverviewDateKey } from './dateRanges';

function getDateKeysBetween(start: Date, end: Date): string[] {
  const dates: string[] = [];

  for (
    const date = new Date(start);
    date.getTime() <= end.getTime();
    date.setUTCDate(date.getUTCDate() + 1)
  ) {
    dates.push(date.toISOString().slice(0, 10));
  }

  return dates;
}

/**
 * Generates exact inclusive Johannesburg calendar dates for an Overview
 * quick action. All periods are calculated relative to the supplied instant.
 */
export function getOverviewQuickActionDates(
  action: string,
  now = new Date(),
): string[] {
  const currentDateKey = getOverviewDateKey(now);
  const currentYear = Number(currentDateKey.slice(0, 4));
  const currentMonth = Number(currentDateKey.slice(5, 7));

  let start: Date;
  let end: Date;

  switch (action) {
    case '7-days':
      end = new Date(`${currentDateKey}T00:00:00.000Z`);
      start = new Date(end);
      start.setUTCDate(start.getUTCDate() - 6);
      break;

    case 'month':
      start = new Date(Date.UTC(currentYear, currentMonth - 2, 1));
      end = new Date(Date.UTC(currentYear, currentMonth - 1, 0));
      break;

    case '3-months':
      start = new Date(Date.UTC(currentYear, currentMonth - 4, 1));
      end = new Date(Date.UTC(currentYear, currentMonth - 1, 0));
      break;

    case 'quarter': {
      const currentQuarterStart = Math.floor((currentMonth - 1) / 3) * 3;
      start = new Date(Date.UTC(currentYear, currentQuarterStart - 3, 1));
      end = new Date(Date.UTC(currentYear, currentQuarterStart, 0));
      break;
    }

    case '6-months':
      start = new Date(Date.UTC(currentYear, currentMonth - 7, 1));
      end = new Date(Date.UTC(currentYear, currentMonth - 1, 0));
      break;

    default:
      return [];
  }

  return getDateKeysBetween(start, end).filter(
    (dateKey) => dateKey <= currentDateKey,
  );
}

export function isOverviewQuickActionActive(
  action: string,
  selectedDates: string[],
  now = new Date(),
): boolean {
  const expectedDates = getOverviewQuickActionDates(action, now);
  const selected = [...new Set(selectedDates)].sort();

  if (
    expectedDates.length === 0 ||
    expectedDates.length !== selected.length
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  return expectedDates.every((date, index) => date === selected[index]);
}

===== src/features/admin/utils/timeRange.ts =====
import type { TimeRange } from '../types/admin.types';

export function inTimeRange(dateStr: string, range: TimeRange): boolean {
  const date = new Date(dateStr);
  if (isNaN(date.getTime())) return false;
  const now = new Date();

  const startOfMonth = (d: Date) => new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), 1);
  const endOfMonth = (d: Date) => new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth() + 1, 1);
  const startOfYear = (d: Date) => new Date(d.getFullYear(), 0, 1);
  const endOfYear = (d: Date) => new Date(d.getFullYear() + 1, 0, 1);

  switch (range) {
    case 'this-month': {
      return date >= startOfMonth(now) && date < endOfMonth(now);
    }
    case 'last-month': {
      const lm = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() - 1, 1);
      return date >= startOfMonth(lm) && date < endOfMonth(lm);
    }
    case 'last-quarter': {
      const currentQuarterMonth = Math.floor(now.getMonth() / 3) * 3;
      const lastQuarterStart = new Date(now.getFullYear(), currentQuarterMonth - 3, 1);
      const lastQuarterEnd = new Date(now.getFullYear(), currentQuarterMonth, 1);
      return date >= lastQuarterStart && date < lastQuarterEnd;
    }
    case 'ytd': {
      return date >= startOfYear(now) && date < now;
    }
    case 'this-year': {
      return date >= startOfYear(now) && date < endOfYear(now);
    }
    case 'last-year': {
      const ly = new Date(now.getFullYear() - 1, 0, 1);
      return date >= startOfYear(ly) && date < endOfYear(ly);
    }
    case 'all':
    default:
      return true;
  }
}

export const TIME_RANGE_LABELS: Record<TimeRange, string> = {
  'this-month': 'This month',
  'last-month': 'Last month',
  'last-quarter': 'Last quarter',
  'ytd': 'YTD',
  'this-year': 'This year',
  'last-year': 'Last year',
  'all': 'All time',
};

===== src/design-system/themes/admin.ts =====
import type { CSSProperties } from 'react';

import { primitives } from '../tokens/primitives';
import { adminTypographyTokens } from '../tokens/adminTypography';
import { spacing } from '../tokens/spacing';
import { elevationShadows, radiusTokens } from '../tokens/elevation';

const n = primitives.neutral;
const a = primitives.accent;
const status = primitives.status;

/**
 * Semantic tokens.
 * Components consume these names, never primitive hex values.
 */

const light = {
  colorBg: n.white,
  colorBgSurface: n.gray50,
  colorBgSurfaceSecondary: n.gray50,
  colorBgSurfaceSecondaryHover: n.gray100,

  colorBgFillPrimary: a.orange500,
  colorBgFillPrimaryHover: a.orange600,
  colorBgFillPrimaryActive: a.orange700,
  colorBgFillDisabled: n.gray100,

  colorTextPrimary: n.gray700,
  colorTextSecondary: n.gray500,
  colorTextTertiary: n.gray400,
  colorTextPrimaryDisabled: n.gray400,
  colorTextAccent: a.orange600,
  colorTextOnAccent: n.white,

  colorBorderPrimary: n.gray200,
  colorBorderSecondary: n.gray100,
  colorBorderTertiaryDefault: n.gray50,

  colorIconPrimary: n.gray700,
  colorIconSecondary: n.gray500,
  colorIconSecondaryHover: n.gray700,
  colorIconDisabled: n.gray300,

  colorFocusRing: a.orange600,

  colorStatusSuccess: status.success.light,
  colorBgStatusSuccess: status.success.backgroundLight,
  colorStatusWarning: status.warning.light,
  colorBgStatusWarning: status.warning.backgroundLight,
  colorStatusDanger: status.danger.light,
  colorBgStatusDanger: status.danger.backgroundLight,
} as const;

const dark = {
  colorBg: n.gray800,
  colorBgSurface: n.gray700,
  colorBgSurfaceSecondary: n.gray600,
  colorBgSurfaceSecondaryHover: n.gray500,

  colorBgFillPrimary: a.orange500,
  colorBgFillPrimaryHover: a.orange600,
  colorBgFillPrimaryActive: a.orange700,
  colorBgFillDisabled: n.gray600,

  colorTextPrimary: n.gray50,
  colorTextSecondary: n.gray200,
  colorTextTertiary: n.gray300,
  colorTextPrimaryDisabled: n.gray300,
  colorTextAccent: a.orange300,
  colorTextOnAccent: n.white,

  colorBorderPrimary: n.gray500,
  colorBorderSecondary: n.gray600,
  colorBorderTertiaryDefault: n.gray700,

  colorIconPrimary: n.gray50,
  colorIconSecondary: n.gray200,
  colorIconSecondaryHover: n.white,
  colorIconDisabled: n.gray400,

  colorFocusRing: a.orange300,

  colorStatusSuccess: status.success.dark,
  colorBgStatusSuccess: status.success.backgroundDark,
  colorStatusWarning: status.warning.dark,
  colorBgStatusWarning: status.warning.backgroundDark,
  colorStatusDanger: status.danger.dark,
  colorBgStatusDanger: status.danger.backgroundDark,
} as const;

export const adminThemes = {
  light,
  dark,
} as const;

export type AdminThemeMode = keyof typeof adminThemes;

function toKebabCase(value: string): string {
  return value.replace(/[A-Z]/g, (letter) => `-${letter.toLowerCase()}`);
}

/**
 * Converts the active TypeScript theme into runtime CSS variables.
 * The same semantic variable names are available to Tailwind and CSS.
 */
export function getAdminThemeCssVariables(
  mode: AdminThemeMode,
): CSSProperties {
  const theme = adminThemes[mode];
  const variables: Record<string, string> = {};

  Object.entries(theme).forEach(([key, value]) => {
    variables[`--ds-${toKebabCase(key)}`] = value;
  });

  Object.entries(adminTypographyTokens.styles).forEach(([name, style]) => {
    const tokenName = toKebabCase(name);

    variables[`--ds-font-size-${tokenName}`] = style.fontSize;
    variables[`--ds-line-height-${tokenName}`] = style.lineHeight;
    variables[`--ds-font-weight-${tokenName}`] = String(style.fontWeight);
  });

  variables['--ds-font-family-heading'] = adminTypographyTokens.family.heading;
  variables['--ds-font-family-body'] = adminTypographyTokens.family.body;

  const spacingVariables: Record<string, string> = {
    '1': spacing.xxs,
    '2': spacing.xs,
    '3': spacing.sm,
    '4': spacing.md,
    '5': spacing.lg,
    '6': spacing.xl,
    '8': spacing['2xl'],
    '10': spacing['3xl'],
    '12': spacing['4xl'],
    '16': spacing['5xl'],
  };

  Object.entries(spacingVariables).forEach(([key, value]) => {
    variables[`--ds-space-${key}`] = value;
  });

  Object.entries(radiusTokens).forEach(([key, value]) => {
    variables[`--ds-radius-${key}`] = value;
  });

  Object.entries(elevationShadows).forEach(([key, value]) => {
    variables[`--ds-shadow-${key}`] = value;
  });

  return variables as CSSProperties;
}
